A calibrated transform chain links the vertebral pose recovered via multi-view 2D-3D CT-to-fluoroscopy registration to the RGB-D coordinate frame, enabling direct comparison of any markerless registration estimate against a subsurface reference. Ex vivo validation against optically tracked ovine vertebrae confirms end-to-end accuracy of 2.06 +- 0.29 mm and 3.35 +- 0.93 degrees, sufficient to serve as independent ground truth for methods with substantially larger errors. We demonstrate the pipeline's utility by evaluating two markerless registration strategies on clinical data spanning 11 vertebrae across 1,500 frames from three surgical sequences: the first such evaluation against an independent subsurface reference in a clinical setting. The proposed framework provides reusable infrastructure for benchmarking emerging markerless tracking methods on real surgical data.

</details>


### [72] [Mixture of Multi-Sequence Feature Difference for Contrast-Enhanced MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21703)
*Jonghun Kim,Junmo Kwon,Hyunjin Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoFD模块，从非增强MRI序列合成增强MRI，降低造影剂风险与成本，在图像保真度和感知质量上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CE-MRI对脑肿瘤诊断和分割很重要，但需注射造影剂，存在健康风险并增加扫描成本，因此希望从非增强序列合成CE-MRI。现有方法多用通道拼接融合多序列信息，未能充分利用序列间的差异信息。

Method: 提出Mixture of Feature Difference (MoFD) 模块，采用动态加权策略，自适应地选择并聚合来自各单一源序列的特征以及序列间显式差异特征，用于CE-MRI合成。

Result: 在UCSF-PDGM和UPenn-GBM数据集上，定量与定性评估均显示方法在图像保真度和感知质量上优于基线；成功重建肿瘤区域，分割性能与真实CE-MRI相当。消融实验表明对抗学习提升感知质量，而基于回归的变体结构保真度更高。

Conclusion: MoFD可有效用于非增强到增强MRI的合成，兼顾风险与成本降低，并在保真度、感知质量和下游分割任务上取得良好表现；对抗学习与回归变体在感知质量与结构保真度上各有侧重。

Abstract: Contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (CE-MRI) is crucial for accurately diagnosing and segmenting brain tumors. However, the acquisition of CE-MRI requires injecting contrast agents, which poses potential health risks and increases scan costs. Here, we propose a CE-MRI synthesis method from non-contrast sequences, reducing both risk and cost. Unlike existing methods that rely on channel-wise concatenation to fuse multi-sequence information for synthesis, we introduce a Mixture of Feature Difference (MoFD) module. The proposed module uses a dynamic weighting strategy to adaptively select and aggregate informative features from both individual source sequences and explicit differences among them. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms baselines in terms of image fidelity and perceptual quality on the UCSF-PDGM and UPenn-GBM datasets. Our method successfully reconstructs tumor regions and achieves segmentation performance comparable to that obtained with real CE-MRI. Our ablation studies show that although adversarial learning improves perceptual quality, a regression-based variant of our method achieves higher structural fidelity.

</details>


### [73] [International Multi-Centre Validation of Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Precise Discrimination between Normal and Abnormal Retinal Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21719)
*Zhanhan Tu,Nikhil Reddy,Helen J. Kuht,Ha-Jun Yoon,Gail D. E. Maconachie,Ravi Purohit,Prateek Pani,Line Kessel,Jinu Han,Jing Jin,Shui-Hua Wang,Karthik Bharadwaj,Viral Sheth,Michael Hisaund,Rebecca J. McLean,George Garratt,Chetan Kantibhai Patel,Kanmin Xue,Callum Hunt,Zisen Zhou,Ian De Silva,Seema Teli,Sonal Shah,Vanessa Rodwell,Aarti Patel,Irene Gottlob,Yu-Dong Zhang,Zedong Zheng,Girish Varma,Mervyn G. Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估了一种无监督域适应模型，可在不同OCT设备间分类正常与异常视网膜发育，验证集准确率达87.75%–96.06%，最佳模型准确率96.06%、AUC 0.994。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜发育停滞可导致儿童视力损害，但OCT扫描的判读难以在不同设备和中心之间实现标准化，缺乏统一的分级标准。

Method: 多中心回顾性研究，使用4国5个中心及UK Biobank的OCT扫描数据，涵盖4种OCT设备（含台式与手持系统）。基于ResNet50构建域对抗神经网络，用源设备有标签图像和目标设备无标签图像训练，评估6组源-目标配对，并与7位临床医师判读比较；另留Heidelberg Spectralis数据集做外部验证。

Result: 验证集准确率87.75%–96.06%，敏感度88.68%–98.38%，特异度78.00%–98.02%。表现最佳的模型（由TM OCT1适应至HH OCT1）准确率96.06%，AUC 0.994（95%CI 0.981–0.999）。部分模型在未见过的Heidelberg设备上仍保持判别能力。

Conclusion: 无监督域适应可在无目标设备标注数据的情况下实现跨设备OCT分类，有望支持更一致的视网膜发育评估及对现有影像数据集的大规模分析。

Abstract: Arrested retinal development can cause childhood visual impairment, but grading optical coherence tomography scans is difficult to standardise across devices and centres. We developed and evaluated an unsupervised domain adaptation model to classify normal and abnormal retinal development across different OCT systems. This retrospective multicentre study used OCT scans from five centres in four countries and the UK Biobank. Data covered four OCT devices, including table mounted and handheld systems. A ResNet50 based Domain Adversarial Neural Network was trained using labelled images from a source device and unlabelled images from a target device. We evaluated six source to target pairings and compared model performance with seven clinician graders. A separate Heidelberg Spectralis dataset was reserved for external evaluation. Across validation sets, accuracy ranged from 87.75% to 96.06%, sensitivity from 88.68% to 98.38%, and specificity from 78.00% to 98.02%. The best performing model, adapted from TM OCT1 to HH OCT1, achieved 96.06% accuracy and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.994, with a 95% confidence interval from 0.981 to 0.999. Several models also maintained discrimination on the unseen Heidelberg device. Unsupervised domain adaptation enabled OCT classification across devices without labelled target device data. The approach may support more consistent assessment of retinal development and large scale analysis of existing imaging datasets.

</details>


### [74] [The Dormant Spiking Neuron: A State-Driven Mechanism for Efficient Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21748)
*Ruichen Ma,Yicai Chen,Pujun Zhou,Yue Zuo,Chongyang Li,Shuang Liu,Shaogang Hu,Guanchao Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出休眠脉冲神经元（DSN）模型，通过状态驱动的动态门控机制，使膜电位低于负阈值的神经元进入休眠状态，从而在实例级别动态剪枝冗余计算，显著降低脉冲神经网络能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络的能效通常依赖于突触前输入驱动的稀疏性，但突触后神经元的连续阈下动态仍带来持续的计算开销。定量分析发现，ImageNet训练的ResNet-18中高达44%的神经元处于深度抑制状态，几乎没有放电概率，却仍持续消耗计算资源，这一低效问题长期被忽视。

Method: 引入DSN模型，加入状态驱动的动态门控：当神经元膜电位低于预设负阈值时进入休眠状态，动态剪枝冗余计算。该休眠机制直接融入神经元动态，并通过替代梯度的随时间反向传播进行端到端训练，使网络能够学习到稳健的休眠策略，自适应地保留关键信息流。

Result: 在静态数据集和神经形态数据集上的大量实验表明，DSN框架可将计算能耗持续降低40%至70%以上。在ImageNet大规模数据集上，DSN在可比成本降低水平下优于最先进的静态剪枝技术，同时将相应基线SNN的准确率提升1.34%。

Conclusion: DSN建立了可训练、状态驱动的稀疏范式，与传统的输入驱动方法正交，可释放协同效率增益，为推进神经形态计算提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a compelling pathway toward energy-efficient artificial intelligence, fundamentally anchored in presynaptic input-driven sparsity. However, a substantial yet chronically overlooked source of computational overhead persists: the continuous subthreshold dynamics of postsynaptic neurons. Quantitative analysis reveals that up to 44% of neurons in an ImageNet-trained ResNet-18 model reside in a deeply inhibited state, imposing a persistent computational burden despite having a negligible probability of firing. To address this inefficiency, the Dormant Spiking Neuron (DSN) model is introduced. By incorporating a state-driven dynamic gate, the DSN allows a neuron to enter a dormant state when its membrane potential falls below a predefined negative threshold, dynamically pruning redundant computations at the instance level. Crucially, this dormancy mechanism is directly integrated into the neuronal dynamics and trained end-to-end via backpropagation through time using surrogate gradients, enabling the network to actively learn a robust dormancy policy that adaptively preserves critical information flow. Extensive experiments across static and neuromorphic datasets demonstrate that the DSN framework consistently reduces computational energy by 40% to over 70%. Notably, on the large-scale ImageNet dataset, the DSN outperforms state-of-the-art static pruning techniques at comparable reported cost reductions and simultaneously improves the corresponding baseline SNN accuracy by 1.34%. By establishing a trainable, state-driven sparsity paradigm orthogonal to conventional input-driven methods, the DSN unlocks synergistic efficiency gains, presenting an effective approach for advancing neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Source Prompts: Towards Visually Grounded AI-Generated Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21758)
*Kehao Zhou,Yixuan Li,Yuxuan Jiang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGCap，用视觉锚定字幕替代源提示词引导AIGV质量评估，在LGVQ和FETV上三维度均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频易受多种失真影响，需专门的质量评估方法；内容语义是关键，但常用作文本参考的源提示词往往不能准确反映生成视频的实际内容，导致意图描述与生成结果之间存在偏差。

Method: 构建以视觉锚定字幕引导的AIGV质量评估框架VGCap，引入两种字幕：基于多帧视觉证据对源提示词做最小修改得到的全局字幕，以及提示词无关、描述局部时序内容的分段字幕；二者互补，为空间质量、时序质量和文本-视频对齐提供文本证据。

Result: 在LGVQ和FETV数据集上，VGCap在三个质量维度上均优于代表性基线；关于字幕来源与时序组件的消融实验验证了视觉锚定字幕对各质量维度的有效性，并显示分段级特征对时序质量预测有增益。

Conclusion: 视觉锚定字幕能有效弥补源提示词与生成内容的偏差，分段级特征有助于时序质量评估；代码将开源于 https://github.com/lolo123-123/VGCap。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence have accelerated video generation technologies. However, AI-generated videos (AIGVs) remain susceptible to various distortions, highlighting the need for dedicated video quality assessment methods. Content semantics play an important role in AIGV quality assessment, with source prompts typically used as textual references. However, these prompts may not accurately reflect the generated video content, resulting in a discrepancy between the intended textual description and the generated result. To address this limitation, this paper proposes VGCap, an AIGV quality assessment framework guided by visually grounded captions that align better with generated content. Specifically, we introduce two forms of captions: a global caption obtained by minimally revising the source prompt based on multi-frame visual evidence, and prompt-independent segment captions that describe localized temporal content. Together, these captions provide complementary textual evidence for assessing spatial quality, temporal quality, and text-video alignment. Experimental results on the LGVQ and FETV datasets show that VGCap outperforms representative baselines across all three dimensions. Further ablation studies on caption sources and temporal components confirm the effectiveness of visually grounded captions for each quality dimension, while highlighting the benefits of segment-level features for temporal quality prediction. The source of VGCap will be available at https://github.com/lolo123-123/VGCap.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Generation: Using Multimodal LLMs as Detectors of LLM-Generated Deepfake Facial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21760)
*Olivia Salamon,Virginia N. L. Franqueira,Shujun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基准测试六个MLLM在400张图像上的合成媒体检测能力，发现模型主要依靠识别物理/结构不一致而非水印，但SynthID水印会使Gemini检测准确率下降约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图像生成模型日趋逼真，传统取证技术难以应对，本文探索MLLM作为合成媒体取证分析工具的可行性，并厘清其检测依据是物理不一致还是数字水印等伪影。

Method: 构建含400张图像的数据集（50张真实照片+350张由Gemini 3、Gemini 2.5、Flux.2 Pro、GPT-Image-1/1.5、Seedream 4.0/4.5生成的图像），对GPT-4o、GPT-5、GPT-5.2、Gemini 2.5、Gemini 3、Seed 1.6六个MLLM进行三阶段评测：单图分类、成对比较、以及针对Gemini的"自检测"实验。

Result: 多个MLLM检测准确率较高，GPT-5.2在成对实验中针对特定生成器峰值达97.9%，整体平均成对准确率为82.5%；自检测实验显示SynthID隐形水印使Gemini检测准确率较干净对照组下降10%。

Conclusion: MLLM可无需依赖水印而检测结构伪影，但性能在不同代际模型间差异显著；SynthID嵌入可能抑制部分被用于检测的结构伪影。

Abstract: As image generation models become increasingly capable of producing highly realistic content, traditional forensic techniques are struggling to keep up. This paper investigates whether MLLMs can instead be used as a form of forensic analysis by identifying synthetic media. Specifically, the study aims to determine whether these models detect fake images by recognising physical inconsistencies, or whether they rely on digital artefacts such as embedded watermarks.Six MLLMs: GPT-4o, GPT-5, GPT-5.2, Gemini 2.5, Gemini 3, and Seed 1.6, are benchmarked using a dataset of 400 images. Fifty images are authentic photographs, while the remaining 350 are generated using seven leading image models: Gemini 3, Gemini 2.5, Flux.2 Pro, GPT-Image-1, GPT-Image-1.5, Seedream 4.0, and Seedream 4.5. The evaluation is structured in three stages: single-image classification, pairwise comparison, and a focused "self-detection" experiment on Gemini. Several MLLMs achieved a high detection accuracy, with GPT-5.2 reaching a peak of 97.9% in the pairwise experiment, against specific generators and an overall mean pairwise accuracy of 82.5% by identifying structural or physical inconsistencies within images. However, results from the self-detection experiment indicate that the presence of invisible SynthID watermarks leads to a 10% reduction in Gemini's detection accuracy compared to the clean control group. One possible explanation is that SynthID embedding reduces some of the visible artefacts the models rely on for detection.These results imply that MLLMs can detect structural artefacts without relying on watermarks, but performance varies substantially across model generations. The findings from comparison C5 also suggest that SynthID embedding may suppress some of the structural artefacts used during detection.

</details>


### [77] [FuseAdapt: Parameter-Efficient Multimodal Fusion for Semantic Segmentation with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21779)
*Xin Zhang,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FuseAdapt框架，在冻结的视觉基础模型（VFM）的适配空间内进行多模态语义分割融合，并引入预测式模态建模（PMM）应对模态缺失，显著提升完整与缺失模态下的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语义分割虽受益于互补传感器，但当测试时某些模态退化或缺失时，依赖专用融合模块或多分支跨模态编码器的方法融合会失效，性能大幅下降。作者希望探索VFM先验能否作为多模态分割的有效共享骨干。

Method: FuseAdapt为每个模态分配轻量级适配分支，在冻结VFM内部进行"适配空间融合"：将Q/V低秩适配保持各模态私有，仅将FFN低秩残差激活跨模态路由。此外提出PMM，通过遮蔽目标模态并从可见模态预测其嵌入、以全模态输入下的教师目标监督，仅在训练时使用。

Result: 在MCubeS、DELIVER、MUSES三个基准上，FuseAdapt以少量可训练参数一致提升完整与缺失模态分割性能，完整模态下最高提升+3.01 mIoU，缺失模态下最高提升+12.56 mIoU。

Conclusion: 在冻结VFM的适配空间内进行融合是一种参数高效且鲁棒的多模态分割方案，PMM能有效利用跨模态冗余且无推理开销。

Abstract: Multi-modal semantic segmentation benefits from complementary sensors, but this benefit is fragile when some modalities are degraded or missing at test time. Many existing methods rely on dedicated fusion modules or multi-branch cross-modal encoders, and the fusion often becomes unreliable when modalities are absent, leading to substantial performance drops. We propose \textbf{FuseAdapt}, a parameter-efficient framework that performs fusion within the adaptation space of a frozen vision foundation model (VFM), exploring whether VFM priors can serve as an effective shared backbone for multi-modal segmentation. Instead of attaching a standalone fusion network, FuseAdapt assigns each modality a lightweight adaptation branch and performs \emph{adaptation-space fusion} by keeping Q/V low-rank adaptation private to each modality and routing only FFN low-rank residual activations across modalities inside the frozen backbone. The pretrained VFM remains fixed while modality-specific corrections and explicit cross-modal interaction are learned through the adaptation paths. To handle missing modalities, we further introduce \textbf{Predictive Modality Modeling (PMM)}, a direct representation-level objective for cross-modal redundancy. During training, PMM masks a target modality and predicts its embedding from the visible ones, supervised by a teacher target computed under full-modality input. PMM is used only during training and adds no inference-time overhead. Across three multi-modal segmentation benchmarks (MCubeS, DELIVER, MUSES), FuseAdapt consistently improves both modality-complete and modality-incomplete segmentation with a small trainable budget, yielding up to +3.01 mIoU under full modalities and +12.56 mIoU under missing modalities over prior state-of-the-art. Project page: https://devinxzhang.github.io/FuseAdapt-Page/.

</details>


### [78] [DINO-FIL: Multi-Layer Transformer Fusion for Generative Inpainting Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21780)
*Simon Freitter,David Fischinger,Silvia Poletti,Marcel Hasenbalg,Martin Boyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DINO-FIL：基于DINOv3 ViT-H+/16多层表征与轻量融合解码器的生成图像伪造像素级定位框架，跨数据集实验中泛化性优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑引入的伪影往往细微且空间局部化，标准单层特征方法难以捕捉，需要兼顾语义结构与细粒度纹理不一致性的表征。

Method: 采用预训练DINOv3 ViT-H+/16编码器的多层中间表征，聚合不同深度的transformer特征，并接轻量级融合解码器实现像素级伪造定位。

Result: 在B-Free上训练、在Beyond the Brush、CocoGlide、TGIF、SAGI-D上跨数据集测试，平均及五个数据集中的四个上超越现有基线，同时在真实图像上保持高真阴性率。

Conclusion: 多层特征聚合能有效提升对细微局部伪造伪影的敏感性与跨数据集泛化能力，DINO-FIL为生成图像伪造定位提供了强有力的基线方案。

Abstract: We propose DINO-FIL, a transformer-based framework for pixel-level localization of generative image forgeries. The method leverages multi-layer representations from a pretrained DINOv3 ViT-H+/16 encoder combined with a lightweight fusion decoder to capture both semantic structure and fine-grained texture inconsistencies introduced by image editing. By aggregating intermediate transformer features across depths, the model improves sensitivity to subtle, spatially localized artifacts that are often missed by standard single-layer approaches. We evaluate DINO-FIL in a cross-dataset setting, training on B-Free and testing on Beyond the Brush, CocoGlide, TGIF, and SAGI-D. The model achieves strong generalization, outperforming existing baselines on average and on four of five evaluated datasets, while maintaining a high true negative rate on pristine images.

</details>


### [79] [Learning to Think and Explore in 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21783)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ACT3D，让 VLM 保持单帧原生视觉编码，在 3D 环境中通过动作与观测交互探索，仅用答案正确性作为 RL 奖励，学到自适应探索策略，在六个 3D 基准上平均达 53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 视觉语言方法通常向模型输入多视角图像、视频或 3D 表征，偏离 VLM 原生的单图视觉编码方式，也缺乏类似人类的主动探索能力。作者希望让 VLM 像人一样逐帧观察，并学会在 3D 环境中主动移动视角、获取证据后再作答。

Method: 保留 VLM 原生单帧视觉输入，将 3D 表征仅当作可交互环境：每次 rollout 从初始第一人称视角和视觉问题开始，模型迭代地推理、导航并调整视角，最后给出答案。先用轨迹监督微调，再用强化学习训练，奖励主要由最终答案正确性驱动，不直接监督访问哪些视角或采取哪些探索动作。

Result: 在六个 3D 基准上平均准确率 53.2%，比基座 Qwen 模型提升 22.0%，超过 Gemini 3.6 Flash；优于现有开源 3D 方法且输入帧数减少 50–70%；在未见域上比监督初始化平均提升 5.9%。从初始视角即可作答的问题提升 0.6%，需大量探索的问题提升 16.3%。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习能让 VLM 不仅对视觉证据进行推理，还能学会何时以及如何主动获取证据，表现出问题条件下的自适应探索（寻找遮挡目标、避免无效探索、为空间比较获取互补视角）。

Abstract: We study how vision-language models (VLMs) can learn to think and actively explore in 3D space. Instead of providing multi-view images, videos, or 3D representations to the model, we preserve the native visual encoding of VLMs, i.e., a single image, one at a time, just like humans, and treat the 3D representation only as an environment with which the model can interact through actions (e.g., move left) and observations (rendered images). Each rollout begins from an initial first-person view and a visual question. The model can then iteratively reason, navigate, and reorient its viewpoint before committing to a final answer. Following an initial trajectory-supervised finetuning, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) with rewards driven primarily by final-answer correctness. Crucially, RL provides no direct supervision over which viewpoints the model should visit or which exploration actions it should take. Instead, the model must learn an effective exploration policy solely from the extent to which its interactions improve performance. Across six 3D benchmarks, our model ACT3D achieves an average accuracy of 53.2% that improves its base Qwen model by 22.0% and surpasses proprietary Gemini 3.6 Flash. ACT3D also outperforms existing open-source 3D methods while using 50-70% fewer input frames, and generalizes to unseen domains with an average improvement of 5.9% over its supervised initialization. Further analysis reveals adaptive, question-conditioned exploration. Performance gains over the base model increase from 0.6% on questions answerable from the initial view to 16.3% on those requiring extensive exploration. ACT3D searches for occluded targets, avoids unnecessary exploration, and acquires complementary viewpoints for spatial comparisons. These findings demonstrate that outcome-based RL enables VLMs not only to reason over visual evidence, but also to learn when and how to acquire it.

</details>


### [80] [NICE Toolbox: Nonverbal Interpersonal Communication Exploration Toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21786)
*Aleksandr Evgrashin,Carolin Schmitt,Timo Lübbing,Ashutosh Jha,Buket Naz Zeren,Sophie K. Bauer,Johannes Kopf-Beck,Anton K. G. Marx,Anamaria Semm,Gökce Ergün,Senya Polikovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍 NICE Toolbox：一个免费开源的 Python 框架，整合现有计算机视觉与语音处理方法，自动测量人际交流中的言语与非言语信号，支持多摄像头、算法替换、可视化、自定义数据评估与结果导出，旨在让心理学研究中的数据探索更具可扩展性、可复现性和可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类交流是心理学的长期目标，人们通过姿态、手势、注视、面部表情和语音等多渠道表达自我。然而，这些信号的测量与标注传统上依赖受过训练的人工编码者，耗时费力，难以规模化。

Method: 提出 NICE Toolbox（Nonverbal Interpersonal Communication Exploration Toolbox），一个免费开源的 Python 软件框架。它将现有最先进的计算机视觉和语音处理方法整合到单一软件包中，并提供多摄像头系统支持、可互换的检测算法、检测器输出的 2D 与 3D 可视化、在用户自定义数据上的评估，以及向第三方软件的导出功能。

Result: 该工具箱实现了对人际交流信号的自动化测量，并通过多摄像头支持、算法可替换、可视化、自定义数据评估和导出等功能，为研究者提供了完整的使用与验证流程。原文为工具发布类论文，未报告具体实验指标。

Conclusion: NICE Toolbox 旨在替代耗时的人工标注流程，使人际交流信号的数据探索变得可扩展、可复现，并更易为心理学研究所获取和使用。

Abstract: Understanding human communication is a long-standing goal in psychological research. People express themselves through many verbal and nonverbal channels: posture, gesture, gaze, facial expression, and speech. Measurement and annotation of these signals has traditionally required time-consuming manual work by trained human labellers. The Nonverbal Interpersonal Communication Exploration (NICE) Toolbox is a free, open-source Python framework that automatically measures these signals by combining existing state-of-the-art computer vision and speech-processing methods into a single software package. It includes support for multi-camera systems, interchangeable detection algorithms, 2D and 3D visualization of detector outputs, evaluation on the user's custom data, and export to third-party software. With the release of this tool, we aim to make data exploration of interpersonal communication signals scalable, reproducible, and accessible for psychological research.

</details>


### [81] [MedPAO-VL: Interpretable and Protocol-Compliant Agent for Structured Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21794)
*Shrish Shrinath Vaidya,Gowthamaan Palani,Minmini Selvam,Gokulraja Srinivasaraja,Ganapathy Krishnamurthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MedPAO-VL 多模态智能体，将胸部X光结构化报告生成从黑箱式端到端映射转变为可解释、迭代、符合协议的智能体流程，用 plan-act-observe 循环提升临床准确性与结构化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型泛化能力强，但端到端生成医学影像报告如同黑箱，易产生事实幻觉和无依据的发现，且不遵循领域特定的结构化协议，可靠性与临床可用性受限。

Method: MedPAO-VL 是一个多模态智能体，采用 plan-act-observe 循环：先通过可解释视觉模块感知胸部X光并捕捉固有诊断不确定性，再通过观察语义差异迭代优化报告，自我纠正生成结果，使其更贴合视觉检测到的临床发现。

Result: 该智能体式推理提升了临床正确性与完整性，获得专家评分：准确度 3.5/5，结构分 4.3/5。

Conclusion: 以病理特异性可解释性和迭代优化取代不透明的端到端映射，MedPAO-VL 为自动化结构化放射报告生成提供了透明且临床上可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable generalization capabilities of Large Language Models, generating clinically accurate and structured radiology reports from medical images remains a significant challenge. Current end-to-end approaches function as black boxes, prone to factual hallucinations and generating ungrounded findings. This lack of reliability and failure to adhere to domain-specific structuring protocols, fundamentally limits their clinical utility. We present MedPAO-VL, a multimodal agent that shifts the paradigm from monolithic generation to explainable, iterative, protocol-compliant structured report generation. Unlike deterministic models, our plan-act-observe loop functions as a goal-oriented agent: it first perceives the Chest X-Ray through an explainable vision module designed to capture inherent diagnostic uncertainty, then iteratively refines the generated report by observing semantic discrepancies and self-correcting its generation for better alignment with visually detected clinical findings. Evaluation of our framework demonstrates that this agentic reasoning improves clinical correctness and completeness, achieving an expert-rated accuracy score of 3.5/5 and a structural score of 4.3/5. By prioritizing pathology specific explainability and iterative refinement over opaque end-to-end mapping, MedPAO-VL offers a transparent and clinically viable alternative for automated structured radiology report generation.

</details>


### [82] [Single-stage joint diffusion of sky images and clear-sky index for ultra-short-term irradiance forecasting: what the frame-to-irradiance stage discards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21795)
*Kanawut Suwandee,Suwichaya Suwanwimolkul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单阶段扩散模型，同时去噪未来天空图像与晴空指数序列，用辐照度头读取去噪预览，无需回归级联与实测辐照度输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天空图像辐照度预测为两阶段：先预测未来天空帧，再用单独训练回归器映射为辐照度。级联会丢失信息，且通常依赖实测辐照度输入，在只有相机无辐照计的站点不可用。

Method: 单阶段替代方案：在一个扩散过程中联合去噪未来天空图像与晴空指数序列；辐照度头读取未来天空的去噪预览而非含噪扩散状态；仅以天空图像和确定性协变量为输入，无需实测辐照度。

Result: 2023 SIRTA数据上汇总RMSE 104.1 W/m²，相对smart persistence的CRPS技巧分+0.34，优于十二个基线配置，并在2021–2022重训后保持技巧。在其自生成帧上，单独训练的回归器为150.6 W/m²，而联合头为104.1，联合训练挽回约46 W/m²。确定性网络读取实测辐照度达到97.6 W/m²，点精度更优，但其CRPS技巧在三个天空条件分位均低于本模型0.26–0.30。

Conclusion: 联合训练消除级联损失，在无辐照计场景提供高技巧概率预测；点精度略逊于使用实测辐照度的确定性网络，但概率预测技巧在各天空条件下均领先。

Abstract: Sky-image irradiance forecasting is dominated by a two-stage design: a video-prediction model forecasts future sky frames, and a separately trained regressor maps those frames to irradiance. We propose a single-stage alternative that co-denoises the future sky-image and clear-sky-index sequences in one diffusion process, with an irradiance head that reads a denoised preview of the future sky rather than the noised diffusion state. The forecaster needs no regression stage and runs from sky images and deterministic covariates alone, with no measured irradiance at its input --- the situation at a site with a camera but no pyranometer. On 2023 SIRTA data the model reaches a pooled RMSE of $104.1$~W\,m$^{-2}$ and a skill score of $+0.34$ on the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) against smart persistence, higher than any of twelve baseline configurations, and retains that skill when retrained on 2021--2022. On the model's own generated frames, a separately trained regressor reaches $150.6$~W\,m$^{-2}$ against the joint head's $104.1$: roughly $46$~W\,m$^{-2}$ that joint training recovers and a cascade discards. Point accuracy is competitive but not best: a deterministic network that also reads measured irradiance attains $97.6$~W\,m$^{-2}$. The model's CRPS skill leads that network's in every sky-condition tertile, by $0.26$ to $0.30$.

</details>


### [83] [Teaching DINOv3 About Partial 3D Geometry: A Self-Supervised Geometry-Aware Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21802)
*Viktoria Ehm,Dongliang Cao,Riccardo Marin,Daniel Scholz,Weikang Wang,Florian Bernard,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用基于LoRA的自监督学习微调DINO特征，使其对部分观测鲁棒，从而在部分形状匹配和左右预测任务上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分形状匹配在3D视觉中重要但研究不足，实际场景中形状常只被部分观测。现有特征描述子在此场景下表现不佳：传统方法难以处理部分形状的边界，或过度依赖形状的空间位置；即便使用DINO特征，其本身也并非为部分观测设计。

Method: 利用基于LoRA的自监督学习对DINO特征进行精炼，生成对部分性鲁棒的特征描述子，并可集成到现有的部分形状匹配流程中。

Result: 相比传统特征和视觉基础模型特征，所提特征在部分形状匹配上性能大幅提升；集成到现有流程后，在部分形状匹配和左右预测基准上达到最优水平。

Conclusion: 通过LoRA自监督精炼DINO特征是提升部分形状匹配的有效途径，兼具鲁棒性与即插即用性。

Abstract: Partial shape matching is a crucial yet underexplored problem in 3D vision, with significant relevance to real-world scenarios where shapes are often only partially observed. Existing feature descriptors face difficulties in this setting, as traditional representations either struggle with the boundaries of partial shapes or heavily depend on the shape's spatial position. While existing approaches have employed DINO features for partial shape matching, these features are not inherently suited for handling partial observations. In this work, we propose a method to refine DINO features using LoRA-based self-supervised learning, enabling the generation of feature descriptors that are robust to partiality. Our features substantially improve performance on partial shape matching compared to traditional or vision foundation features. Additionally, when integrated into existing partial shape matching pipelines, we achieve state-of-the-art results on partial shape matching and left-right prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [84] [From Adults to Children: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Chest X-Ray Classification under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21809)
*Sara Inácio,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个成人到儿科胸片分类的基础模型基准，比较六个基础模型与两个CNN基线，发现所有模型从成人迁移到儿科均出现超过10个百分点的显著性能下降，且加深适应深度无法显著改善跨群体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学影像中应用广泛，但其在不同患者群体间的鲁棒性尚不明确，尤其是从成人到儿科的跨群体泛化能力缺乏系统评估。

Method: 将VinDr-CXR与PediCXR统一为五标签分类任务，评估六个基础模型和两个CNN基线；所有模型仅用成人数据适应，并在成人域与儿科域通过线性探测、部分微调和全微调三种方式进行测试，同时分析适应深度的影响。

Result: 所有模型从成人迁移到儿科数据时性能显著下降，降幅超过10个百分点；成人测试集Macro-AUROC约92%，儿科域降至约78%。增加适应深度未显著提升跨群体泛化，域内强性能也未转化为更好的儿科表现。

Conclusion: 基础模型在成人到儿科的跨群体泛化上存在明显局限，域内高性能与更深适应均不能保证跨群体鲁棒性，提示医学基础模型需针对跨群体泛化进行专门设计。

Abstract: Foundation models have shown significant potential across diverse fields and have been applied to medical imaging, but their robustness across patient populations remains unclear. In this work, we investigate foundation models' ability to generalize from adult to pediatric chest X-ray classification. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark to compare multiple foundation models under a common adult-to-pediatric evaluation setting while also analyzing the effect of adaptation depth. We harmonize VinDr-CXR and PediCXR into a five-label classification task and evaluate six foundation models and two CNN baselines. All models are adapted using adult data only and tested in both the in-domain and pediatric domains using linear probing, partial fine-tuning, and full fine-tuning. The benchmark reveals a clear and consistent trend: all models suffer a substantial performance degradation when transferred from adult to pediatric data, with drops exceeding 10 percentage points. While Macro-AUROC values reach approximately 92% on the adult test set, performance decreases to around 78% on the pediatric domain. Another important finding is that increasing the depth of adaptation does not significantly improve cross-population generalization. Moreover, strong in-domain performance does not translate into better performance on pediatric data.

</details>


### [85] [Temporal Periodic Image Registration with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21845)
*Mathias Lowes,Kristine Aavild Sørensen,Maxime Sermesant,Rasmus R. Paulsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Temporal-IDIR，将隐式神经表示（INR）扩展到周期性时序图像配准，通过单一INR同时建模所有帧的形变，并引入时间一致性损失实现自正则化，在DIR-LAB上达到1.03 mm的TRE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示（INR）因能用简单轻量网络建模函数而受到关注，并在可形变图像配准中表现有效。本文希望将INR进一步扩展到时序图像配准领域，特别是周期性时序图像序列，以利用帧间关系提升配准效果。

Method: 提出Temporal-IDIR，优化单个INR同时建模整个时序图像序列中所有帧的形变，使形变预测能够自我正则化。为此引入时间一致性损失，惩罚直接源到目标变换与经过中间帧的变换之间的差异。

Result: 在DIR-LAB数据集上，以标注与移动地标之间的目标配准误差（TRE）为指标，取得1.03 mm的TRE，优于其他基于INR的配准方法。框架还支持在给定输入帧之间进行时间帧的平滑插值。代码已公开。

Conclusion: Temporal-IDIR表明将INR扩展到周期性时序图像配准是有效的，单INR建模结合时间一致性损失可提升配准精度并支持帧间平滑插值，且代码开源便于复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently gained popularity for their ability to model functions using simple and lightweight networks. These methods have also proven effective in deformable image registration. In this work, we extend INRs for deformable image registration to the domain of temporal image registration, focusing on periodic temporal image sequences. Our approach, Temporal-IDIR, optimizes a single INR to model deformations across all frames in a temporal image sequence simultaneously, allowing for self-regularization through its own deformation predictions. To achieve this, we introduce a temporal consistency loss that penalizes discrepancies between direct source-to-target transformations and those traversing intermediate frames. We evaluate our framework on the DIR-LAB dataset, using the target registration error (TRE) between annotated and moved landmarks as the metric. Here, we achieve a TRE of 1.03 mm, outperforming other INR-based registration methods. Additionally, our framework supports smooth interpolation between time frames by estimating deformations between the given input frames. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MMLowes/Temporal_INR.

</details>


### [86] [Seeing Both the Forest and the Trees: Reusing Holistic 3D Priors for Part-Decomposed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21850)
*Yiqun Zhao,Binbin Huang,Haobin Duan,Zibo Zhao,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRUCTPARTS 提出双坐标系交错机制，在单网格先验基础上仅需轻量微调即可生成兼具部件内部几何细节与整体结构一致性的部件化解剖3D资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像条件3D生成模型虽通过结构化3D表征同时捕捉部件内细节与整体结构一致性，但输出仅为单一融合网格，无法获得显式的部件分解结果。核心问题是：部件分解生成模型如何继承这类整体先验的两种属性。

Method: 提出 STRUCTPARTS 框架，核心技术为双坐标系交错机制：每个形状隐变量同时在部件局部与物体全局参考系下解释，并通过全局—局部交错Transformer处理，在部件尺度复用先验的几何细节、在整体尺度复用其结构一致性。结构输入采用分段网格脚手架——粗网格配以面级分割掩码，完全保留在先验原生的3D表征中。

Result: 在 PartObjaverse-Tiny 上，STRUCTPARTS 以远低于同类方法的训练成本达到或超越既有部件分解模型的性能，并原生支持部件网格细化与部件级编辑。

Conclusion: 仅需轻量微调，部件内细节与部件间一致性即可从同一预训练权重中自然涌现，证明整体先验可有效迁移至部件分解生成任务。

Abstract: Generating 3D assets with explicit parts requires two properties at once: geometric detail inside each part and structural coherence across the whole object. Recent image-conditioned 3D generation models adopt a structured 3D representation that anchors tokens to explicit spatial positions, capturing both properties, but their output remains a single fused mesh. We ask how a part-decomposed generation model can inherit both properties of these holistic priors, and answer with STRUCTPARTS, a framework whose core technical contribution is a dual-frame coordinate interleaving mechanism: each shape latent is interpreted under both a part-local and an object-global reference frame, and processed through a global-local inter-leaved transformer that simultaneously reuses the prior's geometric detail at the part scale and its structural coherence at the whole-object scale. As its structural input, STRUCTPARTS uses a segmented mesh scaffold, a coarse mesh paired with a face-level segmentation mask kept entirely within the prior's native 3D representation. With only lightweight finetuning, both intra-part detail and inter-part coherence emerge from the same pretrained weights. On PartObjaverse-Tiny, STRUCTPARTS matches or surpasses prior part-decomposed models at a fraction of their training cost, while naturally supporting part-mesh refinement and part-level editing.

</details>


### [87] [Anvil: Scaling Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Knowledge Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21882)
*Weilin Ruan,Bohan Zhou,Jinghao Li,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Peiran Dong,Yingjie Xu,Leyi Wu,Haojian Huang,Binghui Xie,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Anvil统一框架，涵盖数据生成、训练与评测，用于具身空间智能，并通过9B学生模型整合异构空间知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身空间智能不仅需要视觉识别，还需定位实体、推理关系并以动作相关形式表达知识；但监督信号分散在不兼容的几何与语义输出中，联合训练会混淆不同阶段所需知识。

Method: 包含三部分：Anvil-Gen异构数据飞轮，跨11种Ground-Relation-Act形式进行生成、跨阶段一致性验证与失败驱动回收；Anvil-Ben提供场景对齐监督与场景关联评测，区分阶段能力与错误传播；Anvil-MOPD为9B学生模型，通过专家教师蒸馏整合空间知识，采用语义优先故障路由与阶段归一化。

Result: 构建580万条源锚定记录，覆盖16万图像，58万条用于留出评测、522万条用于训练。在七个公开基准上平均分别超过Qwen3.5-9B和RynnBrain-8B 9.29和11.03个百分点，VABench-Point达80.67%。

Conclusion: 受控研究支持定向覆盖、选择性迁移和反馈引导修复是框架的互补组成部分，Anvil有效提升具身空间智能能力。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires more than visual recognition: models must localize entities, reason about their relations, and express this knowledge in action-relevant forms. Yet joint training can conflate the knowledge needed at different stages because supervision is fragmented across incompatible geometric and semantic outputs. We introduce Anvil, a unified framework for data generation, training, and benchmarking for embodied spatial intelligence. (1) Anvil-Gen is a heterogeneous data flywheel that combines form-specific generation, cross-stage consistency verification, and failure-driven recollection across 11 Ground--Relation--Act forms. (2) Anvil-Ben provides scene-aligned supervision for learning diverse embodied spatial capabilities and scene-linked assessments that distinguish stage competence from error propagation. (3) Anvil-MOPD is a 9B student that consolidates heterogeneous spatial knowledge through distillation from specialist teachers. Semantic-first-fault routing targets teacher supervision to the student's earliest decisive failure, while stage normalization controls the loss scale across native outputs of different lengths. We construct 5.8M source-grounded records spanning 160K images, reserving 580K spatial records for held-out evaluation and using the remaining 5.22M records for training. On average across seven public benchmarks, Anvil-MOPD outperforms Qwen3.5-9B and RynnBrain-8B by 9.29 and 11.03 percentage points, respectively, while reaching 80.67% on VABench-Point. Controlled studies support targeted coverage, selective transfer, and feedback-guided repair as complementary framework components.

</details>


### [88] [SurgStreamer: proactive, request-driven monitoring of surgical video streams with structured state and a reusable reasoning layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21895)
*Bowen Xiang,Jon S. Heiselman,Michael I. Miga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将手术视频的请求式事件检测拆分为"流程特定感知"与"流程无关推理"两阶段框架 SurgStreamer，在胆囊切除上的封闭测试集实现宏事件F1 0.598（75秒窗口下0.677），显著优于流式视觉语言模型基线，并验证了跨中心与跨术式的可迁移性，指出分布偏移下的感知是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术团队需要能监听视频中特定事件（如"Calot三角解剖时夹钳出现时提醒我"）并回答已发生事件的助手。现有方法缺乏对"精确语义"和"因果告警决策"的形式化定义，且端到端视觉语言模型难以在该任务上取得良好表现，因此需要更合理的任务形式化与系统设计。

Method: 将任务形式化为"请求驱动事件检测"，要求精确语义与因果告警决策。系统分为两部分：(1) 流程特定感知，将1 FPS视频流映射为关于器械、动作、阶段的结构化状态；(2) 流程无关推理，在状态与事件记忆上评估形式化监控条件，可用精确规则或小型控制器，并通过指针而非名称寻址实体。评估在预先注册的封闭测试集（12例腹腔镜胆囊切除）上进行，并与使用适配视觉编码器微调的OneStreamer-4B等流式视觉语言模型对比。

Result: 封闭测试集全流程宏事件F1为0.598，仅给75秒窗口时为0.677；OneStreamer-4B各配置最高仅0.540（Holm校正p≤0.012）。三个外部中心仅重训感知后，保留术式性能从0.239提升至0.470。在新术式（垂体手术）上，用胆囊切除训练的控制器在oracle状态上与域内控制器相当（0.947 vs 0.938）；预测状态上未达到预注册非劣效边界（-0.024 [-0.040, -0.008]），除非重校准告警阈值。

Conclusion: 结果支持将感知与推理分离的设计，并表明分布偏移下的感知能力是当前主要限制，重训感知可显著改善跨中心泛化，而推理层的控制器在跨术式上具有较好可迁移性。

Abstract: Surgical teams could benefit from assistants that watch the video for requested events, such as "tell me when the clip applier appears during Calot triangle dissection", and answer questions about what has already happened. We formalise this task as request-driven event detection with exact semantics and causal alert decisions, and present SurgStreamer. It separates procedure-specific perception, which maps the 1-FPS stream to a structured state over tools, actions and phases, from procedure-agnostic reasoning, which evaluates a formal monitoring condition over this state and an event memory, either with exact rules or with a small controller that addresses entities through pointers rather than names. We evaluate this monitoring core with gold conditions. On a pre-registered, sealed test set of 12 laparoscopic cholecystectomies, SurgStreamer reached a macro event-F1 of 0.598 over full procedures. On identical 75-s windows with only the window available to every method, it scored 0.677. The tested configurations of the streaming vision-language model OneStreamer-4B, including ones fine-tuned on SurgStreamer's state or on video with an adapted vision encoder, scored at most 0.540 (Holm-adjusted p <= 0.012). At three external centres, re-training only perception raised performance on held-out procedures from 0.239 to 0.470. On a new procedure, pituitary surgery, controller weights trained on cholecystectomy matched an in-domain controller on oracle state (0.947 vs 0.938). On predicted state they missed the pre-registered non-inferiority margin (-0.024 [-0.040, -0.008]) unless the alert thresholds were re-calibrated. The results support separating perception from reasoning and point to perception under distribution shift as the main limitation.

</details>


### [89] [Seeing is Believing: Benchmarking and Mitigating the Interference of Language Priors on Visual Perception in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21902)
*Li Ma,Yan Zhuang,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态大模型（MLLMs）中语言先验压倒视觉证据的问题，指出既有基准的假设缺陷，并提出ViSiP-Bench基准与MAPS方法以更精确地度量并缓解先验依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准存在两个关键缺陷：一是假设各模型共享相同的语言先验，但实际跨模型先验一致性低于80%；二是仅依赖准确率评价，而有超过15%的题目无需图像即可答对，导致准确率会高估模型对先验的抵抗能力。因此需要更精确、连续的度量方式来评估模型对语言先验的依赖。

Method: 作者提出ViSiP-Bench基准，基于六类先验中的罕见但真实场景构建，并设计了模型感知的动态先验诱导协议，配套三个比单一准确率更精确、连续的度量指标。基于评估发现，进一步提出MAPS方法：识别每个模型最强的先验，并在事实性知识上进行微调，以削弱过度的先验依赖而不损害通用能力。

Result: 对24个模型的评估显示，先验遵循现象普遍存在，且不随模型规模增大而改善；更大的模型倾向于要么强烈压制先验，要么坚定坚持先验。

Conclusion: 语言先验对MLLMs视觉感知的干扰是系统性且与规模无关的问题；ViSiP-Bench提供了更可靠的度量工具，而MAPS可在保持通用能力的同时有效缓解过度先验依赖。

Abstract: Language priors in MLLMs can override visual evidence, impairing visual perception. Existing benchmarks assume shared priors across models and rely solely on accuracy. However, cross-model prior agreement falls below 80%, and over 15% of items can be answered correctly without any image, causing accuracy to overestimate prior resistance. To address these limitations, we introduce ViSiP-Bench, a benchmark grounded in rare-but-real scenarios across six prior categories, with a model-aware dynamic prior induction protocol and three metrics that more precisely and continuously measure prior resistance than accuracy alone. Evaluating 24 models reveals that prior adherence is pervasive, does not improve with scale, and larger models tend to either strongly override their priors or firmly adhere to them. We therefore propose MAPS, which identifies each model's strongest priors and fine-tunes on factually grounded knowledge to soften excessive prior reliance without disrupting general capabilities.

</details>


### [90] [BridgeAfford: Bridging Query-Target Gap for Generalizable 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21918)
*Yijie Su,Yakun Zhu,Yi Bin,Pengpeng Zeng,Liang Chen,Guoqing Wang,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BridgeAfford框架，通过交互区域对齐（IRA）和视觉证据聚合（VEA）缩小查询-目标语义鸿沟，提升3D可供性定位对未见物体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的3D可供性定位需依据交互意图定位3D物体的功能区域，但现有方法对未见物体泛化性差。核心问题是查询-目标鸿沟：编码交互意图的查询特征与目标区域特征之间存在语义差异，端到端训练使查询偏向已见物体，难以兼容未见物体的目标区域特征。

Method: BridgeAfford从两个互补角度缩小语义鸿沟：1）交互区域对齐（IRA）在训练中促进几何相似交互区域间的跨物体对齐，使未见物体上相似结构产生接近训练时的表示，便于目标区域识别；2）视觉证据聚合（VEA）利用2D可供性模型优化注意力分布，引导查询指向正确区域，生成更关注目标的查询。

Result: 在LASO和PIAD数据集上的大量实验表明，BridgeAfford优于现有方法，尤其在未见物体上表现突出，具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过IRA与VEA缓解查询-目标语义鸿沟，BridgeAfford有效提升了3D可供性定位的泛化性能，尤其适用于未见物体场景。

Abstract: Language-guided 3D affordance grounding aims to localize functional regions on 3D objects according to interaction intents. However, existing methods exhibit poor generalization to unseen objects. One challenge is the query-target gap: a semantic discrepancy between query features encoding interaction intents and target-region features, leading to inaccurate grounding. End-to-end training favors query representations tailored to seen objects, limiting their compatibility with shifted target-region features on unseen objects. To address this issue, we propose BridgeAfford, a framework that reduces the query-target semantic gap from two complementary perspectives. First, Interaction Region Alignment (IRA) promotes cross-object alignment among geometrically similar interaction regions during training. This enables structures on unseen objects that resemble previously observed ones to produce representations similar to those learned during training, facilitating target-region recognition. Second, Visual Evidence Aggregation (VEA) leverages a 2D affordance model to refine the attention distribution and guide the query toward the correct region, yielding more target-aware queries. Extensive experiments on the LASO and PIAD datasets demonstrate that BridgeAfford outperforms existing methods, particularly on unseen objects, highlighting its robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [91] [Flow-CLIP: Image-Conditioned Residual Scoring for CompositionalImage-Text Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21937)
*Minghong Wei,Enguang Zhu,Pu Cao,Lu Yang,Qing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Flow-CLIP，通过图像条件化的 token-patch 证据变化来精炼图文全局对齐，提升组合匹配与属性-物体绑定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言预训练在图文检索与零样本识别上进展显著，但句级对齐不足以应对组合匹配问题：不同字幕可能描述相同的物体与属性，仅在元素绑定方式上存在差异，模型容易依赖语言先验而忽略图像证据。

Method: Flow-CLIP 包含三个组件：全局兼容性校准（GCC）学习句级相似度度量；图像条件证据精炼（ICER）将视觉 patch 上下文注入 token 查询，并利用局部匹配证据的变化调整全局分数；跨图像边际排序（CIMR）在匹配与不匹配图像间比较同一正负字幕对，促使图像支持正确描述时获得更大的排序边际。

Result: 在组合匹配与属性-物体绑定基准上，Flow-CLIP 相比预训练 CLIP 及已学习的全局对齐方法提升了绑定准确率；跨图像排序监督缓解了对语言先验的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过局部证据精炼与跨图像排序监督，可有效弥补句级对齐在组合匹配上的不足，代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision--language pre-training has enabled substantial progress in image--text retrieval and zero-shot recognition by learning transferable cross-modal representations. However, sentence-level alignment remains insufficient for compositional matching, where captions can describe the same objects and attributes while differing in how these elements are bound. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-CLIP, which refines global image-text alignment through image-conditioned changes in token--patch evidence. Global Compatibility Calibration (GCC) learns a sentence-level similarity metric, while Image-Conditioned Evidence Refinement (ICER) incorporates visual patch context into token queries and uses the resulting change in local matching evidence to adjust the global score. We further introduce Cross-Image Margin Ranking (CIMR), which compares the same positive--negative caption pair across matched and mismatched images, encouraging a larger ranking margin when the image supports the correct description. Experiments on compositional matching and attribute--object binding benchmarks show that Flow-CLIP improves binding accuracy over pretrained CLIP and learned global alignment through local evidence refinement, while cross-image ranking supervision mitigates reliance on language priors. Code will be released at link.

</details>


### [92] [VesperMUSE: Multi-Modal Fusion Vision Transformer for Image Semantic Segmentation Under Diverse Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21990)
*Raul Balmez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VesperMUSE，一个统一的多模态语义分割框架，通过物理启发的合成低光适应、热引导低光增强以及模态无关的自注意力融合 RGB、热成像与表面法向，实现极端低光环境下的鲁棒分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割对自主系统导航与场景理解至关重要，但在低光环境中因噪声、伪影、颜色失真和结构细节丢失而性能严重下降。现有方法难以在多样光照条件下保持鲁棒性，因此需要一个能够从多个层面应对光照不足的多模态统一框架。

Method: 提出 VesperMUSE 框架，在多个层面解决光照不足问题：1）新颖的物理启发式合成低光适应算法；2）以热成像引导的低光图像增强（LLIE）范式；3）通过多模态融合恢复语义分割所需的场景内在细节。增强后将主 RGB 特征与辅助的热表示及突出几何不连续与材质边界的表面法向图动态整合。核心是可扩展、模态无关的自注意力机制，可从任意数量辅助输入中提取全局上下文与精细局部特征，并将分层多模态表示组合投影到统一组件，使网络选择性关注相关跨模态线索，且在不同传感器配置下无需修改架构。

Result: 大量评估表明，VesperMUSE 能成功恢复缺失的视觉线索，在极端和非结构化真实环境中提供灵活的最新（SOTA）机器感知解决方案。

Conclusion: VesperMUSE 是一个灵活且达到 SOTA 的多模态语义分割框架，通过物理启发合成数据、热引导增强与模态无关注意力融合，有效解决低光环境下的分割退化问题，适用于真实世界的自主系统感知。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is critical for the reliable navigation and scene understanding of autonomous systems, yet its performance degrades severely in low-light environments due to noise, artifacts, colour distortion, and the loss of structural details. To address this challenge, this work introduces VesperMUSE, a unified multi-modal framework designed to achieve robust image semantic segmentation across diverse illumination conditions. The architecture addresses the light deficiency problem on multiple levels: proposing a novel physics-informed synthetic low-light adaptation algorithm, approaching Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) through a thermal-guided paradigm, and finally aiming to recover the intrinsic scene details for semantic segmentation through a multi-modal fusion approach. Following enhancement, the framework dynamically integrates the primary RGB features with auxiliary thermal representations and surface normal maps highlighting geometric discontinuities and material boundaries. At the core of this integration is a highly scalable, modality-agnostic self-attention mechanism that extracts both global context and fine local features from an arbitrary number of auxiliary inputs. By combining and projecting these hierarchical multi-modal representations into a unified component, the attention module allows the network to selectively attend to relevant cross-modal cues without requiring any architectural modifications when different sensor configurations are used. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VesperMUSE successfully recovers missing visual cues, providing a flexible, state-of-the-art (SOTA) solution for machine perception in extreme and unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [93] [Data-Efficient ConvNeXt Pretraining for High-Resolution Sentinel-1 GRD SAR Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21993)
*Yuki Natsume,Sang-Ho Yun,Chai Kiat Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SparK-SAR：基于稀疏卷积掩码预训练框架（SparK+ConvNeXt），面向Sentinel-1 GRD影像，在受限算力下有效提升下游表现，并以更少参数超越大型ViT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S1GRD标注数据稀缺，限制监督深度学习；该领域自监督预训练多依赖ViT掩码图像建模，需大量数据与算力。因此需探索更高效、适配卷积归纳偏置的预训练方案。

Method: 基于SparK与ConvNeXt构建稀疏卷积掩码预训练框架，包含三项技术贡献：(i) ConvNeXt-RS编码器，将stem步幅由4x降至2x，在16x步幅下产生14x14特征图，参数量不变而空间token增4倍；(ii) 轻量ConvNeXt-512重建解码器，采用PixelShuffle上采样；(iii) 刻画标准SparK UNet-768解码器在S1GRD上FP16混合精度预训练的不稳定性，并发现微调时使用逐层学习率衰减（LLRD）可提升下游性能。

Result: 在四个Copernicus-Bench S1GRD任务上，微调的27.8M参数ConvNeXt-RS编码器相比未预训练基线分别提升+4.7、+5.0、+5.8、+1.4个百分点（EuroSAT-S1、BigEarthNet-S1、DFC2020-S1、Flood-S1）；在四任务中的三个上以约1/5参数量超越Copernicus-FM ViT（139M参数，冻结）。预训练VRAM较UNet-128基线每GPU降低7.5-8.5%且精度相当。数据缩放显示任务相关效率：分类在10-25%的SSL4EO-S12 v1.1语料即饱和，稠密预测在25-50%仍显著提升。另含CKA表征分析与地理去碰撞实验。

Conclusion: SparK-SAR证明卷积稀疏掩码预训练在S1GRD遥感任务中参数量更省、算力更友好，并可媲美甚至优于大型ViT自监督方案；同时揭示FP16不稳定与LLRD有效性及任务相关的数据效率规律。

Abstract: Labeled Sentinel-1 Ground Range Detected (S1GRD) datasets remain scarce, limiting supervised deep learning for land-surface remote sensing, while self-supervised pretraining for this domain has been dominated by Vision Transformer (ViT)-based masked image modeling requiring substantial data and compute. We propose SparK-SAR, a sparse convolutional masked pretraining framework built on SparK and ConvNeXt, designed to exploit convolutional inductive biases for S1GRD imagery under constrained compute. SparK-SAR makes three core technical contributions: (i) ConvNeXt-RS, a domain-adapted encoder that halves the stem stride (4x to 2x) to produce 14x14 feature maps at stride 16x, providing 4x more spatial tokens at identical parameter count; (ii) a lightweight ConvNeXt-512 reconstruction decoder with PixelShuffle upsampling that reduces pretraining VRAM by 7.5-8.5% per GPU relative to a UNet-128 baseline while matching its accuracy; and (iii) an empirical characterization of FP16 mixed-precision pretraining instability in the standard SparK UNet-768 decoder on S1GRD data, complemented by a finding that finetuning with layer-wise learning rate decay (LLRD) improves downstream performance of pretrained encoders. On four Copernicus-Bench S1GRD tasks, our finetuned 27.8M-parameter ConvNeXt-RS encoder improves over a non-pretrained baseline by +4.7, +5.0, +5.8, and +1.4 pp on EuroSAT-S1, BigEarthNet-S1, DFC2020-S1, and Flood-S1 respectively, and outperforms the Copernicus-FM ViT (139M parameters, frozen) on three of four tasks at roughly 5x fewer parameters. A pretraining data-scaling study reveals task-dependent data efficiency: classification saturates with as little as 10-25% of the SSL4EO-S12 v1.1 corpus, while dense prediction continues to improve significantly up to 25-50%. The study also includes a CKA representation analysis and a geographic de-collision experiment.

</details>


### [94] [Visual Sanad: Auditing Vision-Language Models for False Acceptance of Qur'anic Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22009)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Reyana Shahid,Evan Maksud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Visual Sanad 基准，用 2400 张不同视觉呈现的《古兰经》经文引用图像，测试视觉语言模型能否正确验证引用真伪，发现模型间存在明显能力分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多用于解读具有文化敏感性的视觉内容，但其在引用验证上的可靠性尚未被充分研究，尤其是视觉呈现方式是否会影响模型对错误引用的判断。

Method: 构建包含 100 条英文《古兰经》经文、每条 4 种事实状态、6 种视觉条件（从纯文本到装饰性、权威框架式排版）的 2400 张渲染图像基准；所有引用-参考文献对在渲染前均经过来源核查与人工审核；共进行 24 组完整模型-提示运行。

Result: 模型能力出现明显分化：专有 API 模型从近乎完美到中等程度的错误接受不等；近期开源权重模型辨别力较弱，多数图像默认判为 VALID。去偏提示可降低错误接受率，但往往只是将决策阈值推向 INVALID，而非真正提升引用辨别能力。

Conclusion: Visual Sanad 揭示了一种植根于文化的多模态事实性失效：流畅的视觉语言模型可能将展示的宗教引用视为自证真实，除非其具备强大的引用验证能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret religious, educational, and culturally sensitive visual content, yet their reliability on citation verification remains underexplored. We introduce Visual Sanad, a controlled benchmark for testing whether VLMs falsely accept incorrect Qur'anic verse attributions when the same quote-reference pair is rendered under different visual framings. The benchmark contains 2,400 rendered images built from 100 English Qur'an verses, four factual states per verse, and six visual conditions ranging from plain text to decorative and authority-framed layouts. All quote-reference pairs were source-checked and human-audited before rendering. Across 24 full model-prompt runs, we observe a sharp capability split. Proprietary API-served VLMs range from near-perfect citation verification to moderate false acceptance, whereas recent open-weight VLMs often show weak discrimination and default to VALID on most images. De-biased prompting can reduce false acceptance, but often by shifting the decision threshold toward INVALID rather than by producing strong citation discrimination. Visual Sanad therefore exposes a culturally grounded multimodal factuality failure: fluent VLMs may treat displayed religious citations as self-authenticating unless they have strong citation-verification ability.

</details>


### [95] [When Models Misread Ink: Auditing VLMs for Arabic Calligraphy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22018)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Evan Maksud,Reyana Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了12个视觉语言模型在阿拉伯书法六分类任务上的表现，发现最佳准确率仅60.9%，主要失效模式是"默认标签坍缩"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯书法对穆斯林社群具有重要文化意义，但现有VLM评测很少测试其细粒度书体传统，需要构建一个文化根基扎实的鲁棒性压力测试。

Method: 使用清洗后的1,195张图像六分类基准，对12个VLM进行评测，采用数值提示、视觉线索提示和Diwani优先提示三种策略。

Result: 闭集设置下，闭源/API模型最佳数值提示准确率为60.9%，开源权重模型为47.2%；每次数值运行都被单一标签主导（占预测的33-63%，而多数类基线仅18.8%）；12个模型中有8个默认预测Thuluth，GPT-4o将52.9%图像标为Thuluth（实际仅占18.1%）；提示效果不均衡，视觉线索对部分模型提升超10个百分点却损害其他模型。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯书法识别可作为VLM鲁棒性的紧凑、文化根基型压力测试，用于衡量哪些视觉传统能被自动化系统识别，提示往往只是转移默认标签而非消除它。

Abstract: Arabic calligraphy is a high-stakes cultural visual domain for Muslim communities, yet VLM evaluations rarely test its fine-grained script traditions. We audit 12 VLMs on a cleaned 1,195-image six-way benchmark using numeric, visual-cue, and Diwani-first prompts. In this closed-set setting, the best numeric-prompt accuracy is 60.9% for closed/API models and 47.2% for open-weight models. The main failure is default-label collapse: every numeric run is dominated by a single label taking 33-63% of predictions, against an 18.8% majority-class baseline. For 8 of 12 models the default is Thuluth; GPT-4o labels 52.9% of all images as Thuluth although it is only 18.1% of the set. Prompting helps unevenly: visual cues move accuracy by more than 10 points for some models but hurt others, and foregrounding Diwani often relocates the default rather than removing it. Arabic calligraphy recognition is therefore a compact, culturally grounded stress test for VLM robustness and for measuring which visual traditions become legible to automated systems.

</details>


### [96] [FeaTok: Feature-Loss Tokenization for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22060)
*Zhang Xiaofeng,Samuel Lavoie,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Felix Friedrich,Jakob Verbeek,Aaron Courville,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero-Soriano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出特征损失分词方法 FeaTok，通过重建预训练特征而非像素来训练离散 token，显著提升生成性能并降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图像生成器需要建模分词器产生的 token 分布，这些 token 表征应易于生成器建模，而不仅仅是支持忠实的图像重建。但现有分词器通常以重建像素为目标训练，这会优先保证低层保真度，可能破坏对生成有用的表征结构。

Method: 受特征预测式自监督学习启发，提出特征损失分词（FeaTok），通过重建预训练特征而非像素来训练离散 token。并在 SigLIP、SigLIP2、DINOv2、DINOv3 上进行消融实验。

Result: 在多个特征骨干上均显示更好生成性能。类条件生成中，FeaTok 推进质量-计算帕累托前沿，256x256 下 gFDr6 相对最强离散基线最多降低 29%，并以少 38 倍采样计算量超过最强无引导连续基线 RAE2；人类偏好奖励模型得分高于先前工作且无需奖励训练；仅需 500 次微调更新即在三个基准上超越连续与离散文本条件基线。

Conclusion: 以重建预训练特征替代重建像素来训练离散分词器，可产生更利于生成建模的 token 表征，在质量、计算效率和人类偏好等方面全面优于现有离散与连续基线。

Abstract: Discrete image generators learn the distribution of tokens produced by an image tokenizer. These token representations should be easy for generators to model, not merely enable faithful image reconstruction. Yet these tokenizers are commonly trained to reconstruct pixels, which prioritizes low-level fidelity and may disrupt representation structure useful for generation. Motivated by feature-predictive self-supervised learning, we introduce feature-loss tokenization (FeaTok), which trains discrete tokens by reconstructing pretrained features rather than pixels. Ablations across SigLIP, SigLIP2, DINOv2, and DINOv3 show better generative performance with FeaTok. In class-conditional generation, FeaTok advances the quality-compute Pareto frontier, reducing gFDr6 at 256x256 by up to 29 percent over the strongest discrete baselines and outperforming the strongest unguided continuous baseline (RAEv2) with 38 times less sampling compute. FeaTok also achieves higher human-preference reward-model scores than prior art without reward-based training and surpasses continuous and discrete text-conditional baselines on three benchmarks with only 500 fine-tuning updates after pre-training.

</details>


### [97] [Memory as Weights: Internalizing Long-Term History for Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22070)
*Zhuoming Liu,Jinhong Lin,Lin Zhang,Yiwu Zhong,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaLoW将历史上下文内化为不断更新的轻量级LoRA参数，用循环HyperNetwork实现流式视频记忆，避免历史与当前观测争抢上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视觉语言模型需持续整合历史信息并保持对当前观测与用户查询的响应。现有记忆机制通过压缩、检索或缓存历史观测并重新注入输入上下文，使历史记忆与当前观测争夺有限上下文空间。作者希望历史不再占用上下文，而是以参数形式携带。

Method: 提出MaLoW流式记忆框架，使用循环HyperNetwork将不断到来的视频观测增量地固化进固定大小的LoRA权重中，从而以权重表示历史、以输入表示当前观测，无需回看完整视频流。同时构建MaLoW-Instruct大规模流式视频数据集，含56K视频上的381K问答对，用于支持训练。

Result: 在OVO-Bench、StreamingBench和StreamArena上取得较强表现。在OVO-Bench的backward分片上，MaLoW在不同视频骨干上比先前记忆方法高出3.9%至11.8%，且延迟开销适中。此外方法可泛化到视觉-语言-动作模型，在Simpler WidowX上动作成功率提升9.3%。

Conclusion: 将历史上下文内化为演化式轻量参数更新，是缓解流式VLM上下文竞争的有效途径，并在流式视频理解与视觉-语言-动作任务上均展现出良好效果与泛化性。

Abstract: Streaming vision-language models (VLMs) must continuously integrate information from the past while remaining responsive to current observations and user queries. Existing memory mechanisms typically compress, retrieve, or cache historical observations and reintroduce them into the input context, forcing historical memory and current observations to compete for limited context. We propose MaLoW, a streaming memory framework that instead internalizes historical context into evolving lightweight parameter updates. MaLoW uses a recurrent HyperNetwork to incrementally consolidate incoming video observations into fixed-size LoRA weights, allowing historical information to be carried forward without revisiting the full video stream. In doing so, MaLoW represents history as weights, while keeping the current observation as input. To support training, we further construct MaLoW-Instruct, a large-scale streaming video dataset containing 381K question-answer pairs over 56K videos. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate that MaLoW achieves strong performance on OVO-Bench, StreamingBench, and StreamArena. On the backward split of OVO-Bench, MaLoW outperforms prior memory approaches by 3.9% to 11.8% across different video backbones while incurring only modest latency overhead. Beyond streaming video understanding, MaLoW also generalizes to vision-language-action models, improving the action success rate on Simpler WidowX by 9.3%.

</details>


### [98] [Retrofitting Recurrent Vision-Language Models from Pretrained Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22075)
*Madhav Iyengar,Andreas Geiger,Hilde Kühne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作将深度循环（depth recurrence）范式从预训练语言模型迁移到视觉理解任务，通过对VLM语言骨干剪枝并循环复用部分层，在减少23.3%语言参数的情况下，性能超越同等恢复数据训练的未剪枝基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明深度循环可被回填进预训练语言模型，但该范式能否用于减少预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的参数规模尚不明确。作者希望用深度循环来压缩VLM语言骨干的参数占用。

Method: 剪掉VLM语言骨干中的部分层，并对剩余层的子集进行循环复用。每次循环中，线性适配器将该块输出与前面各层的token表示融合，再投影回该块。由于剪枝损害原模型性能，提出三阶段恢复训练：先做纯语言预训练恢复语言骨干，再对齐视觉-语言投影器，最后通过视觉指令微调进行全量微调；第一与第三阶段还使用预训练非循环模型的输出做蒸馏。在TinyLLaVA-1.4B骨干上评测，回填过程不引入新视觉-语言数据。

Result: 回填后的循环模型在相同恢复数据下优于未剪枝基线，同时语言参数减少23.3%。该回填循环方案也优于将循环LM转换为VLM的方案。消融显示循环间适配器与更大循环块等设计选择带来性能提升。模型在推理导向基准上获得收益，基础视觉理解基准与基线持平。

Conclusion: 深度循环回填是一种有效的VLM压缩范式，可在不新增视觉-语言数据的情况下显著减少语言参数，并在推理类任务上取得提升，为视觉理解模型的高效化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent work shows that depth recurrence can be retrofitted into pretrained language models. In this work, we adapt this paradigm for visual understanding, using depth recurrence to reduce the parameter footprint of a pretrained vision-language model (VLM). To this end, we prune layers from the language backbone of the VLM and loop over a subset of the remaining layers. At each step of the loop, a linear adapter merges the block's output with the token representations from the preceding layers and projects the fused representation back into the block. As the pruning impacts the original model performance, we propose a three-stage recovery training: first, we leverage a language-only pretraining to recover the performance of the language backbone, followed by an alignment of the vision-language projector, and a full fine-tuning of the model via visual instruction tuning. In the first and the last stage, we distill the outputs of the pretrained non-recurrent model into the recurrent model, which further improves the recovery. We evaluate the proposed approach on the TinyLLaVA-1.4B backbone without introducing any new vision-language data during retrofitting. Our experiments demonstrate that the retrofitted recurrent model outperforms the unpruned baseline trained with the same recovery data while storing 23.3% fewer language parameters. We further show that the proposed retrofitting recurrence outperforms the setup of converting a recurrent LM into a VLM on the same data. Additionally, our ablations show how design choices, such as the adapter between recurrences and a larger recurrent block, lead to the improved performance of our approach. Finally, we find that our proposed model gains on reasoning-oriented benchmarks, whereas its performance on basic visual understanding benchmarks remains on par with the baseline.

</details>


### [99] [Pathology foundation models for training-free case retrieval in salivary gland tumors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22087)
*Maximilian J. Roesel,Niklas Kiermeyer,Philipp Keyl,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用病理基础模型进行基于内容的检索，无需任务特定训练即可辅助罕见癌症诊断，通过对137例唾液腺肿瘤的留一病例法评估，发现Atlas2等模型在Top-5检索和分类F1上表现良好，并可用改进的Chamfer相似度区分正确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理模式识别依赖长期训练和经验建立的参照框架，但面对罕见癌症亚型时仍可能不足。已诊断肿瘤的档案中常含有相关先例，但识别最相关病例十分困难。研究旨在探索能否借助病理基础模型的内容检索，在无需任务特定训练的情况下辅助罕见癌症诊断。

Method: 病理学家可用全切片图像（WSI）中的感兴趣区域（ROI）查询数字化档案，检索形态相似的病例及其ICD-O编码和对应WSI。ROI可手动选择，也可用门控注意力多实例学习模型自动选择。研究在137例唾液腺肿瘤、涵盖12个ICD-O形态类别的非平衡数据集上进行留一病例法评估，比较四种病理基础模型（Atlas2、Atlas2-B、Atlas2-S和UNI2h），并采用改进的Chamfer相似度分析Top-5匹配。

Result: Top-1/3/5检索准确率分别为：Atlas2 62.5%/76.7%/81.3%，UNI2h 50.2%/66.6%/72.9%，Atlas2-B 52.3%/71.5%/77.6%，Atlas2-S 54.4%/65.2%/73.7%。在至少含十例的五个类别上，宏平均F1从最小模型Atlas2-S的69.0%到最大模型Atlas2的76.6%。改进的Chamfer相似度在Atlas2（AUC 0.754）上比Atlas2-B（0.679，p=0.020）和Atlas2-S（0.653，p=0.003）更可靠地区分Top-5中的正确与错误匹配，但与UNI2h（0.697，p=0.196）无显著差异。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外训练或手动标注，即可用病理基础模型使科室档案可检索，为基于病例的鉴别诊断提供低门槛途径，并将个体的参照框架扩展到科室的集体经验。

Abstract: In pathology, pattern recognition relies on a frame of reference built through extensive training and experience, which may still fall short when encountering rare cancer subtypes. Archives of previously diagnosed tumors often contain relevant precedents, yet identifying the most relevant cases remains challenging. Here we explore whether content-based retrieval with current pathology foundation models facilitates rare cancer diagnostics without any task-specific training. Pathologists can query a digitized archive using a region of interest (ROI) from a whole slide image (WSI) and retrieve morphologically similar cases, along with their ICD-O codes and corresponding WSIs. The ROI can be selected manually or automatically using a gated attention-based multiple instance learning model. We evaluate our approach by leave-one-case-out on an imbalanced dataset of 137 salivary gland tumor cases spanning 12 ICD-O morphology categories, comparing four pathology foundation models (Atlas2, Atlas2-B, Atlas2-S, and UNI2h). Top-1/3/5 retrieval was 62.5%/76.7%/81.3% for Atlas2, 50.2/66.6/72.9% for UNI2h, 52.3%/71.5%/77.6% for Atlas2-B and 54.4/65.2/73.7% for Atlas2-S. Macro-averaged F1 ranged from 69.0% for the smallest model Atlas2-S to 76.6% for the largest model Atlas2 across the five classes with at least ten cases. Our modified Chamfer similarity separated correct from incorrect matches among the Top-5 more reliably for Atlas2 (AUC 0.754) than for Atlas2-B (0.679, p = 0.020) and Atlas2-S (0.653, p = 0.003), but not UNI2h (0.697, p = 0.196). Requiring no additional training or manual annotation, this approach makes departmental archives searchable with pathology foundation models, providing a low-barrier route to case-based differential diagnosis and extending the individual frame of reference to the collective experience of the department.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond the Dominant Label: Recovering Coexisting Lesion Evidence with Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22091)
*Lubin Gan,Jing Zhang,Xinming Zhang,Chengpeng Xu,Siying Wu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DomSet-MIL框架，将弱监督全切片图像中"仅标注最严重诊断"的设定建模为主导标签多实例学习，通过标签感知设计同时提升主导标签预测与潜在共存病灶证据的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像常含共存病灶，但弱监督标注通常只给最严重的诊断。标准多分类MIL将其视为完整one-hot标签，从而在主导病灶共现时压制了低严重度病灶的证据。作者希望在不牺牲次要临床发现的前提下改善诊断预测。

Method: 提出DomSet-MIL标签感知框架，在包级与实例级对齐标注机制：1）主导标签删失似然（Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood），在不宣称未报告病灶不存在的前提下建模报告诊断；2）严重度有序残差路由（Severity-Ordered Residual Routing），按严重度从高到低分配patch证据并为次要发现保留残差支持；3）top-q池化将局部路由分数与包级预测关联。

Result: 在自然与受控实验设置下，DomSet-MIL在主导标签预测上达到最先进性能，同时更有效地恢复潜在的共存病灶证据。

Conclusion: 对主导标签机制进行建模可以在不牺牲临床上有意义的次要发现的情况下改善诊断预测，表明显式建模标注机制对弱监督医学图像分析具有价值。

Abstract: Whole-slide images may contain coexisting lesions, yet weak supervision typically provides only the most severe diagnosis. We formulate this setting as dominant-label multiple instance learning, where the observed label is the maximum-severity element of a latent lesion set. Standard multiclass MIL treats it as a complete one-hot description and therefore suppresses lower-severity evidence whenever it co-occurs with a dominant lesion. We propose DomSet-MIL, a label-aware framework that aligns the annotation mechanism across bag and instance levels. Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood models the reported diagnosis without declaring unreported lesions absent. Severity-Ordered Residual Routing assigns patch evidence from high to low severity and preserves residual support for secondary findings, while top-$q$ pooling links local routing scores to bag prediction. Experiments across natural and controlled settings show that DomSet-MIL achieves state-of-the-art performance in dominant-label prediction while more effectively recovering latent coexisting-lesion evidence. These findings demonstrate that modeling the dominant-label mechanism can improve diagnostic prediction without sacrificing clinically meaningful secondary findings.

</details>


### [101] [SCCL-DT: Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning with Dual-Rule Teacher Distillation for Zero-Shot Chest X-Ray Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22094)
*Ting Liu,Haoran Lai,HaobinChen,Ming Liu,S Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对胸片视觉-语言预训练中"语义冲突"假阴性问题，提出SCCL方法，通过EMA统计与自适应阈值硬掩码，并结合双教师蒸馏得到SCCL-DT，在五个胸片基准上实现0.850的平均零样本AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对比学习把批次内所有非对角图像-报告对视为负样本，但放射学中不同患者常出现相同发现，实例不匹配并不等于语义不兼容，由此产生被称为"语义冲突"的假阴性监督。固定阈值会随相似度分布漂移而失准，软重加权又残留排斥梯度。

Method: 提出语义冲突感知对比学习（SCCL）：用EMA统计追踪模态特定相似度分布并导出自适应阈值，在softmax归一化前对标记候选进行硬掩码；用包容（OR）与共识（AND）规则定义两个教师，通过标准蒸馏将其双向跨模态排序整合为单一可部署学生模型SCCL-DT。

Result: 在五个公开胸片基准上，SCCL-DT的平均零样本AUC为0.850，优于所评估最强基线的0.837。

Conclusion: 训练轨迹审计显示在线掩码选出的成对观测富集了报告来源的发现一致性（该代理分析不足以确立临床等价性）。SCCL通过自适应硬掩码与双教师蒸馏有效缓解语义冲突，提升零样本胸片诊断性能，代码将公开。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language pre-training supports zero-shot chest X-ray diagnosis, but standard objectives treat every off-diagonal image-report pair in a batch as a negative. In radiology, recurring findings across patients mean that instance mismatch need not imply semantic incompatibility, creating false-negative supervision that we term a semantic conflict. A training-trace audit shows that pair-observations selected by the online mask used during training are enriched for report-derived finding agreement, although this proxy analysis does not establish clinical equivalence. Fixed thresholds can become miscalibrated as similarity distributions evolve, while soft reweighting leaves residual repulsive gradients. We therefore propose Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning (SCCL), which tracks modality-specific similarity distributions with exponential-moving-average (EMA) statistics, derives adaptive thresholds, and hard-masks flagged candidates before softmax normalization. Inclusive (OR) and consensus (AND) rules define two SCCL teachers, whose bidirectional cross-modal rankings are consolidated through standard distillation into a single deployable student, SCCL-DT. Across five public chest X-ray benchmarks, SCCL-DT achieves a mean zero-shot AUC of $0.850$, compared with $0.837$ for the strongest evaluated baseline in our comparison. Code will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [102] [FALCON: FPGA-Accelerated Latency-Certifiable Occupancy Networks for Camera-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22143)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON，一个部署在AMD Kria KR260嵌入式FPGA上的摄像头3D占据前端，通过将延迟保证分层（PL计算周期级有界、推理测量包络、看门狗强制输出）实现每帧截止时间保证，并在100k帧轨迹上验证了最坏情况延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知系统（如为行人刹车的系统）不需要低平均延迟，而是需要在每一帧、包括最坏情况下都在截止时间前给出答案。但商用边缘加速器通过动态调度、OS抖动、热节流和资源竞争来优化平均延迟，其逐帧延迟可测量却无法给出边界。因此需要一种能够提供明确延迟保证的方案。

Method: 作者在AMD Kria KR260嵌入式FPGA上构建FALCON摄像头3D占据前端，将延迟保证分层且显式化：可编程逻辑(PL)计算在分析上具有周期边界；推理基于100k帧轨迹得到测量包络；看门狗强制输出交付，当帧迟到时发布上一个输出并标记为过期。截止时间运行使用固定工作代理（每帧六次ResNet-50 DPU推理）而非完整占据网络。DPU无法运行的两个操作——视图变换gather和双线性resize——实现为固定跳数计数的PL IP，并与软件参考数值匹配。

Result: 在三次100k帧强制运行中，全部300k代理帧都在100ms截止时间加上最多10.2ms（三次运行中最差的一次）内交付输出。完整六摄像头FlashOcc流水线在板上每帧约1.3s，尚未达到10Hz。在完整Occ3D-nuScenes验证集上，板卡达到mIoU 29.88，为DPU无法运行的FP32基线的92.8%。在100k帧的ResNet-50探针上，KR260尾延迟保持在中位数的1.5倍以内（六推理代理为2.0倍），而Jetson Orin达到4.4倍，最差的数据中心GPU（NVIDIA L4）运行达到23.6倍。

Conclusion: 分层显式的延迟保证方法使嵌入式FPGA在逐帧最坏情况延迟上显著优于商用边缘加速器和数据中心GPU，同时保持有竞争力的精度。部署方案、HLS源码和原始时序轨迹已公开。

Abstract: A perception system that must brake for a pedestrian does not need a low average latency. It must deliver an answer before the deadline on every frame, the worst case included. Commodity edge accelerators optimize the mean through dynamic scheduling, OS jitter, thermal throttling, and contention, so their per-frame latency can be measured but not bounded. We build FALCON, a camera-based 3D occupancy front-end on the AMD Kria KR260, an embedded FPGA drawing 4.3 W idle and up to 6.6 W under inference, and make the latency guarantee layered and explicit: programmable-logic (PL) compute is cycle-bounded analytically; inference is a measured envelope over 100k-frame traces; and a watchdog enforces output delivery, publishing the previous output flagged stale when a frame is late. The deadline runs use a fixed-work proxy of six ResNet-50 DPU inferences per frame, not the full occupancy network: across three 100k-frame enforced runs, all 300k proxy frames delivered an output within the 100 ms deadline plus at most 10.2 ms, the worst of the three runs. The full six-camera FlashOcc pipeline composes to about 1.3 s per frame on the board and is not yet a 10 Hz system. The two operations the DPU cannot run, a view-transform gather and a bilinear resize, become fixed-trip-count PL IPs numerically matched to their software references. On the full Occ3D-nuScenes validation set the board reaches mIoU 29.88, 92.8% of an FP32 baseline the DPU cannot run. On a ResNet-50 probe over 100k frames the KR260 tail stays within 1.5 times its median (2.0 times for the six-inference proxy), where Jetson Orin reaches 4.4 times and the worst datacenter GPU run (NVIDIA L4) 23.6 times. The deployment recipe, HLS sources, and raw timing traces are released.

</details>


### [103] [MagiC: Evaluating Multimodal Cognition Toward Grounded Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07297)
*Chengfei Wu,Ronald Seoh,Bingxuan Li,Liqiang Zhang,Fengrong Han,Dan Goldwasser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MagiC 基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型是否真正进行有依据的多模态推理，而非依赖表面模式与数据偏差。基准包含约5500个弱监督 QA 样例和900个人工精心标注样例，并在四个维度上评估了15个7B至70B参数的模型，同时引入 MagiScore 和 StepSense 新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型在视觉问答和多模态推理上表现亮眼，但不清楚其是否真正执行了有视觉依据的推理，还是仅依赖表面模式与数据集偏差。现有评估多聚焦答案准确性，缺乏对逐步推理质量及其与视觉证据一致性的考察。

Method: 作者构建了 MagiC 综合基准，包含约5500个由强模型输出生成的弱监督 QA 样例，以及900个带细粒度人工标注（答案、推理依据、边界框 grounding）的样例。评估15个7B至70B参数的视觉语言模型，覆盖四个维度：最终答案正确性、推理有效性、grounding 保真度和自我纠错能力。此外设置诊断场景，考察模型在对抗性视觉线索下的鲁棒性与内省式纠错能力，并提出 MagiScore 与 StepSense 等新指标。

Result: 通过所提出的基准与指标，对15个视觉语言模型进行了多维度评测与综合分析，揭示了当前 grounded 视觉推理方法中的关键局限与潜在机会。

Conclusion: MagiC 为评估多模态认知的视觉依据性提供了系统性基准和新的度量方式，表明现有模型在有依据的视觉推理方面仍存在明显不足，并为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models have led to impressive performance in visual question answering and multimodal reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely perform grounded visual reasoning or rely on superficial patterns and dataset biases. In this work, we introduce MagiC, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate grounded multimodal cognition, assessing not only answer accuracy but also the quality of step-by-step reasoning and its alignment with relevant visual evidence. Our benchmark includes approximately 5,500 weakly supervised QA examples generated from strong model outputs and 900 human-curated examples with fine-grained annotations, including answers, rationales, and bounding box groundings. We evaluate 15 vision-language models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters across four dimensions: final answer correctness, reasoning validity, grounding fidelity, and self-correction ability. MagiC further includes diagnostic settings to probe model robustness under adversarial visual cues and assess their capacity for introspective error correction. We introduce new metrics such as MagiScore and StepSense, and provide comprehensive analyses that reveal key limitations and opportunities in current approaches to grounded visual reasoning.

</details>


### [104] [Motion- and Gravity-Aware Feedforward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22177)
*Shiyao Qian,Yuan Ren,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于特权信息学习（LUPI）的动感与重力感知框架，用于多视角前馈式3D重建，在不增加推理复杂度的情况下提升动态场景重建精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式Transformer在多视角3D重建中表现良好，但主要依赖外观注意力，在动态场景和几何线索较弱时性能受限。作者希望在不增加测试阶段计算负担的前提下，引入运动与物理先验来增强几何表示学习。

Method: 采用LUPI范式：训练阶段将残差光流和重力方向作为特权信号，用于指导几何表示学习；推理阶段仅使用标准视觉输入。通过轻量级结构改动，将上述先验注入Transformer骨干网络，保持前馈效率。

Result: 在标准基准上，重建精度与鲁棒性均有稳定提升，尤其在含动态物体、结构模糊和相机运动复杂的场景中表现更优。分析表明最优注入阶段依赖骨干网络：对VGGT而言中层融合通常更优，对π3则后层融合更有效。

Conclusion: LUPI为前馈式3D重建提供了一种实用途径，可在不增加测试时复杂度的条件下注入运动与物理先验，并为不同骨干网络的特权信息注入策略提供了经验性指导。

Abstract: Feed-forward transformer models have recently achieved strong performance in multi-view 3D reconstruction, but they still rely primarily on appearance-based attention and often struggle in dynamic scenes with weak geometric cues. In this work, we introduce a motion- and gravity-aware framework under the Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI) paradigm. During training, residual optical flow and gravity direction are provided as privileged signals to guide geometric representation learning; during inference, the model uses standard visual inputs only.
  We integrate these priors into transformer backbones with lightweight architectural changes, preserving feed-forward efficiency. Experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent gains in reconstruction accuracy and robustness, especially in scenes with dynamic objects, ambiguous structure, and challenging camera motion. Our analysis further shows that the optimal injection stage is backbone-dependent: middle-layer integration is generally preferable for VGGT, while late-stage integration is more effective for π3. These results suggest that LUPI offers a practical way to inject motion and physical priors into feed-forward 3D reconstruction without increasing test-time complexity.

</details>


### [105] [LAMP: Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors for Training-Free Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22182)
*Zhipeng Ye,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LAMP，一种免训练框架，通过逐层自适应混合预测器融合编码器多层特征，提升小样本分类，并在多数据集与骨干网络上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本适配多依赖编码器最终全局表示，中间层的互补信息未被充分利用；而直接融合不同层特征因维度不一致、任务间效用差异大而困难。

Method: LAMP 对每个视图单次前向获取各层描述子；全局与经支持集校准的区域核预测器分别拟合同一标签残差，在类分数空间用带标签验证数据选择混合权重，保持原生特征维度、无需学习投影。LAMP-F 进一步自适应文本锚定的类原型，利用缓存响应精确更新固定核残差预测。

Result: 在 11 个数据集及 ViT、ResNet 骨干上评测：LAMP 在 4/8/16-shot 下取得对比的已发表免训练 ResNet-50 方法中最优平均精度，16-shot 达 78.73%；五种支持集预算平均下，较仅使用全局特征的配对基线在 ViT-B/16 与 ResNet-50 上分别提升 1.14 与 1.59 个百分点，LAMP-F 提升至 1.96 与 2.54 个百分点，16-shot 精度分别为 83.57% 与 79.10%。

Conclusion: 受控实验表明多层融合是性能提升的主要来源，并考察了池化、校准、验证标签与计算开销；结合逐层预测可有效利用冻结编码器的互补特征以改进小样本分类。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation often relies on an encoder's final global representation, leaving complementary information in intermediate layers underused. Combining these features is challenging because their dimensions can differ and their usefulness varies across tasks. We propose LAMP, Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors, a training-free framework that combines global and regional information from multiple encoder layers. A single forward pass per view supplies all descriptors. Global and support-calibrated regional kernel predictors independently fit the same label residuals. Labeled validation data select their mixture in class-score space, preserving native feature dimensions without learned projections. LAMP-F further adapts text-anchored class prototypes, using a cached response to update the fixed-kernel residual predictions exactly. Experiments cover eleven datasets and ViT and ResNet backbones. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy among the compared published training-free ResNet-50 methods at 4, 8, and 16 shots, reaching 78.73% at 16 shots. Averaged across five support budgets, LAMP improves over a paired global-feature-only baseline by 1.14 and 1.59 percentage points on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50; LAMP-F raises these gains to 1.96 and 2.54 points. LAMP-F reaches 16-shot accuracies of 83.57% and 79.10%, respectively. Controlled experiments identify multi-layer integration as the main source of improvement and examine pooling, calibration, validation labels, and computational cost.
