These findings show that combining layer-specific predictions can exploit complementary features from frozen encoders to improve few-shot classification.

</details>


### [106] [EVA-Cap: Optimizing Audiovisual Video Captioning via Event-Centric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22186)
*Jiapeng Shi,Qiuxia Hou,Jiaqi Leng,Junke Wang,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVA-Cap框架，将音视频视频描述分解为原子音视频事件，通过SFT与GRPO训练，显著提升事件级对齐与描述质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态语言模型的视频描述方法将描述视为无结构文本生成，忽略了视频内在语义结构，导致细粒度音视频对齐效果不佳。

Method: 将描述分解为原子音视频事件，把无结构文本监督转化为以事件为中心的对齐；在自建数据集EVA-Data上采用SFT与GRPO训练，GRPO阶段针对全局事件完整性、事件内归因、事件间同步三个目标优化。

Result: 在事件为中心的评价上显著超越现有方法（ChronusAV平均提升9.0%），在通用音视频描述基准上保持竞争力（WorldSense提升3.3%）。

Conclusion: 事件为中心的结构化对齐能有效提升音视频视频描述的细粒度质量，兼顾事件级与通用基准表现。

Abstract: Recent advances in Omni Language Models (OLMs) provide a unified solution for audiovisual video captioning. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly treat captioning as an unstructured text generation process, neglecting the inherent semantic structure of videos and leading to suboptimal fine-grained audiovisual alignment. To bridge this gap, we propose EVA-Cap, a novel framework that decomposes captions into atomic audiovisual events and transforms unstructured text supervision into event-centric alignment. We train EVA-Cap via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on a self-curated dataset, EVA-Data, which is built with event-centric quality control. During the GRPO phase, the model is explicitly optimized against three event-centric alignment targets: global event completeness, intra-event attribution, and inter-event synchronization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA-Cap significantly outperforms existing methods in event-centric evaluation (e.g., +9.0% average gain on ChronusAV), while achieving competitive performance on general audiovisual captioning benchmarks (e.g., +3.3% on WorldSense).

</details>


### [107] [OpenGCUA: Toward a Fully Open Stack for Generalist Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22200)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Long Li,Quyu Kong,Lei Wang,Panrong Tong,Long Chen,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Xu Zhang,Chen Liu,Jianan Zhang,Chenglin Cai,Zhengwen Zeng,Steven Hoi,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出通用计算机使用智能体（GCUA）概念，并发布首个开源版本 OpenGCUA，通过代码执行与"harness-to-model 决策迁移"提升跨界面任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿计算机使用智能体（如 GPT-6 Astra 的系统）多为闭源实现，限制了对其设计的系统性研究，因此需要向社区开放完整技术栈。

Method: 将 GCUA 定义为模型自主选择如何观察、行动并组织跨视觉与编程接口工作的系统；设计中引入代码执行能力，使中间结果决定后续操作，并让模型直接复用已有编程能力；同时提出 harness-to-model 决策转移，将部分观察与任务状态决策从固定 harness 规则移交给模型。

Result: 在 OSWorld-v2 上，完整配置在八个评测模型（含 GPT-6 Astra、Claude Opus 5）上均提升任务得分，平均部分得分提高 +19.6 分，报告步数平均减少 53.0%；发布实现、提示词、八个模型的评测轨迹、真实设备 harness 与跨设备 SDK 的初步版本；在四个真实 macOS 任务中，Qwen3.8-Max 的推理 token 用量为 GPT-6 Astra 的 19.4 倍。

Conclusion: 代码执行与决策迁移是提升通用计算机使用智能体性能与效率的有效设计，开源全栈有助于社区推进该方向研究，且不同模型在真实设备执行效率上差异显著。

Abstract: Computer use has reached a turning point: the focus is expanding from GUI-only interaction to systems that complete tasks on users' computers through visual and programmatic interfaces. We define a generalist computer-use agent (GCUA) as a system in which the model chooses how to observe, act, and organize its work across visual and programmatic interfaces. Yet frontier implementations, such as GPT-6 Astra's computer-use system, remain largely closed, limiting systematic study of their design. We introduce Open Generalist CUA (OpenGCUA), an ongoing project to develop and open the full stack for GCUAs with the community. This report presents the first release of OpenGCUA. Code execution is a key feature of our agent design: it lets intermediate results determine subsequent operations and enables models to apply their existing programming capabilities directly to computer interaction. We also propose harness-to-model decision transfer, moving selected observations and task-state decisions from fixed harness rules to the model. On OSWorld-v2, our full configuration improves task scores across all eight evaluated models, including GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5, raising mean partial score by +19.6 points and reducing reported steps per task by an average of 53.0% relative to batched GUI-only execution. We release our implementation and prompts, evaluation trajectories from all eight models, and preliminary versions of a real-device harness and a cross-device SDK. The harness addresses real-device challenges not fully captured by benchmarks, while the SDK provides a common programming interface across computers, mobiles and browsers. Across four real macOS tasks under our real-device harness, selected Qwen3.8-Max runs use 19.4 times as many reported reasoning tokens as GPT-6 Astra, highlighting substantial differences between models in real-device execution efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [TRACE: Topology-Aware Reconnection via Prediction-Adaptive Connectivity Enforcement for Thin-Structure Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22210)
*Junho Moon,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种拓扑感知、自适应的预测目标，通过骨架定义局部连接，在训练中随预测动态选择最易有效的重连瓶颈进行监督，提升细长结构的连通性与拓扑正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长的管状/丝状结构对连通性错误极为敏感，少数局部分割错误即可切断原本连续的路径。现有基于骨架的目标通常监督固定的中心线位置，未考虑可恢复连通性的其他局部路径，因此难以纠正预测过程中的断裂问题。

Method: TRACE利用目标骨架定义所需的局部连接；对每条连接，在当前预测下找出最易实现的有效重连，并将其瓶颈与对应骨架点联合监督，使监督随预测演化而自适应，而非固定于中心线。此外引入拓扑感知的骨架载体，同时保持前景拓扑与类别特定拓扑，以可靠地定义这些连接。

Result: 在六个2D与3D基准上开展大量实验，结果显示TRACE在保持较强分割精度的同时改善了连通性与拓扑质量，且训练开销较低。

Conclusion: TRACE通过预测自适应的拓扑感知监督有效缓解细长结构的分割断裂问题，兼顾连通性、拓扑与分割精度，训练成本低，代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Thin, elongated structures are particularly sensitive to connectivity errors, as even a few local segmentation mistakes can disrupt otherwise continuous paths. Existing skeleton-based objectives typically supervise fixed centerline locations and do not account for alternative local routes that may restore connectivity. We propose TRACE, a topology-aware, prediction-adaptive objective that uses a target skeleton to define required local connections. For each connection, TRACE identifies the easiest valid reconnection under the current prediction and jointly supervises its bottleneck with the corresponding skeleton point. The supervision thus adapts to the evolving prediction rather than remaining fixed to the centerline. We further introduce a topology-aware skeleton carrier that preserves both foreground and class-specific topology, enabling reliable definition of these connections. Extensive experiments across six 2D and 3D benchmarks show that TRACE improves connectivity and topology while maintaining strong segmentation accuracy with low training overhead. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [109] [FutureLift: Lifting Trajectory Proposals into Bidirectional Future Graphs for Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22215)
*Daniel Grimm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureLift是一种与主干网络无关的预测精修器，将每个多模态提议表示为异质未来图，通过双向时间边一次性修正并输出残差坐标与置信度修正，在Argoverse2和WOMD上持续提升冻结提议并取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精修器要么压缩完整提议，要么按顺序更新片段，无法让完整预测未来作为显式的关系上下文用于修正；同时不同预测主干结构差异大，缺乏统一精修接口。

Method: 将每个多模态提议建模为异质未来图，包含空间锚定的时间节点，并连接智能体历史、地图元素、备选模式以及联合预测场景下其他智能体的未来轨迹；采用双向时间边使后段证据可在单次前向中修正前段，更新后的节点预测残差坐标与置信度修正；仅需提议、分数和场景上下文。

Result: 在Argoverse2和Waymo Open Motion Dataset上持续改进冻结提议，在边际与联合预测中优于原生及专用精修器；在WOMD运动预测排行榜上，不使用LiDAR或集成的情况下，minADE为0.5049m、minFDE为0.9942m，居标准输入方法首位，Soft mAP达0.4874。

Conclusion: FutureLift提供通用、即插即用的精修接口，无需重训主干即可提升多种结构不同的预测模型，验证了以完整未来提议作为关系上下文进行残差修正的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate motion prediction is critical for safe autonomous driving, yet even strong forecasters leave residual errors in their multimodal proposals. Existing refiners compress complete proposals or update their segments sequentially, preventing the full proposed future from serving as explicit relational context for correction. We introduce FutureLift, a backbone-agnostic refiner that represents each multimodal proposal as a heterogeneous future graph, with spatially grounded temporal nodes connected to agent histories, map elements, alternative modes, and, for joint prediction, other agents future trajectories. Bidirectional temporal edges allow later evidence to revise earlier segments in a single pass, and the updated nodes predict residual coordinate and confidence corrections. Requiring only proposals, scores, and scene context, FutureLift provides a common refinement interface for structurally different forecasting backbones. On Argoverse2 and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, it consistently improves frozen proposals and outperforms native and specialized refiners across marginal and joint forecasting. On the WOMD motion-prediction leaderboard, FutureLift ranks first in minADE (0.5049m) and minFDE (0.9942m) among the compared standard-input methods, without LiDAR or ensembling, while achieving a competitive 0.4874 Soft mAP.

</details>


### [110] [Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation for Unified Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22217)
*Yuqi Xiong,Chunyi Peng,Zhipeng Xu,Qing Yang,Zhenghao Liu,Zulong Chen,Yu Gu,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOPED，通过技能解耦的在线策略专家蒸馏，将独立训练的视觉语言模型专家能力整合到统一学生模型中，在八个多模态基准上超越基线和混合能力强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可激发视觉语言模型的专门视觉能力，但联合优化异构目标会引入跨能力的梯度冲突；独立训练专家虽可缓解干扰，却可能将专业知识与奖励黑客偏差纠缠在一起。

Method: SCOPED 训练能力专属专家，并通过奖励引导的反思提取显式技能；在在线策略蒸馏中，这些技能用于指导专家监督，但对统一学生模型不可见，从而促使学生在推理时内化程序性知识而不依赖显式技能。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上，SCOPED 持续优于骨干模型和混合能力强化学习，验证了技能解耦在线策略专家蒸馏在整合独立获得能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 进一步分析揭示了联合能力优化中的干扰问题，并表明技能增强能促进更一致的专家轨迹、对任务相关视觉证据的更多关注，以及统一学生模型中专业知识的更好保留。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can elicit specialized visual capabilities in vision-language models (VLMs), yet jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives often introduces cross-capability gradient conflicts. Training experts independently mitigates this interference, but direct reward optimization may entangle specialized knowledge with reward-hacking biases. This paper proposes Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation (SCOPED), which trains capability-specific experts and extracts explicit skills through reward-guided reflection. During on-policy distillation, these skills guide expert supervision while remaining unavailable to the unified student, encouraging it to internalize procedural knowledge without explicit skills at inference time. Across eight multimodal benchmarks, SCOPED consistently outperforms the backbone and mixed-capability RL, demonstrating the effectiveness of skill-decoupled on-policy expert distillation for integrating independently acquired capabilities. Further analyses reveal interference during joint capability optimization and suggest that skill augmentation promotes more consistent expert trajectories, greater attention to task-relevant visual evidence, and better preservation of specialized expertise in the unified student. All code and data are available at https://github.com/NEUIR/SCOPED.

</details>


### [111] [How Much Physics Can Vision Learn from Audio? Probing and Distilling Damping Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22221)
*Zainab Afolabi,Vadim Selyutin,Zhao Gao,Babs Khalidson,Mohamed Ghoneim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 音频嵌入能有效编码材料阻尼特性（Q值），而视觉模型（单帧CLIP）表现较差；通过将冻结的音频教师模型蒸馏到冻结的CLIP骨干中，视觉模型可恢复部分音频优势，且四帧输入能进一步缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理理解对决策至关重要，但视觉模型难以推断如阻尼这类决定材料行为的属性。音频因直接记录物体受击的声学响应（可通过衰减测量阻尼）而蕴含此类信息，作者希望探究这种物理信号能否从音频迁移到视觉模型中。

Method: 作者用Spearman秩相关ρ探测冻结编码器对品质因数Q的排序能力；对比音频嵌入与CLIP单帧视频嵌入；随后通过训练投影头将冻结音频教师蒸馏到冻结CLIP骨干；并采用留一材料协议，在四种帧输入条件下评估蒸馏效果。

Result: 音频嵌入达到ρ=0.73，并在未见材料上保持优势，而CLIP单帧仅ρ=0.28。留一材料协议下，四帧输入蒸馏将相关从0.234提升至0.338（相对匹配四帧基线+0.053，17种材料中14种提升）；将视觉输入扩展为跨冲击的四帧可无音频代价地获得约一半提升。

Conclusion: 音频在编码阻尼等材料物理属性上显著优于视觉，且该优势可通过蒸馏部分迁移至视觉模型；单帧视觉所缺失的部分信息是可见的动态过程，扩大时间窗口能有效弥补这一差距。

Abstract: Physical understanding is crucial for decision making, yet vision models struggle to infer properties such as damping that govern how materials behave. Audio encodes such properties directly, because an object's acoustic response to impact reveals its damping through measurable decay. We probe frozen encoders for the quality factor $Q$, a dimensionless measure of how many oscillations an object completes before its ring dies away, and report how well each embedding orders impacts by $Q$ using the Spearman rank correlation $ρ$, for which $ρ=1$ is a perfect ordering and $ρ=0$ an unrelated one. Audio embeddings reach $ρ=0.73$ and retain most of that advantage on materials withheld from training, while a single video frame taken at the moment of impact, encoded by CLIP, supports only $ρ=0.28$. Having established where the signal lives, we ask how much of that gap vision can recover. We distill a frozen audio teacher into a frozen CLIP backbone through a trained projection head and find that this recovers a useful fraction of the audio advantage. Because pooled correlations on this task are dominated in practice by category identity, we also evaluate under a leave-one-material-out protocol in which the held-out material is withheld from the projection head as well as the probe. Distillation with a four-frame input improves the correlation there from 0.234 to 0.338 (+0.053 over the matched four-frame baseline, 14 of 17 materials), a gain that no category-level strategy can produce. Widening the visual input to four frames spanning the impact supplies half of that improvement at no acoustic cost, which indicates that part of what a single frame misses is visible dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [WaveLLM: Wavelet-Domain LLM Entropy Modeling for Lossless Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22232)
*Yue Yu,Bowei Kang,Changsheng Gao,Dong Liu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveLLM，一种基于小波域的LLM熵模型，用于无损图像压缩，通过将图像分解为多层小波子带将异构预测问题转化为更结构化的子问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的无损图像压缩方法主要强调LLM的建模能力，但直接将图像序列化为像素或残差序列，导致异构信号模式纠缠在单一预测流中。作者认为有效的熵建模不仅取决于模型容量，还取决于输入表示的可预测性。

Method: 将图像分解为多层小波子带，把异构预测问题转化为更结构化、统计上更均匀的子问题，更适合自回归概率估计。引入专门的数值接口，包括值嵌入和系数预测头，以连接数值编码符号与LLM的原生文本token空间。设计任务自适应提示策略，使单一共享模型能在统一框架内适应不同子带。基于Llama-3.2-3B并结合LoRA适配。

Result: 在Kodak、CLIC.pro和DIV2K上分别达到2.51、2.24和2.27 bpsp，比使用相同小波变换的PixelCNN编解码器分别优0.43、0.36和0.46 bpsp，并在三个基准上均超过近期基于LLM的无损压缩器。

Conclusion: 有效的熵建模不仅依赖模型容量，还依赖输入表示的可预测性；通过小波域分解与统一的LLM框架，可显著提升无损图像压缩性能。

Abstract: Lossless image compression fundamentally relies on accurate entropy modeling, driving a recent shift toward large language model (LLM)-based methods. However, existing approaches mainly emphasize the modeling capacity of LLMs while directly serializing images into pixel or residual sequences, leaving heterogeneous signal patterns entangled within a single prediction stream. In this paper, we argue that effective entropy modeling depends not only on model capacity, but also on the predictability of the input representation. Motivated by this insight, we propose WaveLLM, a wavelet-domain LLM-based entropy model for lossless image compression. By decomposing an image into multi-level wavelet subbands, WaveLLM converts a heterogeneous prediction problem into a set of more structured and statistically homogeneous subproblems that are more amenable to autoregressive probability estimation. To bridge numerical coding symbols with the LLM's native text token space, we introduce a dedicated numerical interface featuring value embeddings and a coefficient prediction head. Furthermore, a task-adaptive prompting strategy is designed to enable a single shared model to adapt to diverse subbands within a unified framework. Built on Llama-3.2-3B with LoRA adaptation, WaveLLM achieves 2.51, 2.24, and 2.27 bpsp on Kodak, CLIC.pro, and DIV2K, respectively, outperforming the PixelCNN-based codec using the same wavelet transform by 0.43, 0.36, and 0.46 bpsp, and surpassing recent LLM-based lossless compressors on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] [MMSearch-OPD: Easy Made Hard, Hard Made Learnable for Long-Horizon Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22240)
*Huanyao Zhang,Jiepeng Zhou,Jiaye Lin,Ontai Tang,Jingtian Hao,Mugeng Liu,Ru Peng,Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Liyan Liu,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMSearch-OPD，通过可控数据精炼与强化学习+在线策略蒸馏联合训练，提升长程多模态深度搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态深度搜索正从短查询转向需持续获取证据的长程调查，但训练此类智能体受限于缺乏具挑战性的训练问题和过程反馈不足。

Method: 方法包含两部分：其一为Refine流水线，对现有简单多模态问题推导的推理链进行定向扩展，通过参数化变换控制搜索深度与跨模态证据需求，并复用现有图文对；其二为训练策略，将答案奖励与轻量行为惩罚结合用于强化学习，同时用奖励差距调节对非完美学生轨迹的词元级教师指导，并采用指数衰减蒸馏系数随训练降低教师依赖。

Result: 实验表明数据精炼带来性能提升，且在SFT后进一步通过联合训练获得改进，显示出更强的长程多模态深度搜索能力。

Conclusion: 可控数据精炼与RL+OPD联合训练能有效缓解训练数据与过程反馈不足的问题，增强长程多模态深度搜索智能体能力。

Abstract: Multimodal deep search is moving from short queries to long-horizon investigations that continually acquire evidence. However, training such agents remains constrained by limited challenging training questions and insufficient process feedback.
  We introduce MMSearch-OPD, combining controllable data refinement with joint reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). Our Refine pipeline performs targeted expansion of reasoning chains derived from existing simple multimodal questions. Parameterized transformations control search depth and cross-modal evidence requirements while reusing existing image--question pairs. For training, answer rewards and lightweight behavior penalties guide RL, while the reward gap modulates token-level teacher guidance on imperfect student trajectories. An exponentially decaying distillation coefficient reduces teacher dependence as training proceeds.
  Experiments show gains from data refinement and further improvements from joint training after SFT, indicating stronger long-horizon multimodal deep search capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [FPG-Seg: Annotation-free Retinal Vessel Topology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22259)
*James Willoughby,Irina Voiculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FPG-Seg是一种无需人工标注的视网膜血管分割方法，融合经典血管性滤波、贝叶斯深度学习与拓扑感知图重建，在四个基准数据集上达到全监督基线86–98%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视网膜血管分割方法依赖大量标注，而标注过程耗时且高度依赖临床专业知识。作者希望摆脱这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出FPG-Seg流程：结合经典血管性滤波（vesselness filtering）、贝叶斯深度学习以及拓扑感知的图重建，实现无标注的血管拓扑分割，训练与超参数调优均不使用人工标注。

Result: 在四个常用的视网膜血管分割基准数据集上测试，clDice达到0.74–0.78，相当于等效全监督基线性能的86–98%。同时首次在FIVES数据集上给出无标注血管分割方法的性能基准。

Conclusion: 无需人工标注即可获得接近全监督方法的视网膜血管分割性能，显示无标注流程在该任务上的可行性，并为FIVES提供首个无标注基准。

Abstract: State-of-the art approaches to retinal vessel segmentation typically require extensive annotations, a highly time-consuming process, reliant on clinical expertise. We present FPG-Seg, an annotation-free pipeline for retinal vessel segmentation combining classical vesselness filtering, Bayesian deep learning and topology-aware graph reconstruction to segment retinal vessel topology without manual labels. Tested across four widely used benchmark datasets for retinal vessel segmentation, FPG-Seg attains a clDice of $0.74{-}0.78$, representing $86{-}98\%$ of the performance of an equivalent fully-supervised baseline, despite using no manual annotations for training or hyperparameter tuning. As part of this work, we also provide, to our knowledge, the first performance benchmark of an annotation-free vessel segmentation method on FIVES.

</details>


### [115] [SCOPE: Semantic Bounded Credit Optimization for Prompt Engineering in Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22264)
*Yufei Jiao,Jinfa Huang,Zeyue Xue,Tianyu Xie,Yuexiao Ma,Rongrong Ji,Xiawu Zheng,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE 提出一种基于 GRPO 的目标函数，通过将语义片段内的策略对数概率变化进行池化，使提示词重写的更新权重与语义结构对齐，在多个图像生成器上超越普通 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的提示词重写方法通常把来自图像的单一信号应用到各个子词 token 上，但对象、数量、属性、关系等实际构成连贯的语义片段，这种 token 级别的更新方式与语义结构不匹配。

Method: 重写器同时生成增强提示词及其语义划分；SCOPE 在每个语义片段内对策略对数概率的变化进行池化，得到该片段 token 损失的共享系数，同时为每个 completion 保留单一优势值，无需额外的片段奖励或额外图像渲染。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image、SD3 和 FLUX.1-dev 上，SCOPE 相对普通 GRPO 在 GeneVal 上平均相对提升 9.48%，在 T2I-CompBench 上提升 7.62%；消融实验表明语义边界与片段内池化均有贡献。

Conclusion: 语义结构可作为强化学习提示词工程中有效的归纳偏置，为 T2I 生成中的提示词优化提供实用方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables language models to improve text-to-image (T2I) generation by rewriting prompts for a frozen image generator. Existing methods typically apply a single signal derived from the image to individual subword tokens, although objects, counts, attributes, and relations form coherent semantic spans. We propose SCOPE, an objective based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that aligns update weighting with this structure. The rewriter jointly generates an enhanced prompt and its semantic partition. SCOPE pools shifts in policy log probabilities within each span to derive a shared coefficient for its token losses while retaining a single advantage for each completion. It requires neither separate span rewards nor additional image renders. Across Qwen-Image, SD3, and FLUX.1-dev, SCOPE achieves average relative gains over vanilla GRPO of 9.48% on GeneVal and 7.62% on T2I-CompBench. Ablation studies show that both semantic boundaries and pooling within spans contribute to these gains, supporting semantic structure as a practical inductive bias for prompt engineering with RL.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Pairwise Repulsion: Collective Diversity via Determinant Maximization in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22267)
*Kwanyoung Lee,SeungJu Cha,Yebin Ahn,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DetNO（Determinant Noise Optimization），将多样本生成建模为初始噪声上的RKHS体积最大化，通过最大化一步Tweedie预测的RBF Gram矩阵对数行列式实现批级多样性，无需训练或修改采样器，在多个骨干网络上取得领先的批内多样性与下游少样本分类增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型的强引导会使采样集中于条件分布的高密度模式，导致独立采样的噪声向量坍缩到几乎相同的视觉原型，限制了需要分布广度的任务（尤其是合成数据增强）。而多样性是批级的集合属性：一组样本即使保持所有成对距离，也可能坍缩到低维子空间，现有基于成对噪声优化与排斥的目标对这类高阶失效响应较弱。

Method: DetNO将多样生成表述为初始噪声上的RKHS体积最大化，即在一阶Tweedie预测上最大化RBF Gram矩阵的对数行列式。该集合级泛函等于批体积的平方，其排斥力在批最坍缩时最强，并随着多样性提升而自我衰减。方法加入基于锚点的保真项使优化噪声靠近合理区域，并通过拉格朗日松弛得到含单一超参数λ的两项目标，用于权衡多样性与质量，全程无需训练或修改采样器。

Result: 在SD1.5、SDXL、SD3三个骨干网络上，DetNO在VendiScore与Mean Self-Similarity上取得领先的批内多样性；下游少样本分类实验证实更广的模式覆盖在低样本场景下带来统计显著的合成数据增强精度提升。在固定流程、仅改变批目标的情况下，两种基于RBF的聚合器均优于InfoNCE，且对数行列式在两个基准上取得最佳下游精度；随批规模增大，其相对InfoNCE的多样性优势扩大。

Conclusion: DetNO通过集合级体积最大化目标有效缓解引导采样导致的批内坍缩，以单超参数实现多样性与质量权衡，无需训练或改采样器，在多样性与下游少样本增强任务上表现领先，且多样性优势随批规模增长而扩大。

Abstract: Strong guidance in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models concentrates sampling toward high-density modes of the conditional distribution, collapsing independently sampled noise vectors onto nearly identical visual prototypes. This limits utility for tasks that demand distributional breadth, most critically synthetic data augmentation. Diversity, however, is a collective property of the whole batch: a set can preserve every pairwise distance yet still collapse onto a low-dimensional subspace. Existing sum-of-pairwise noise-optimization and repulsion objectives respond only weakly to this higher-order failure. We propose DetNO (Determinant Noise Optimization), which formulates diverse generation as RKHS volume maximization on the initial noise, maximizing the log-determinant of an RBF Gram matrix over one-step Tweedie predictions. This set-level functional equals the squared batch volume; its repulsion is strongest when the batch is most collapsed and self-attenuates as diversity is achieved. An anchor-based fidelity term keeps the optimized noises near plausible regions, and Lagrangian relaxation gives a two-term objective with a single hyperparameter $λ$ governing the diversity-quality trade-off, without training or sampler modification. Across three backbones (SD1.5, SDXL, SD3), DetNO achieves leading intra-batch diversity in VendiScore and Mean Self-Similarity, and a downstream few-shot classification experiment confirms that broader mode coverage brings statistically significant accuracy gains for synthetic data augmentation in the low-shot regime. Fixing the pipeline and varying only the batch objective, both RBF-based aggregators outperform InfoNCE, and the log-determinant attains the best downstream accuracy on both benchmarks. Its diversity advantage over InfoNCE widens as the batch grows. Code is available at https://github.com/mobled37/DetNO.

</details>


### [117] [Unsupervised Clustering of Public Transport Vehicles Using Deep Morphological Features and Color Histograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22270)
*Juan Irving Vasquez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督聚类方法，融合冻结的DINOv2形态特征与HSV颜色直方图，对墨西哥城公交车辆进行路线级聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市道路监控产生大量无标注视频，可用于公共交通监测，但不同线路公交车外形相似、主要靠配色区分，人工标注成本高，因此需要无监督的车辆分类方法。

Method: 采用加权早期融合，将冻结DINOv2 Vision Transformer提取的形态特征与手工设计的HSV颜色直方图结合，随后进行UMAP降维和K-Means聚类；融合权重为65%形态特征、35%颜色特征。

Result: 在墨西哥城一条主干道约1,460小时视频中提取的7,972张公交车图像上进行评估，生成14个簇，可区分不同路线和车型，且相比仅用形态特征，提升了目标簇（RTP公交网络）的精度。

Conclusion: 颜色信息可补充自监督特征以实现路线级聚类，但前提是颜色不能主导特征表示。

Abstract: Surveillance cameras along urban roads produce large volumes of unlabeled video that could support the monitoring of public transport, but grouping the observed vehicles into meaningful categories without manual annotation remains challenging, since buses from different routes often share the same body structure and differ mainly in their color schemes. This study presents an unsupervised clustering methodology for public transport vehicles that combines morphological features extracted with a frozen DINOv2 Vision Transformer and handcrafted HSV color histograms through weighted early fusion, followed by UMAP projection and K-Means clustering. The method was evaluated on 7,972 bus images extracted from about 1,460 hours of video of an arterial road in Mexico City, with the goal of isolating the buses of the city's public bus network (RTP) without supervision. With a fusion of 65\% morphological and 35\% color features, the pipeline produced 14 clusters that separate distinct routes and vehicle types, and it increased the precision of the target cluster with respect to morphological features alone. These results show that color complements self-supervised features for route-level clustering, provided that it does not dominate the representation.

</details>


### [118] [MJev-Spatial: System One Spatial Intelligence via Native Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22276)
*Luozheng Qin,Yuhan Zhu,Changhai Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Yuhua Zhou,Harry Zhang,Xiangyu Zeng,Yuxuan Liu,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MJev-Spatial 将空间理解任务统一为分类框架，用单次前向传播输出决策，无需生成答案 token，比自回归基线更快且精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在空间智能任务中需以交互速率做出判断，但自由文本生成存在自回归解码与答案解析的开销，而输出空间其实很紧凑（少数选项、标量或二维点），因此需要更高效的决策方式。

Method: 提出 MJev-Spatial，将三类空间理解任务统一为分类：把候选（选项、数值分箱、图像区域）视为类别，利用骨干网络预训练的 query 和 key 投影打分且不增加参数；数值与点预测采用由粗到细分类。训练上联合监督各阶段并用 teacher forcing 单次前向完成，结合长度感知全局批处理与跨 GPU 负载均衡。基于 Qwen3.5-4B，用 12.3M 空间问答对训练。

Result: 在八个测试模型中综合得分最高（65.64%），十二个基准中七个排名第一，全部十二个基准均优于未修改的自回归基座模型；推理速度比最快的自回归基线快 2.5 倍，比基座模型快 5.6 倍；训练吞吐较原批处理策略提升 30.8%。

Conclusion: 将空间理解建模为紧凑分类任务，可在不牺牲甚至提升精度的同时显著加速推理与训练，为交互式空间智能提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence lets an agent perceive and act in the physical world, and many of its judgments must be made at interaction rates: which spatial relation holds, how far an object is, or where to place it. Vision-language models (VLMs) produce these answers by free-form text generation, paying for autoregressive decoding and answer parsing even though the output space is compact: one of a few options, a scalar, or a 2D point. In light of this, we introduce MJev-Spatial, a System 1 multimodal decision model that tackles all three types of spatial understanding tasks within a unified classification framework. Specifically, MJev-Spatial treats each candidate as a class and scores it using the backbone's pretrained query and key projections, without adding parameters. The candidates are the given options for choice tasks, or value bins and image regions refined through coarse-to-fine classification for numerical and point prediction, respectively. At inference, each classification decision takes a single forward pass and generates no answer tokens. To train on heterogeneous spatial data efficiently, we supervise all coarse-to-fine stages jointly with teacher forcing in one forward pass, and combine this with length-aware global batching and cross-GPU load balancing, raising training throughput by 30.8% over the original batching strategy. Built on Qwen3.5-4B and trained on 12.3M question-answer pairs converted from existing spatial datasets, MJev-Spatial achieves the highest overall score (65.64%) among eight tested models, ranks first on seven of twelve benchmarks, and outperforms its unmodified autoregressive base model on all twelve, while running 2.5 times faster than the fastest autoregressive baseline and 5.6 times faster than the base model.

</details>


### [119] [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282)
*Qiuhong Shen,Yiding Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Xinwei Huang,Minxuan Lin,Bo Liu,Hao Kang,Haotian Yang,Yizhi Wang,Chongyang Ma,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPO将注意力路由作为随机探索动作，在保持原采样器的前提下实现强化学习对齐，训练效率提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数函数的方法通过扰动反向去噪轨迹引入随机性，导致高昂的基于rollout的优化成本，因此需要一种在生成器内部引入随机探索、同时保留原始采样器的方法。

Method: 在若干选定的去噪层级上，每个查询块保留自身块并按生成器原生注意力权重采样更多键块（或保持稠密注意力）；采样路由扰动该转移的速度，用稠密速度附近的高斯似然以分数函数目标打分。采用受控双rollout估计器：两次rollout共享提示、初始隐变量、路由去噪层级与稠密锚定层，仅采样路由不同，其奖励差为路由转移提供优势信号。

Result: 在文本到图像和文本到视频生成任务上，RPO提升了生成质量，且训练效率比Flow-GRPO类方法高近4倍。

Conclusion: 由于似然对稠密速度本身求导，去除路由后学习到的更新依然有效；推理时恢复稠密注意力并保留原始确定性采样器。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator's native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/rpo for visual results.

</details>


### [120] [Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22295)
*Jay N. Paranjape,Jurijs Nazarovs,Rohan Sarkar,Yue Guo,Lipeng Ke,Sankar Venkataraman,Vishal M. Patel,Nataraj Jammalamadaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GHADAR新任务，构建数据集、方法和评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多人场景中理解个体特征与活动很重要，但现有数据集把问题简化成群体活动识别、单人活动识别或闭集活动识别，限制了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 定义GHADAR任务：在给定视频中每个人位置的前提下，描述每个人的特征和活动。基于AVA-Actions构建含开放集描述的图像/视频描述数据集；训练时通过约束VLM中的交叉注意力掩码来利用定位信息；并引入基于VLM的整体评测方案，直接比较视频与生成预测中的概念。

Result: 实验表明该方法在该任务上优于当前最先进的视觉语言模型；同时揭示现有评测指标过度依赖人工标注和精确文本匹配的局限。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了包含数据集、新方法和评测方案的完整GHADAR框架，为该领域后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Understanding characteristics and activities of individuals in complex multi-person environments is crucial for real-world applications. However, existing datasets often simplify this problem to grounded group activity recognition, single-person activity recognition, or close-set activity recognition, thus limiting the generalizability of models trained on these datasets. In this work, we introduce the task of Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR), which involves describing the characteristics and activity descriptions of \textbf{every} person in a video, provided the location of the person in the video, which is more practical for real-world applications. To facilitate this, we introduce a new dataset derived from AVA-Actions by generating open-set captions for the person description and activity. In addition, we propose a novel method to effectively utilize the information contained in grounding during training by constraining the cross-attention masks during training in VLMs to improve performance for this task. Our experiments show that our method outperforms SOTA VLMs on this task. Finally, we demonstrate the limitations of existing evaluation metrics, which are overly reliant on human-annotations and exact text-text matching. As an added video-based evaluation, we propose a holistic VLM-based evaluation schema that compares concepts {\em directly} between the video and the generated predictions. Thus, in this work, we develop a complete framework for GHADAR, including a dataset, a novel method and an evaluation schema, thereby establishing a strong foundation for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [121] [ARC-SORT: Adaptive, Robust, Compensated Multi-Object Tracking with Interacting Multiple Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22306)
*Girish Kumar Adari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARC-SORT是一种无需训练、实时的跟踪-检测式多目标跟踪器，用IMM卡尔曼滤波的预测协方差驱动不确定性自适应的搜索区域与关联决策，辅以交叉后至多一次的身份纠正和纯外观的离线重识别，在SportsMOT和DanceTrack上取得领先HOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育转播中的多目标跟踪面临两大困难：运动员快速移动并急变向，摄像机同时平移和缩放。作者希望设计一个无需训练、可实时运行且鲁棒的跟踪器，核心思路是让跟踪器测量自身不确定性，并由该测量决定其行为方式。

Method: 提出ARC-SORT（自适应、鲁棒、补偿的SORT）。用IMM卡尔曼滤波为每条轨迹给出预测协方差，并将其转化为逐轨迹搜索区域：运动预测越不可靠，允许匹配的检测区域越大（不确定性自适应Expansion IoU）。作者强调这种连续耦合是关键：把同一协方差用作硬马氏门限在所有阈值下都更差，更丰富的协方差概要也无增益。当两条轨迹交叉时，用分配代价本身判断决策是否接近；对这类配对保持审查，并在交叉后外观证据显示首次决策错误时最多执行一次身份交换纠正。对离帧数秒的球员，运动外推无信息，离线重识别放弃运动门限，仅按外观连接轨迹片段。

Result: 在SportsMOT测试集上达到81.82 HOTA，在截至2026年8月的69个榜单条目中身份切换最少，MOTA并列第三（97.09）；在DanceTrack测试集上用相同关联设置达到62.1 HOTA。身份交换纠正使两个测试集均+1.0 HOTA，开销低于每帧1毫秒。在Deep-EIoU的公开检测与嵌入上达到80.31 HOTA，比Deep-EIoU高3.1。在笔记本RTX 3060上约50 FPS运行。

Conclusion: 不确定性自适应是ARC-SORT有效性的核心机制；结合谨慎的交叉后纠正与纯外观离线重识别，可在无需训练、实时的条件下取得当前领先的跟踪精度与最少的身份切换。代码将公开。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking in sports broadcasts is hard for two reasons: athletes move fast and change direction abruptly, and the camera pans and zooms at the same time. We present ARC-SORT (Adaptive, Robust, Compensated SORT), a tracking-by-detection tracker that trains nothing and runs in real time. Its design rule: the tracker measures its own uncertainty, and that measurement decides how it acts. An Interacting Multiple Model (IMM) Kalman filter gives every track a predictive covariance, and we turn it into a per-track search region: the less reliable the motion prediction, the larger the region in which a detection may match (uncertainty-adaptive Expansion IoU, EIoU). On identical inputs, this continuous coupling is what matters: the same covariance used as a hard Mahalanobis gate hurts at every threshold we swept, and richer covariance summaries add nothing measurable. When two tracks cross, the assignment cost itself reports whether the decision was close. ARC-SORT keeps such pairs under review and applies at most one corrective identity swap once post-crossing appearance evidence shows the first decision was wrong; this adds +1.0 HOTA (higher order tracking accuracy) on both the SportsMOT and DanceTrack test sets at under a millisecond per frame. For players who leave the frame for seconds, motion extrapolation carries no information, so our offline re-identification drops the usual motion gate and links tracklets by appearance alone. ARC-SORT reaches 81.82 HOTA on the SportsMOT test set with the fewest identity switches of the 69 leaderboard entries as of August 2026 and tied-third multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA, 97.09), and 62.1 HOTA on DanceTrack test with the same association settings. On Deep-EIoU's public detections and embeddings it reaches 80.31 HOTA, +3.1 over Deep-EIoU. It runs at about 50 frames per second on a laptop RTX 3060 GPU. Code will be released.

</details>


### [122] [Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311)
*Qiuhong Shen,Shizun Wang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Velox 是一种免训练的区域级注意力路由器，通过流式几何的视锥板（frustum slab）对应关系进行上下文选择，缓解分块视频扩散模型中 KV 缓存不断增长导致的延迟问题，在保持密集基线质量的同时大幅加速注意力与去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需要将动作与相机运动转化为长时间的可交互视觉 rollout，既要求响应迅速，又需保留已生成内容的视觉记忆。分块视频扩散模型通常把上下文保存在不断增长的 KV 缓存中，导致后续块越来越慢，成为交互式生成的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Velox，一种免训练的注意力路由器，用于区域级上下文选择。它将历史块表示为带深度区间的相机视锥板，并通过"近因优先多帧扭曲（recency-priority multiwarp）"获取当前块深度。对覆盖良好的区域，视锥板重叠会软性地调制各层由模型导出的块重要性；未覆盖区域则保留模型导出的路由，不带几何偏置。固定的 token-block 粒度与 FlashAttention 风格的平铺执行相匹配，从而缩减随 rollout 长度增长的注意力项。视锥板构建在同一个 H200 上与去噪异步运行。

Result: 在第 48 个块时，Velox 将注意力加速 4.36 倍，去噪加速 2.43 倍。在完整的 158 个案例的 WBench 导航划分上，其表现与密集的 LingBot-Fast-Cam 相差在 0.15 API-free 平均分以内。

Conclusion: Velox 通过几何引导的免训练注意力路由与异步视锥板构建，在显著降低长 rollout 延迟的同时维持了接近密集基线的生成质量，为交互式视频世界模型提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer's model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/velox/ for more details.

</details>


### [123] [Not Just a Subject: Capturing Adaptive Scene-Specific Variance for Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22320)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,Kwanyoung Lee,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 EOS-Story，通过文本空间跨上下文桥接与视觉空间自适应身份保持，实现视觉叙事中身份一致与场景适配的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉叙事方法侧重身份保持，难以适应多样场景变化，导致语义错位与结构僵化。

Method: 文本空间将叙事解耦为身份与场景子提示，提出 Cross-Contextual Bridging 将短语级外观残差软注入场景的 <EOS> 嵌入；视觉空间提出 Adaptive Identity Preservation，含 Identity Anchoring 与 Identity-Aware Self-Attention，缓解尺度不匹配导致的结构伪影。

Result: 扩展 Consistory+ 基准为 Consistory+_+，覆盖多样取景场景；实验显示在两个基准上取得有竞争力或更优的表现。

Conclusion: EOS-Story 无需训练即可协调文本与视觉空间的身份一致性与场景自适应，提升视觉叙事生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advancements in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-image generation, enabling their application to visual storytelling, where coherent image sequences are generated from narrative prompts. However, existing methods primarily focus on identity preservation and often fail to adapt to diverse scene variations, resulting in semantic misalignment and structural inflexibility. We propose EOS-Story, a training-free framework that harmonizes identity consistency with scene-specific adaptation across text and vision spaces. In the text space, we decouple the narrative into identity and scene sub-prompts to prevent information dilution, but this leaves the scene without access to appearance cues. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cross-Contextual Bridging, which softly injects a phrase-level appearance residual into the scene's <EOS> embedding. In the vision space, we find that scale mismatch between identity and scene subjects causes a few identity tokens to be repeatedly transferred, producing structural artifacts. We address this with Adaptive Identity Preservation, which anchors identity features at the scene-specific scale (Identity Anchoring) and integrates them via Identity-Aware Self-Attention. To evaluate performance under varied spatial compositions, we extend the Consistory+ benchmark with diverse framing scenarios (Consistory+_+). Extensive experiments show that EOS-Story achieves competitive or superior performance on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [124] [AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321)
*Yuruo Wu,Kaihang Pan,Kai Shen,Jiacheng Li,Shuyuan Tu,Jisheng Dang,Li Tuoyu,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以事件为中心的音视频一致性评估框架，并实例化为可训练奖励模型 AVC-Reward，配合 AVC-Benchmark 实现与人类判断对齐的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多沿用早期简单、单向任务的指标，将整段音视频整体评估并依赖预定义的简单关系，无法应对多事件组合、多层音频混合与不对称跨模态关系等复杂场景。

Method: 在事件层面解耦音视频事件，独立分析每个事件，跨模态交叉验证关系，并聚合为粗到细的三级一致性指标：全局叙事和谐、事件级时间同步、细粒度属性对齐；据此实例化可训练奖励模型 AVC-Reward，并构建 AVC-Benchmark。

Result: AVC-Reward 在自动方法中与人类判断的对齐达到最优，能提供定位失败的可解释依据，并作为在线强化学习信号提升联合生成模型的音视频一致性。

Conclusion: 该事件中心框架为多样生成设置与跨模态关系下的音视频一致性评估提供了通用表述，兼具评估、诊断与优化价值。

Abstract: As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: https://tsuki0512.github.io/AVC-Reward.github.io/

</details>


### [125] [Dense4D: Pixel-Level 4D Human Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22325)
*Rıza Alp Güler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dense4D：首个无需人体模板的前馈模型，从单目RGB图像集联合预测Human Point Maps与深度，实现跨越身体、衣物和头发的稠密4D对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建4D人体运动需在关节活动、形变及可见性变化中识别同一表面点，而现有方法依赖人体模板且难以处理稠密对应。

Method: Dense4D联合处理以人为中心的裁剪图像及已知图像到裁剪变换，预测每帧的Human Point Maps与深度。HPM为每个人体像素分配其在统一参考时刻的表面点3D位置，支持非模态对应；通过跨裁剪匹配HPM坐标建立对应，无需预测相机位姿或大场景位移，再由逐帧深度将匹配提升为3D轨迹。

Result: 在真人数据上，跟踪精度显著高于对比基线，且与扫描表面的吻合度更高；不同帧的点可组装为参考姿态下连贯的人体几何，直观体现关节活动与衣物形变中的一致稠密对应。

Conclusion: Dense4D验证了无需模板的前馈稠密4D人体对应可行性，为4D人体运动重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing human motion in 4D requires identifying the same surface points through articulation, deformation and changes in visibility. We introduce Dense4D, to our knowledge the first feed-forward model dedicated to dense 4D correspondence across the body, clothing and hair from a set of monocular RGB images, without a body template. Trained specifically for human geometry, Dense4D jointly processes person-centred crops with known image-to-crop transforms to predict Human Point Maps (HPMs) and depth for each image. An HPM assigns each person pixel the 3D position of its observed surface point at a common reference time. These maps support amodal correspondence: points hidden in the reference image can be localised from observations in other frames. Matching HPM coordinates across crops establishes correspondence without predicting camera poses or large scene displacements; per-image depth then lifts the matches into 3D tracks. Experiments on real people demonstrate substantially higher tracking accuracy and closer agreement with scanned surfaces than the evaluated baselines. Points from different frames assemble into coherent human geometry in the reference pose, providing visible evidence of consistent dense correspondence through articulation and clothing deformation. Additional qualitative results, videos, and an interactive viewer are available at https://alpguler.com/dense4d.

</details>


### [126] [ACEdit: Stable Multi-Turn Image Editing via Asymmetric Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22328)
*Sara Dorfman,Nir Goren,Gal Barak,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练方案，使多轮图像编辑在累积分布偏移下保持稳定，核心是将Self-Forcing思想引入图像编辑，并利用教师编辑器的蒸馏目标与不对称条件机制来解决自生成轨迹监督不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑模型在单次编辑上表现出色，但在多轮连续编辑中质量往往下降。每一轮编辑都会引入细微的分布偏移，这些偏移在多轮推理中不断累积，导致输入分布与编辑器原始训练分布之间出现差距。因此需要一种能让多轮图像编辑在累积分布偏移下保持稳定的训练方法。

Method: 作者将Self-Forcing原则引入图像编辑，在训练时强制编辑器基于自身前几轮的输出进行操作。由于自生成编辑轨迹会偏离真实轨迹，直接监督不可靠，因此采用蒸馏目标，借助教师图像编辑器在模型处理自身先前输出时提供可靠的训练信号。同时提出不对称条件方案：学生模型基于自身输出顺序执行编辑，而教师模型始终锚定于原始干净图像，并接收复合编辑指令。

Result: 实验表明，在长多轮编辑序列中，该方法显著减少了质量退化，同时保持了编辑质量和指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 通过在训练中引入Self-Forcing并结合蒸馏目标与不对称条件机制，可以有效缓解多轮图像编辑中的累积分布偏移问题，实现稳定的长序列多轮图像编辑。

Abstract: Image editing models produce impressive results for individual edits, but their quality often deteriorates over multiple editing turns. Each editing turn introduces small distribution shifts that accumulate over successive edits, creating a gap between the input distributions encountered during the editor's original training and multi-turn inference. We introduce a training scheme that makes multi-turn image editing stable under this accumulating distribution shift. A key is to force the editor to operate on its own outputs from previous editing turns during training, bringing the principle of Self-Forcing to image editing. The main challenge is that self-generated editing trajectories deviate from ground-truth trajectories, making direct supervision unreliable. We address this challenge through a distillation objective that leverages a teacher image editor to provide a reliable training signal when the model operates on its own previous outputs. We introduce an asymmetric conditioning scheme: while the student performs edits sequentially on its own outputs, the teacher remains anchored to the original clean image and receives a compound editing instruction. Experiments show substantially reduced degradation over long multi-turn sequences while maintaining editing quality and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [127] [3DCodeVerse: Exploring 3D Worlds as Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22341)
*Yipeng Gao,Ziyao Zeng,Jinfa Huang,Yijiang Li,Haolin Xiong,Wang Qin,Xinlei Yu,Youheng Yao,Jie Zhang,Kun-Yu Lin,Rong Liu,Yinyuan Zhao,Yajie Zhao,Meiqi Guo,Jiebo Luo,Lei Shu,Yunhao Ge,Xinyu Hu,Laurent Itti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 3DCodeVerse，一个面向编码智能体的大规模 3D 代码研究生态，包含 200 万级可执行 3D 代码数据集、多智能体自动验证框架及系统性实证研究，微调开源模型显著提升 3D 编码能力，其框架在人类成对评测中相对 Claude Design（3D Object）取得 90% 胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 内容生成难以做到可编辑、可验证且规模化，缺乏高质量可执行 3D 代码数据与自动化空间推理验证手段，因此需要构建一套完整的研究生态来支撑编码智能体的 3D 内容创作。

Method: 工作分三部分：(1) 构建 3DCodeVerse-2M 数据集，通过网络开源数据采集、专用表示转换和智能体生成三种途径获得独立可执行 3D 代码，每个程序打包源码、依赖资产、描述与验证记录；(2) 开发 3DCodeVerse Harness，采用多智能体有向无环图框架，通过执行检查、空间测量和视觉反馈自动评估并修正生成程序，生成过程中无需人工反馈；(3) 开展系统性实证研究，基于微调开源模型建立基线，并通过组件级分析提炼实践指导。

Result: 在该数据集上微调可显著提升开源模型的 3D 编码能力；3DCodeVerse Harness 在由 Claude Opus 5.5 驱动的成对人类评测中，对 Claude Design（3D Object）取得 90% 胜率。

Conclusion: 3DCodeVerse 通过数据、工具与实证研究三位一体的生态，验证了以代码为中心、可自动验证的 3D 内容生成范式的有效性，为后续 3D 编码智能体研究提供了数据基础、框架支持与实践经验。

Abstract: We introduce 3DCodeVerse, a research ecosystem that enables coding agents to create editable and verifiable 3D content at scale. 3DCodeVerse comprises large-scale, high-quality datasets of executable 3D code, a coding harness for spatial reasoning and automated verification, and practical guidance for 3D coding. First, we construct 3DCodeVerse-2M, a large-scale curated dataset of standalone 3D executable code. We build this corpus by collecting open-source data, converting specialized representations, and generating new programs with coding agents. Each program is packaged with the source files and associated assets required for execution in its declared environment, along with available descriptions and verification records. Second, we develop the 3DCodeVerse Harness, a multi-agent directed acyclic graph framework for generating and refining 3D content. It uses execution checks, spatial measurements, and visual feedback to automatically evaluate and revise generated programs without human feedback during generation. Third, we conduct systematic empirical studies of 3D coding, establishing baselines with fine-tuned open models and deriving practical guidance through component-level analyses of our harness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning on our dataset significantly improves the 3D coding capabilities of open-source models. Notably, our 3DCodeVerse harness achieves a 90% win rate against Claude Design (3D Object) in pairwise human evaluations, when both are utilized by Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [128] [GeoState: Learning View-Decoupled Geometry States for 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343)
*Hongchang Liu,Jian Liu,Yujuan Ding,Liang Peng,Xun Jiang,Fei Ma,Xing Xu,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoState 提出一种视角解耦的几何表示，用于预测式 3D 世界建模，将场景状态演化与相机相关观测分离，从而提升预测精度与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 世界模型常使用几何基础模型（GFM）特征作为预测性世界状态，可受益于其丰富的几何先验。但这些特征具有视角依赖性，会把场景动态与相机引起的观测变化纠缠在一起，使状态演化更难预测。

Method: 方法核心包含：1）场景分词器将多视角 GFM 特征聚合为解耦视角的场景状态；2）几何解码器通过相机射线查询该状态；3）自回归世界模型预测未来场景状态，相机运动则单独建模。

Result: 实验表明 GeoState 在 Waymo 上优于先前的 3D 世界模型，支持几何一致的跨视角查询，并可零样本泛化到 KITTI 和 nuScenes。

Conclusion: GeoState 通过视角解耦的场景状态表示，实现了可预测、可跨相机解码且保持上下文一致尺度的 3D 世界建模，验证了表示解耦对预测式世界模型的有效性。

Abstract: Recent 3D world models increasingly use features from geometry foundation models (GFMs) as predictive world states, benefiting from their rich geometric priors. However, the view dependence of these features entangles scene dynamics with camera-induced variation, making the resulting state evolution harder to predict. We propose GeoState, a view-decoupled geometry representation for predictive 3D world modeling that separates scene-state evolution from camera-dependent observation. Specifically, a scene tokenizer aggregates multi-view GFM features into a view-decoupled scene state, which a geometry decoder queries using camera rays. An autoregressive world model then predicts future scene states, while camera motion is modeled separately. The predicted states can therefore be decoded under either predicted or externally specified cameras, while retaining a single context-derived scale throughout the rollout without per-frame scale alignment. Experiments show that GeoState outperforms prior 3D world models on Waymo, supports geometrically consistent cross-view querying, and generalizes zero-shot to KITTI and nuScenes. Project page: https://hongchang-liu.github.io/GeoState-project-page/

</details>


### [129] [Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347)
*Hyunwook Choi,Seungryong Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Speculative Forcing 框架，将少步自回归视频生成中的中间清洁估计视为草稿，预测每个 token 何时可被提前接受并冻结，从而跳过后续去噪计算，在保持全步采样质量的同时显著降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频生成器虽然支持流式生成，但对每个 token 都执行完整的最终去噪步骤，忽视了个体差异。作者发现这种统一计算往往是不必要的：许多 token 在初始步骤就已接近其最终全步输出，而另一些则需要持续精炼到最后。

Method: 提出 Speculative Forcing 框架，把中间清洁估计当作草稿，预测单个 token 能否被安全地提前接受。一旦接受，该 token 被冻结为最终输出、不再去噪，但其缓存的 keys 和 values 仍作为上下文供剩余 token 使用。设计了两种验证器：一是无需训练的版本，基于与清洁内容的相似度及跨步骤稳定性；二是学习型版本，读取冻结生成器的中间特征来估计每个 token 到全步输出的剩余差距。

Result: 在短视频、长视频和世界生成任务中，Speculative Forcing 在保持全步采样质量的同时大幅减少计算。在 LongLive 上，以约 1.7 个去噪步/token 匹配四步生成器，速度提升超过 1.5 倍，达到 41.7 和 37.8 fps。在 Lingbot-World 2.0 上，面对相机运动持续改变可见内容的场景，以约 2.5 个去噪步/token 保持质量，速度提升 1.35 倍，达到 24.5 fps。

Conclusion: 逐 token 自适应地提前接受并冻结输出，可以在不损失生成质量的前提下有效降低少步自回归视频生成的计算成本，且在短视频、长视频及动态世界生成等多种场景中均适用。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generators enable streaming generation, yet they run every token through the final denoising step, regardless of how much refinement it needs. We find this uniform computation is often unnecessary: many tokens already closely match their eventual full-step outputs in the initial steps, while others require refinement until the end. We introduce Speculative Forcing, a framework that treats intermediate clean estimates as drafts and predicts when individual tokens can be safely accepted early. Once accepted, a token is frozen as its final output and undergoes no further denoising, while its cached keys and values remain available as context for the remaining tokens. We explore two alternative verifier designs: a training-free one based on similarity to clean content and stability across steps, and a learned one that reads the frozen generator's intermediate features to estimate each token's remaining gap to its full-step output. Across short-video, long-video, and world generation, Speculative Forcing preserves the quality of full-step sampling while substantially reducing computation. On LongLive, it matches the four-step generator with ~1.7 denoising steps per token, running over 1.5x faster at 41.7 and 37.8 fps. On Lingbot-World 2.0, where camera motion continually changes the visible content, it maintains quality with ~2.5 denoising steps per token and runs 1.35x faster at 24.5 fps.

</details>


### [130] [SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352)
*Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Fanpeng Meng,Zihao Huang,Jichen Pang,Guanjun Wu,Zhikuan Bao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUPRA-Tex 提出将可见材质估计、表面对齐与观测引导的3D补全解耦，实现单图3D材质的超高保真生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图3D生成方法在融合多视角预测时易产生伪影，或难以通过隐式图像特征条件保留细节，因此需要新的框架来兼顾可见区域的忠实还原与不可见区域的合理补全。

Method: 基于MLLM的图像编辑模型预测参考视角下的光照分离PBR贴图；将其投影到可见表面并用预训练材质VAE编码为与生成目标同空间的观测latent；采用交错流Transformer将观测latent作为固定只读锚点，交替进行不可见区域与全表面更新，生成完整材质latent。

Result: 在仅使用公开数据集训练的条件下，SUPRA-Tex在几何条件纹理化与端到端资产生成任务上均取得SOTA性能，在真实图像上匹配甚至超越领先的商业系统。

Conclusion: 通过任务解耦与观测锚点机制，SUPRA-Tex实现了对参考图的超对齐，可见区域达到"所见即所得"的保真度，同时保持不可见表面的连贯补全。

Abstract: Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image. Existing methods either introduce artifacts when fusing inconsistent multi-view predictions or struggle to preserve fine details through implicit image-feature conditioning. To resolve these challenges, we introduce SUPRA-Tex, built on the principle of decoupling visible-material estimation, surface alignment, and observation-guided 3D completion. This division of labor allows the 3D generator, trained on limited 3D data, to focus its capacity on preserving visible evidence and completing unobserved surfaces. Specifically, we first train an MLLM-based image editing model to predict lighting-separated PBR maps in the reference view. We then project these maps onto the visible surface and encode them with the pretrained material VAE encoder into native observation latents that share the generation target's latent space. An interleaved flow transformer holds these latents as fixed, read-only anchors while alternating unobserved-region and full-surface updates to generate complete material latents. This design achieves super-alignment with the reference, delivering What You See Is What You Get fidelity on visible regions and coherent completion on unobserved surfaces. Trained solely on public datasets, SUPRA-Tex achieves SOTA performance in both geometry-conditioned texturing and end-to-end asset generation, matching or even exceeding leading commercial systems on in-the-wild images. Project page: https://supra-3d.github.io/supratex/

</details>


### [131] [DK-VLM: Geometry as Position for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22356)
*Han Su,Meng Tian,Tianyu Huang,Mingyu Gao,Lennox Wei,Dongxu Li,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Xiaohe Wu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DK-VLM，用双核注意力将几何信息以位置形式注入注意力，并引入参考系组合监督，提升多视角3D空间理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多视角3D空间关系理解以及随观察者移动更新空间判断方面表现不佳；常见做法是把3D重建特征融合进视觉token，但在空间监督有限时，这类特征难以与预训练语义表示对齐。

Method: 设计双核注意力（Dual-Kernel Attention）：语义核保留预训练模型的原始注意力与位置编码；几何核利用估计的3D点和相机位姿，通过点位移与相对视角变换直接调制注意力；再用学习到的门控融合两者输出，使几何以"位置"而非"融合重建特征"的形式进入注意力。同时提出参考系组合（Reference-Frame Composition, RC）监督，基于已验证的单步标注生成逐步的转向与朝向目标，训练模型在连续判断中更新参考系。

Result: 在六个空间基准上，DK-VLM-8B取得最高平均分，并在VSI-Bench（74.33）和ReVSI-32（58.28）上取得最高总分；RC监督进一步提升了多步空间推理能力，且完整模型仍保持通用视频理解能力。

Conclusion: 将几何信息作为位置直接调制注意力，并配合参考系组合监督，是提升视觉语言模型多视角3D空间推理的有效途径，且不损害通用视频理解性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models still struggle to understand 3D spatial relations across views and update spatial judgments as the observer moves. A common approach fuses 3D reconstruction features into visual tokens, but aligning these features with pretrained semantic representations remains challenging under limited spatial supervision. We propose DK-VLM with Dual-Kernel Attention, which computes attention from both a visual token's input position and its geometric position in the scene. A semantic kernel retains the pretrained model's native attention and positional encoding, while a separate geometric kernel uses estimated 3D points and camera poses to directly modulate attention through point displacements and relative view transformations. Then, a learned gate fuses their outputs. Geometry thus enters attention as position rather than as fused reconstruction features. We further introduce Reference-Frame Composition (RC) supervision to train the model to update its reference frame across successive judgments. It provides stepwise turn and heading targets derived from verified single-step annotations. Among the compared methods, DK-VLM-8B achieves the highest average score across six spatial benchmarks and the highest overall scores on VSI-Bench (74.33) and ReVSI-32 (58.28). RC supervision further improves multi-step spatial reasoning, while the full model preserves general video understanding. Project page: https://sumuru789.github.io/DK-VLM-website/.

</details>


### [132] [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360)
*Shuanglin Li,Jiaying Lin,Shuquan Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向3D内容的可靠验证反馈框架，包含基准测试3DVerifierBench、细粒度错误分析与训练数据流水线，并展示验证在3D生成候选选择与递归精修中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型与3D生成虽提升视觉逼真度，但难以保证任务相关的正确性。作者认为可靠3D系统不仅需要更强的生成与推理，还需能识别未满足需求并解释违规的验证反馈，以支持系统自我反思与改进；同时验证需整合多视角观测，区分真实不一致与投影依赖的表观差异。

Method: 1）构建3DVerifierBench基准，覆盖10项任务，评估决策、拒绝解释以及对规格和候选受控变化的响应，并进行人类研究；2）按细粒度事实类别分析验证，揭示数值与目标绑定错误，并建立可扩展流水线，结合多视角事实抽取、受控3D编辑与原生标注，生成14,126个带矛盾解释和关联反事实的训练样本；3）研究验证如何通过候选选择与递归精修支持3D生成。

Result: 人类研究与最强受测模型GPT-6 Astra存在21.6个百分点差距，解释感知分析暴露拒绝可靠性弱点。在人工标注的16候选池中，oracle选择将完整事实成功率从26.7%提升到90.0%。在另一项8输入研究中，人类引导的递归精修使每个输入均满足全部7项要求，而并行Oracle@8仅3项。

Conclusion: 该基准、分析与数据流水线为模型通过可靠验证反馈反思3D内容奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

</details>


### [133] [Perceive, Predict, and Plan: a Unified World Action Model for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363)
*Zirui Wu,Chen Guo,Tianjian Jiang,Zeren Jiang,Zeming Li,Lingchen Yang,Longteng Duan,Ying Xue,Handi Yin,Shenyuan Gao,Martin R. Oswald,Jun Zhang,Ping Tan,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P³-WAM，一个统一的“世界动作模型”，通过感知、预测、规划三种互补模式联合学习3D perception、动作条件预测与目标导向规划，实验表明统一训练优于单模式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将感知、预测与规划分离处理，缺乏统一框架来共享视觉与动作知识，因此需要构建一个能同时学习3D感知、动作条件预测和目标导向规划的统一模型。

Method: 采用集成紧凑结构化3D动作表示的预训练视频扩散transformer，通过mixture-of-transformers架构（共享基础权重、模态特定适配、双向视觉-动作注意力）实现三种模式；并引入随机模态可见性，使视频和3D动作在三种模式中可灵活作为条件上下文或预测目标。

Result: 统一训练在三种能力上均优于单模式训练，规划质量在视觉-语言模型评估下提升；在四个数据集上，3D手部重建和动作条件视频预测超过专用基线，生成的目标导向操作视频在指令遵循和动作进展上优于视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 统一训练能够有效整合感知、预测与规划三种能力，使P³-WAM在多项任务上超越专用基线，验证了统一世界动作模型的可行性与优势。

Abstract: We introduce P$^{3}$-WAM, a unified world action model for egocentric dexterous manipulation that learns 3D perception, action-conditioned prediction, and goal-directed planning through three complementary modes: Perceive, which reconstructs articulated hand motion and camera trajectories from video; Predict, which generates visual futures under prescribed actions; and Plan, which jointly generates action trajectories and their anticipated visual consequences conditioned on an initial image, a language instruction, and an optional visual goal. Our model integrates a compact, structured 3D action representation into a pretrained video diffusion transformer through a mixture-of-transformers architecture with shared base weights, modality-specific adaptation, and bidirectional visual-action attention. Randomized modality visibility lets video and 3D actions serve as either conditioning context or prediction targets across the three modes. Experiments show that unified training improves all three capabilities over single-mode training, including gains in planning quality under a vision-language model evaluation. Through unified training, our model outperforms specialized baselines on 3D hand reconstruction and action-conditioned video prediction across four datasets, while producing goal-directed manipulation videos with stronger instruction adherence and action progression than video-generation baselines. Project Page: https://p3-wam.pages.dev/

</details>


### [134] [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369)
*Shimon Malnick,Nadav Orenstein,Ohad Fried,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SoftCache，将动态缓存中的二值刷新信号改造为"软缓存"，用阈值余量自适应决定缓存步的步长，实现无需训练、跨骨干网络的加速，在图像与视频生成上以 3.5×–6.9× 加速领先全部基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型生成成本主要由每步完整网络前向决定。现有无训练动态缓存方法只把输入探针累积量当作二值开关（刷新或复用），并始终按固定步长推进，忽略了该信号中蕴含的更多信息。

Method: 观察到探针信号距阈值的大小（余量）可预测复用带来的误差。SoftCache 据此进行软缓存：余量大时让缓存步跳过多 个调度节点，余量随刷新临近而缩小、步长回归原始步长；自适应跳步可能落在调度节点之间，使采样器偏离固定网格。

Result: 无需训练、无需逐骨干网络调参。在图像模型 FLUX 以及视频模型 HunyuanVideo、Wan2.1 上，取得 3.5×–6.9× 加速，并在全部四项保真度指标上超过所有基线。

Conclusion: 缓存刷新信号不应只当作二值开关使用，其中余量信息可指导自适应步长，从而在保持生成质量的同时实现更大幅度的加速。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

</details>


### [135] [Which Tokens to Merge? Diffusion Dynamics for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,HyunGee Kim,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的动态感知Token合并框架DaTMe，基于扩散去噪动力学构建重要性图以决定token保留或合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token合并方法依赖文本相关性保留前景区域，忽视了背景物体与提示未提及的局部细节，导致结构一致性下降。

Method: 从Tweedie去噪估计构建动态感知重要性图，结合时序信号（相邻步预测干净图像的变化）与结构信号（边界、细节等局部高频内容），在给定压缩率下自适应分配token角色：重要token作为锚点保留，时序稳定且结构均匀的token作为安全合并候选。

Result: 在PixArt-α与FLUX上的实验表明，DaTMe在保持全图视觉保真度方面表现良好，验证扩散动力学作为token级合并决策的有效性。

Conclusion: 以扩散动力学为依据的token重要性度量可替代纯文本相关性准则，实现更全局一致的加速生成。

Abstract: Token merging has emerged as an effective training-free strategy for accelerating diffusion models by reducing redundant token computation while minimizing generation degradation. A key challenge is determining which tokens can be safely merged and which should be preserved for subsequent computation. Existing method addresses this by preserving prompt-aligned foreground regions, thereby protecting semantically relevant structures. However, this text-relevance criterion overlooks background objects, local details that are not explicitly mentioned in the prompt, leading to degraded structural coherence. In this work, we propose Dynamics-aware Token Merging (DaTMe), a training-free framework that grounds token merging in the underlying diffusion dynamics. Our key insight is that token importance should reflect whether a token remains unresolved during denoising and whether it carries structural information. To this end, DaTMe constructs a dynamics- aware importance map from the Tweedie denoised estimate. The map combines a temporal signal, measuring inter-step changes in the predicted clean image, with a structural signal, capturing local high-frequency content such as boundaries and fine details. Using this map, DaTMe adaptively assigns tokens into merging roles under a given compression ratio: important tokens are preserved as anchors, while tokens that are both temporally stabilized and structurally homogeneous are selected as safe merge candidates. Experiments on PixArt-a and FLUX demonstrate that DaTMe maintains visual fidelity across the entire image, highlighting diffusion dynamics as an effective token-level merging decision in efficient generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungJuCha/DaTMe.

</details>


### [136] [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371)
*Yitong Xia,Zeren Jiang,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gen4P是一个统一框架，能将预训练视频生成器改造为前馈式4D感知器，从单目视频中恢复4D几何与运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预训练视频生成模型在统一4D感知任务中的潜力，避免迭代去噪带来的低效。

Method: 基于按需查询（on-demand querying），单次编码器前向处理干净的视频潜变量，通过轻量交叉注意力解码器预测所需4D属性；提出连续时间查询实现时间灵活性；引入预训练视频深度LoRA作为冻结几何先验。

Result: 在3D点跟踪、视频深度和相机位姿估计任务上达到最优或 competitive 性能；野外视频上展现出跟踪复杂运动并保持精细结构的能力。

Conclusion: 证明视频生成预训练对统一4D感知具有潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Gen4P, a unified framework for recovering 4D geometry and motion from monocular video. We show that a pretrained video generator can be repurposed into a feed-forward 4D perceiver without iterative denoising. Building on on-demand querying, Gen4P processes clean video latents in a single encoder pass and predicts the requested 4D attributes through a lightweight cross-attention decoder. The on-demand query formulation enables flexible spatial sampling, and our proposed continuous-time queries further provide temporal flexibility. To strengthen geometric knowledge, we propose a straightforward plug-in solution by incorporating a pretrained video-depth LoRA as a frozen geometry prior, improving overall performance without adding extra trainable parameters. Gen4P achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on tasks of 3D point tracking, video depth and camera pose estimation. Qualitative results on in-the-wild videos illustrate its capability of tracking complex motion while maintaining the delicate structure. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of video generative pretraining for unified 4D perception. See https://gen4p.github.io for more visual results.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [DeepUnfolding_Agent: A Classical_Quantum Adaptive Control API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20615)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DeepUnfolding Agent，一个基于 FastAPI 的 schema 驱动平台，将深度展开自适应控制外部化为独立软件服务，实现控制器与执行环境的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化流程在机器学习、联邦学习、量子机器学习及混合经典-量子系统中，常将控制器逻辑直接嵌入任务特定的训练脚本中，导致复用性差、可复现性低、安全性验证困难，且难以在不同执行环境间进行公平比较。

Method: 采用 schema 驱动的 FastAPI 服务架构，外部执行器注册策略 schema、提交轮级诊断变量、接收受 schema 约束的控制动作、执行一轮优化并返回反馈。框架通过策略注册表、诊断-状态适配器、控制器工厂、安全守卫、执行器抽象、反馈更新路径、SQLite 持久化、Docker 部署及可复现基准脚本，实现控制器智能与领域执行的分离。支持经典、量子感知、混合、Qiskit Aer 及可选 IBM 量子处理等执行路径。

Result: 通过单元、schema、API、安全、编排、量子执行、检查点和 Docker 等测试完成验证，并在金融、医疗、基因组学、交通、噪声量子模拟及硬件支持的 IBM Quantum 执行中展示固定与自适应外部用户工作流。

Conclusion: DeepUnfolding-Agent 为异构优化工作流提供了一个可复用、可追溯的自适应控制层；量子实验被解读为 API 级量子控制与硬件可移植性的证据。

Abstract: Adaptive optimization workflows in machine learning, federated learning, quantum machine learning, and hybrid classical quantum systems often embed controller logic directly inside task specific training scripts. This coupling limits reuse, reproducibility, safety validation, and fair comparison across execution environments. This paper presents DeepUnfolding Agent, a schema-driven FastAPI platform that exposes deep unfolded adaptive control as an external software service. External executors register a strategy schema, submit round-level diagnostic variables, receive schema-bounded control actions, execute one optimization round, and return feedback for controller update. The framework separates controller intelligence from domain specific execution through a strategy registry, diagnostic to state adapter, controller factory, safety guard, executor abstraction, feedback update path, SQLite persistence layer, Docker deployment, and reproducible benchmark scripts. The implementation supports classical, quantum-aware, hybrid, Qiskit Aer, and optional IBM quantum processing execution pathways. We validate the software through unit, schema, API, safety, orchestration, Quantum execution, checkpointing, and Docker tests, and demonstrate external user fixed versus adaptive workflows across finance, healthcare, genomics, transport, noisy quantum simulation, and hardware backed IBM Quantum execution. The results show that DeepUnfolding-Agent provides a reusable and traceable adaptive control layer for heterogeneous optimization workflows. Our quantum experiments are interpreted as API level quantum control and hardware portability evidence.

</details>


### [138] [PRISM: PRIvileged Sibling-guided Multi-Agent Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20628)
*Phuong Tuan Dat,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRISM，一种面向 LLM 多智能体协作拓扑生成器的后训练方法，通过特权信息学习从同一执行验证语料中恢复逐查询偏好信号，在六个基准上超越所有拓扑设计基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统的效果关键取决于协作拓扑（哪些智能体参与、如何通信）。现有方法让生成器从任务查询生成拓扑，并通过执行验证过的图语料进行拟合，会经由持续 SFT 更新生成器。但语料只列出能解决某查询的图，从不给出排序，因此缺少逐查询偏好；没有该偏好，生成器会偏向语料中出现最多的拓扑，而非最适合各查询的拓扑，导致在一个任务上的收益可能以另一个任务的代价换取。

Method: PRISM 从同一语料中通过特权信息学习恢复逐查询信号。教师模型经由一个零初始化的低秩分支读取一个同样解决了该查询、但部署时不可用的兄弟图；学生模型在自身采样的轨迹上匹配教师的逐决策目标。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上，PRISM 优于所评估的所有拓扑设计基线；匹配教师的对照实验支持了特权查询信息的贡献。

Conclusion: PRISM 表明可从无需排序的执行验证语料中恢复逐查询偏好信号，缓解持续 SFT 的拓扑漂移问题，为 LLM 多智能体协作拓扑生成器的后训练提供了有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) based on large language models (LLMs) have become a powerful way to address complex problems, and their effectiveness depends critically on the collaboration topology that decides which agents participate and how they communicate. Recent work learns to generate this topology from the task query, fitting the generator to corpora of execution-verified graphs. The post-training method for such generators remains a limitation: a corpus lists the graphs that solved a query but never ranks them, leaving continued supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with no per-query preference to exploit. Without such a preference, the generator drifts toward the topologies the corpus contains most often rather than those that suit each query, so a gain on one task can come at a cost on another. We propose PRISM, which recovers per-query signal from the same corpus by learning with privileged information: a teacher reads, through a zero-initialized low-rank branch, a sibling graph that also solved the query and is unavailable at deployment, and the student matches its per-decision targets on trajectories the student samples itself. Across six diverse benchmarks PRISM improves over every topology-design baseline evaluated, and matched-teacher controls support the contribution of privileged query information. Code is available in this link.

</details>


### [139] [A Large-Scale Dataset for Multi-Class Financial News Classification in Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20630)
*Leomax Bandeira,Gabriel Aguiar,Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares,Luiz Pereira,Helder Fernando de Araújo Oliveira,Gustavo Xavier,Andson M. Balieiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了约16万篇巴西葡语金融新闻语料，并用余弦相似度、KNN和FAISS做弱监督多分类基准，发现KNN在人工标注子集上最优（47.3%准确率，31.9% F1），而LLM标注子集因标签不平衡与噪声导致F1骤降至约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西葡语金融NLP研究受限于领域数据集的稀缺，缺少可用的语料与基准，因此需要构建大规模金融新闻语料并建立初步评测基线。

Method: 从网络采集约16万篇巴西葡语金融新闻构建语料；对子集进行人工标注与LLM标注；基于多语言语义嵌入，采用余弦相似度、KNN和FAISS近邻检索三种相似度方法进行弱监督多分类。

Result: 人工标注子集上KNN表现最好（47.3%准确率，31.9% F1），FAISS紧随其后；LLM标注子集准确率约50%，但F1降至约20%，显示标签不平衡和弱标注噪声的负面影响。

Conclusion: 该任务难度高且高度依赖标签质量与类别分布，凸显高质量人工标注的必要性；所发布数据集与基线结果为巴西葡语金融NLP后续研究提供参考。

Abstract: The development of Financial Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems for Brazilian Portuguese is hindered by the scarcity of domain-specific datasets. To address this, we present a large-scale corpus of approximately 160,000 Brazilian Portuguese financial news articles collected from web sources. To establish initial benchmarks for a weakly supervised multi-class classification approach based on semantic similarity, we annotated subsets of this corpus both manually and using a Large Language Model (LLM). We evaluated three similarity-based methods -- cosine similarity, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and FAISS-based nearest-neighbor search -- using multilingual semantic embeddings. Our experiments reveal that the task is highly sensitive to label quality and class distribution. On the manually labeled subset, KNN achieved the best performance (47.3% accuracy, 31.9% F1-score), closely followed by FAISS. Conversely, on the LLM-annotated subset, while accuracy reached approximately 50%, F1-scores dropped to near 20%, highlighting the negative impact of label imbalance and noisy weak annotations. These findings establish foundational baselines for a challenging task, underscoring the critical need for high-quality manual annotations. Ultimately, this dataset and our preliminary results provide a reference point for future research in Brazilian Portuguese Financial NLP.

</details>


### [140] [Real-time Chatbot Romance Scam Detection in Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20631)
*Yi-cheng Liu,Guan-ru Lai,Yi-cheng Lai,Lyn Chao-ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于Line Messaging API的聊天机器人，利用社会科学中的七阶段理论和大语言模型，实时检测网络恋爱诈骗并提供阶段解释与防骗建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络恋爱诈骗通过社交媒体已成为严重问题，而人工智能的进步使得身份伪造变得更加容易，因此需要一种能在真实场景中降低恋爱诈骗风险的实用工具。

Method: 通过Line Messaging API开发聊天机器人，结合社会科学领域的七阶段理论，使用大语言模型检测恋爱诈骗所处的阶段；一旦检测到诈骗，聊天机器人通过大语言模型提供阶段解释和防范建议。

Result: 该系统能够在实时对话中检测恋爱诈骗的各个阶段，并在检测到诈骗时输出相应的阶段解释与防范建议。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个实用的应用，可在真实场景中使用，以降低网络恋爱诈骗的风险。

Abstract: Romance scam has become a serious problem via social media, and the improvement of artificial intelligence enable to fake identify easily. In the study, a chatbot has developed to detect romance scam in real-time through Line Messaging API. Using the seven stage theories in the social science field, the stages of romance scam can be detected through large language model. In addition, once a romance scam is detected, the chatbot provides explanations of the stage and prevention advices through large language model. The contribution of the work provides a practical application, and can be used in real scenario for reducing the risk of romance scam.

</details>


### [141] [MeddeX: A Retrieval-Augmented LLaMA-3 System with Gemini Vision for Evidence-Backed Medical Report Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20632)
*Rajdeep Pandhare,Rohit Kundaikar,Yakub Kojar,Shreyansh Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeddeX是一个结合QLoRA微调的LLaMA-3(8B)、RAG和多模态Gemini Vision的医疗报告分析系统，可对多种格式医疗文档生成准确、简洁且有依据的摘要，并在多项评估指标上超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医疗产生日益复杂的医学报告，给临床专业人员带来时间与解读负担；人工审阅流程缓慢、易出错且难以规模化，因此需要AI辅助的临床决策支持工具。

Method: 系统采用QLoRA微调的LLaMA-3(8B)模型，结合检索增强生成(RAG)与多模态Gemini Vision层；通过智能混合提取管线处理文本型PDF、扫描图像PDF和直接放射影像，并包含针对损坏及UTF-16编码文档的垃圾文本检测机制；还引入报告范围检索、自动纵向报告对比引擎和自愈式回填管线；架构由FastAPI后端、Supabase向量存储和Next.js前端组成。
