Result: 与基础LLaMA3-Med42-8B模型相比，MeddeX在ROUGE-1(+9.2%)、ROUGE-2(+10.0%)、ROUGE-L(+11.8%)、BLEU(+71.4%)和BERTScore(+0.8%)上均取得一致提升，验证了领域特定微调的有效性。

Conclusion: MeddeX是迈向AI辅助临床决策支持的实用且稳健的一步。

Abstract: Modern healthcare generates increasingly complex medical reports that challenge clinical professionals in terms of time and interpretive burden. Manual review processes are slow, error-prone, and difficult to scale. This paper presents MeddeX, a Medical Report Analyzer that combines a QLoRA fine-tuned LLaMA-3 (8B) model with Retrieval-Augmented Gen eration (RAG) and a multimodal Gemini Vision layer to deliver accurate, concise, and evidence-backed summaries of medical documents. The system handles text-native PDFs, scanned image based PDFs, and direct radiological images through an intelligent hybrid extraction pipeline that includes a novel garbage-text detection mechanism for corrupted and UTF-16-encoded doc uments. Beyond single-report Q&A, MeddeX introduces report scoped retrieval for precise contextual grounding, an automated longitudinal report comparison engine, and a self-healing backfill pipeline that lazily processes legacy documents on demand. A FastAPI backend, Supabase vector store, and Next.js fron tend complete the end-to-end architecture. Evaluation against the base LLaMA3-Med42-8B model demonstrates consistent improvements across ROUGE-1 (+9.2%), ROUGE-2 (+10.0%), ROUGE-L (+11.8%), BLEU (+71.4%), and BERTScore (+0.8%), validating the benefit of domain-specific fine-tuning. MeddeX represents a practical and robust step toward AI-assisted clinical decision support.

</details>


### [142] [Context Injection and Long-Horizon Reliability: Separating Duplicate Recognition, Local Processing, and Counter Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20633)
*Thomas Serval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在400步账本任务上逐步分解评分，发现重复识别错误随步数上升，而计数器在无关内容干扰下全面失效、经外部精确提醒即可修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程失败通常只用端任务成功率或时程长度衡量，掩盖了不同子能力的差异化失效模式，因此需要逐步、按组件评分来定位错误来源。

Method: 设计400步账本任务，将每步拆为三组件：识别5至300步前见过的记录、局部处理当前记录、写入逐步自增计数器，模型自答回注上下文。在Qwen3-8B（非思考模式、20种子、双机双后端复现）上逐步评分；并做三项预注册追踪实验：注入损坏值、每消息前重复精确外部提醒、以及无关内容块下由oracle提供紧凑状态提醒（对照长度匹配中性文本）。

Result: 重复识别错误在后期上升（第200步附近由0.0019升至0.011每风险步），来自长距回忆错误与误报上升；计数器8000步零错、局部处理稳定。每步前插入无关内容块时，计数器在20/20种子上失效（无干扰则无一失效），对局部处理影响小且与无影响不可区分。损坏计数器值在所有种子中持续不纠；每消息前精确提醒可在1-2步内重新对齐；无关块下oracle紧凑状态提醒使计数器错误为0/20（无提醒为15），优于长度匹配中性文本；单次提醒是否足够尚无定论，其他组件未受损亦未确证。

Conclusion: 按组件逐步评分可揭示被聚合或排他式错误分类掩盖的差异化失效：计数状态高度依赖上下文中的精确值，可被无关内容摧毁也可被外部提醒修复。

Abstract: Long-horizon failures of language models are usually measured as end-task success or as a horizon length. We instead score, step by step, three components of a 400-step ledger task in which every step is easy in isolation and the model's own answers are re-injected into its context: recognising a record already seen 5 to 300 steps earlier, processing the current record locally, and writing a counter incremented at every step. On Qwen3-8B (non-thinking, 20 seeds, replicated on a second machine and backend), with every step scored component by component, two results hold. First, duplicate-recognition errors rise late in the episode -- from 0.0019 to 0.011 per step at risk across step 200 -- while the counter makes no error in 8,000 steps and local processing is descriptively stable; the rise combines long-distance recall errors and rising false alarms. Second, a block of irrelevant content inserted before each step makes the counter fail in 20 of 20 seeds on each machine, against none without it, while its effect on local processing is much smaller and not distinguishable from none. Three pre-registered follow-ups probe the counter. Its value depends on the value present in the response history: a corrupted value persists without correction in every seed, and an exact external reminder, repeated before each message, re-aligns it within one to two steps. Under the irrelevant block, no counter error was observed when an exact compact reminder of the state, supplied externally by an oracle, preceded each message (0 of 20 seeds with an error, against 15 without it), and this reminder outperformed a length-matched neutral text; whether a single reminder suffices was not conclusive, and the absence of damage to the other components was not established. An aggregate or exclusive error classification can hide the difference we report.

</details>


### [143] [Jev-as-a-Judge for RAG Claim Verification: Confidence-Gated Cascading Between a Non-Generative Verifier and a Frontier LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20634)
*Alden Do Rosario,Kirollos Atef*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了非生成式决策模型与前沿LLM在RAG事实核查中的表现，发现二者在传统指标上统计不可区分，但错误结构差异显著，且级联方案的收益主要来自升级比例而非概率信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG管线通常用文本生成器来判断"证据是否支持主张"这一二分类问题，因而为生成文本支付高昂成本。作者希望探究：若改用只返回枚举值和概率、不产生文本的非生成式决策模型，能否以极低成本达到前沿LLM的准确率，并揭示传统单一指标所掩盖的部署差异。

Method: 在LLM-AggreFact的495条人工标注主张上，对两类系统进行逐项配对比较：一是非生成式决策模型（返回声明枚举中的类型化值及原生概率、无文本），二是按工程师部署方式配置的前沿LLM二分类器。同时构建级联方案，将便宜模型高置信的样本直接路由、其余升级给前沿模型，并设置随机升级对照与折外阈值。

Result: 两系统在常规指标上统计不可区分，配对差异仅-0.56分（Jev减Astra），但成本相差187倍。二者在15.2%的主张上判断不一致；便宜模型将23.2%被标注为不支持的主张误判为支持，而前沿模型为14.1%。级联达到74.10分、每千条2.92美元，对比73.83分、8.93美元；但同等预算的随机升级对照表现相当。

Conclusion: 相同准确率掩盖了不同的错误结构，常规指标无法反映部署时真正关心的误判率。级联的成本节省主要来自升级比例而非选择升级的概率信号；相对其构成中的便宜模型，级联成本高61倍仅换得0.83分提升（95% CI [-3.36, 4.86]），且是两者中更宽松的守门者。所有预测、提示、定价与代码均已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation asks one question at volume: does this evidence support this claim? The answer is one bit, yet most pipelines obtain it from a text generator and pay generation prices for the prose wrapped around it. We compare a non-generative decision model, which returns a typed value from a declared enumeration together with a native probability and no text, against a frontier LLM configured as an engineer would deploy it for binary classification. Both answer the same 495 human-labelled claims from LLM-AggreFact, so every comparison is within-item. We report three things. First, the two systems are statistically indistinguishable on the metric this task is conventionally scored by, a paired difference of -0.56 points, Jev minus Astra, at a 187-fold cost difference. Second, that tie carries almost no information about deployment: the systems disagree on 15.2% of claims, and the cheaper one admits 23.2% of the claims annotators marked unsupported against the frontier model's 14.1%. A component whose job is to catch unsupported claims should be judged on that rate, and the conventional headline hides it. Third, because the errors differ they compose: routing the claims the cheap model is confident about and escalating the rest reaches 74.10 at USD 2.92 per thousand claims against 73.83 at USD 8.93, with the threshold chosen out of fold. But a matched random-escalation control at in-sample thresholds, spending the same budget by coin flip, does as well, so what buys the saving is the escalation share and not the probability that picks what to escalate. Measured against the cheap model it is built from, the cascade costs 61-fold more for 0.83 points, 95% CI [-3.36, 4.86], and it is the more permissive guard of the two. Equal accuracy conceals unequal errors. All predictions, prompts, pricing and code are released.

</details>


### [144] [Exploring the Design Space of Permutation Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20635)
*Zhe Xu,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Jean-Pascal Billaud,Tianyi Chen,Yanhui Guo,Christina Huang,Baekjin Kim,Jimmy Kim,Yufei Li,Daqi Sun,Jiachen Sun,Dian Yu,Zaiwei Zhang,Kaushik Rangadurai,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散语言模型下的置换生成问题，比较硬指派、软指派与直接排序打分三种参数化方式，在亚马逊三领域推荐重排任务上评估，发现三种设计各有取舍，且在单次函数评估下即可与受限掩码扩散基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换生成需要同时满足文本条件排序和候选与输出位置之间的全局一对一匹配。不同参数化方式在预测与解码的不同阶段引入置换约束，导致训练目标和采样流程各异，因此有必要系统比较这些设计以明确各自适用条件。

Method: 基于扩散语言模型，提出并对比三种置换参数化：(1) 对打乱的候选索引序列做硬指派预测；(2) 对双随机矩阵做软指派预测；(3) 直接预测排序分数后排序。在三个亚马逊领域的推荐重排任务上评测，候选集规模为10和20。

Result: 在单次函数评估下，硬指派与软指派变体的 Recall@1 与受限掩码扩散基线相当；直接排序分数预测无需匈牙利指派或 Sinkhorn 归一化即可保证输出为合法置换。增加函数评估次数并不能稳定提升排序质量，其效果强烈依赖于状态表示与训练目标。

Conclusion: 扰动方式、预测参数化、训练目标与解码器应当针对基于语言模型去噪器的置换扩散进行联合设计，而非独立选择。

Abstract: Permutation generation with language models requires both text-conditioned ranking and a global one-to-one assignment between candidates and output positions. We study this problem through three diffusion-LLM parameterizations: hard-assignment prediction over shuffled candidate-index sequences, soft-assignment prediction over doubly stochastic matrices, and direct ordering-score prediction followed by sorting. The three families place permutation constraints at different stages of prediction and decoding and therefore support different training objectives and sampling procedures. We evaluate these designs on recommendation reranking across three Amazon domains with candidate sets of size 10 and 20. At one function evaluation, the hard- and soft-assignment variants achieve Recall@1 comparable to a constrained masked-diffusion baseline, whereas direct ordering-score prediction guarantees valid permutations without Hungarian assignment or Sinkhorn normalization. Additional function evaluations do not consistently improve ranking quality, and their effect depends strongly on the state representation and training objective. These results show that corruption, prediction parameterization, objective, and decoder should be designed jointly for permutation diffusion with language-model denoisers.

</details>


### [145] [AbridgeKV: Compressing Long Reasoning into the KV Cache of Learned Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20641)
*Jack Cai,Lijie Yang,Vasilis Kontonis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shivam Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AbridgeKV 通过训练模型将已生成文本块压缩为少量可学习的暂停 token 的 KV 条目，从而在长链式思维解码中大幅降低 KV 缓存占用，在保持准确率的同时实现 3.5 倍缓存缩减与 3.2 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维解码受显存限制：生成 token 的 KV 缓存随测试时计算量线性增长。单纯的缓存驱逐无法解决问题，因为模型未曾在被移除条目的缓存上训练，会生成更长甚至触及长度上限的输出。因此作者主张训练模型主动压缩自身输出。

Method: AbridgeKV 将每个已完成生成文本块压缩为少量可学习暂停 token 的 KV 条目（这些暂停 token 为额外输入 token，有训练好的嵌入但不产生输出）。压缩完成后释放该块原始缓存，后续 token 转而关注暂停 token 的 KV 条目。三个关键设计：暂停 token 的 KV 条目位于其所替代的块内位置；按各层条目实测价值在层间分配缓存预算；在相对较大的块上进行压缩。

Result: 在 AIME、HMMT、AMC、BRUMO、CMIMC 与 SMT 的 446 道竞赛题上（生成 32K token），保留四分之一生成缓存即与同等训练的全 KV 模型在准确率和输出长度上持平；预算相同时最强已发表驱逐方法输出远更长，最接近的训练压缩器在相同数据上重训后损失 10 分。在 vLLM 中部署，每序列缓存缩小 3.5 倍，饱和 H100 完成一题比全 KV 快 3.2 倍。

Conclusion: 通过训练模型用暂停 token 压缩自身输出缓存，可在显著降低 KV 缓存占用与提升推理效率的同时保持长推理任务的准确率与输出长度，是一种有效的长链思维解码显存优化方案。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought decoding is memory-bound: the KV cache of the generated tokens grows linearly with the test-time compute being spent. Evicting cache entries does not fix this, because a model never trained on a cache with entries removed generates longer, often up to the length limit. We instead train the model to compress its own output. AbridgeKV compresses each completed block of generated text into the KV entries of a few learned pause tokens. These are extra input tokens with trained embeddings that produce no output. Once the pause tokens have encoded the block, AbridgeKV frees the block's original cache; subsequent tokens attend to the pause tokens' KV entries in its place. Three novel choices make this work: the pause tokens' KV entries take positions inside the block they replace, the cache budget is allocated across layers according to the measured value of each layer's entries, and compression operates on relatively large blocks. On 446 competition problems from AIME, HMMT, AMC, BRUMO, CMIMC and SMT with 32K-token generations, keeping a quarter of the generated cache matches an identically trained full-KV model in accuracy and output length; the strongest published evictor at a matched budget writes far longer, and the closest trained compressor, retrained on the same data, loses 10 points. Served in vLLM, the cache is 3.5x smaller per sequence and a saturated H100 finishes a problem 3.2x faster than with full KV.

</details>


### [146] [CoTS: Competitive Token Selection for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20642)
*Jie Yu,Yuxi Sun,Zhaoxu Li,Chunxiao Liu,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的竞争性令牌选择框架CoTS，将潜在推理干预从二元模式切换重新表述为令牌级的连续分配，利用两个最可能候选令牌的概率竞争动态平衡离散承诺与软探索，在六个推理基准上超越了标准CoT和现有免训练潜在推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法用连续软嵌入或隐状态替代采样令牌，能保留更丰富的中间表示和多种推理路径。但潜在推理的需求在解码步骤间存在差异：高置信预测适合离散提交，模糊步骤则更受益于保留多个候选项。现有方法要么全局启用软激活，要么基于不确定性阈值进行硬切换，将潜在干预视为全有或全无的决策，无法连续自适应地调整强度。

Method: CoTS是一个免训练框架，把潜在干预从二元模式选择重构为令牌级的连续分配。它利用两个最可能候选令牌之间的概率竞争来动态平衡离散承诺与软探索：第二候选代表模型偏好续写的最强替代项，二者的分离程度直接反映当前离散选择的稳定性。当某一候选明显占优时，使表示贴近采样得到的离散令牌以维持稳定推理轨迹；竞争增强时则增大软表示的贡献以保留合理备选。该分配机制无需人工调参的切换阈值或混合系数，也不增加额外前向传播。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，CoTS持续优于标准CoT和最先进的免训练潜在推理方法。

Conclusion: CoTS通过基于概率竞争的连续令牌级分配机制，无需阈值调参和额外计算开销，有效统一了离散承诺与软探索，为免训练潜在推理提供了更优的解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods move beyond discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by replacing sampled tokens with continuous soft embeddings or hidden states, enabling richer intermediate representations and preserving alternative reasoning paths. However, the need for latent reasoning varies across decoding steps: confident predictions favor discrete commitment, whereas ambiguous steps benefit from preserving multiple plausible alternatives. Existing approaches typically handle this variation through either global soft activation or hard switching based on uncertainty thresholds, treating latent intervention as an all-or-nothing decision rather than continuously adapting its strength. To address this limitation, we propose Competitive Token Selection (CoTS), a training-free framework that reframes latent intervention from binary mode selection into continuous allocation at the token level. CoTS uses the probability competition between the two most likely candidates to dynamically balance discrete commitment and soft exploration at each reasoning step. The second candidate represents the strongest alternative to the model's preferred continuation, so the separation between the two directly reflects the stability of the current discrete choice. When one candidate clearly dominates, CoTS keeps the representation close to the sampled discrete token to preserve a stable reasoning trajectory; as the competition strengthens, it increases the contribution of the soft representation to retain plausible alternatives. This competition-based allocation requires neither manually tuned switching thresholds nor mixing coefficients, and introduces no additional forward passes. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CoTS consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free latent reasoning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning.

</details>


### [147] [Does Personalization Break Trust? Open-Set Rejection Calibration Under Few-Shot On-Device Intent Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20652)
*Vaibhav Prajapati,Pushp Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 少样本LoRA个性化会损害虚拟助手的域外(OOS)拒绝能力，改用马氏距离打分可在多数数据集上以零训练成本挽回大部分损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对端侧虚拟助手少样本个性化(如LoRA微调)的评估几乎只关注是否学会新命令，而忽略了助手能否拒绝不匹配任何意图的输入(OOS拒绝)这一问题。

Method: 在五个公开意图分类基准上，用五个随机种子评估朴素少样本LoRA个性化对OOS拒绝AUROC的影响；通过打分器不变性检验区分打分器校准假象与真实效应；分析已知意图区域嵌入边距收缩(合并Spearman rho=0.61)；并提出以马氏距离替代softmax置信度进行拒绝打分。

Result: 每个基准上OOS拒绝AUROC均下降(平均降幅0.055–0.258)，而域内准确率保持不变；部分下降源于标准置信度打分器在校准上的假象，但任意打分器下仍存在较小真实效应；马氏距离方案在5个数据集中3个恢复了大部分损失质量且无准确率损失，优于两个额外OOD打分基线，并在样本数、LoRA秩和第二个基座编码器上均具泛化性。

Conclusion: 少样本个性化确实会真实削弱OOS拒绝能力，马氏距离打分是一种部署成本低的有效缓解手段；作者如实报告该方法的不足并开源全部代码以保证可复现。

Abstract: Always-on virtual assistants increasingly personalize on-device to new user-specific commands via few-shot fine-tuning (e.g., LoRA), but this adaptation is evaluated almost exclusively on whether it learns the new commands, not on whether the assistant can still recognize an input that matches none of its intents - the out-of-scope (OOS) rejection problem. We show that naive few-shot LoRA personalization degrades OOS-rejection AUROC (area under the ROC curve) on every one of five public intent-classification benchmarks (mean drop $0.055$--$0.258$, five seeds each), while in-scope accuracy is preserved. A scorer-invariant check shows part of this drop is an artifact of the standard confidence scorer's own miscalibration under adaptation, though a smaller, genuine effect remains under any scorer; we trace it to a measurable shrinkage of the embedding margin around known-intent regions (pooled Spearman rho=0.61$). A deployment-cheap fix is scoring rejection by Mahalanobis distance instead of softmax confidence without extra training/retraining recovers most of the lost quality on 3 of 5 datasets at no accuracy cost, beats two further OOD scoring baselines, and generalizes across shot count, LoRA rank, and a second base encoder. We report honestly where the fix falls short and release all code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [148] [Reference-Guided Turn-Taking Style Transfer for Full-Duplex Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20763)
*Yuxuan Chen,Yang Xiao,Jack Sun,Chak Sun,Brick Li,Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从全双工语音语言模型的残差流中提取出一个单方向向量——"主动性向量"，它既能读取模型的发言决策（提前最多800毫秒预测起始），也能调节发言比例（从6-12%到42-61%），并可将特定说话者的轮流风格迁移到模型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音语言模型每帧都需决定是否发言，这构成了其对话"性格"。然而每组权重只对应一种固定性格，而人类说话者的风格是可调节的。现有控制手段多从外部干预，且没有研究指出究竟哪个量决定了模型说多少。

Method: 作者在模型自身上进行无训练、无标注的识别，从后期层的残差流中提取出一个单一方向（initiative vector）。该方向既能读取发言决策，也能在生成时按比例缩放以平移该决策；此外利用一段简短的参考录音来迁移说话者的轮流风格。

Result: 该方向可提前最多800毫秒预测发言起始；缩放后发言比例从6-12%移至42-61%（不引导时为21-30%），且在对话、权重和语料上保持一致。短参考录音足以迁移说话者风格，且迁移具有参考特异性：换用他人参考会使匹配变差（93-98%的情况），而最佳统一剂量与不引导难以区分。

Conclusion: 轮流发言风格并非固化在权重中，而是一个可以被读取、平移并按个体校准的方向。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken language models decide at every frame whether to take the floor, defining a conversational personality -- yet each set of weights encodes exactly one, which human speakers bracket rather than share. Existing controls mostly act from outside; none identifies the quantity that sets how much the model speaks. We identify it: a single late-layer residual-stream direction, the initiative vector, extracted from the model's own conversations without training or annotation. The same direction reads the decision, anticipating onsets up to 800 ms ahead, and shifts it: scaled during generation, it moves speech ratio from 6-12% to 42-61% (no steering: 21-30%), consistently across conversations, weights, and corpora. A short reference recording suffices to transfer a speaker's turn-taking style to the model, and the transfer is reference-specific: swapping in another speaker's reference worsens the match in 93-98% of cases, whereas the best uniform dose is indistinguishable from no steering. Turn-taking style is not frozen into the weights but a direction that can be read, shifted, and calibrated per person. Audio examples are available at https://elpsykongloo.github.io/initiative-vector/.

</details>


### [149] [Verdict: Encode Once, Decide Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20778)
*Avdhesh Singh Chouhan,Jack Perry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Verdict-MQ 是一个 395M 开源编码器，一次读取案例即可回答所有问题，在类型化决策基准上显著优于 421M 的 Laya 专家模型，但在 RACE 和 BoolQ 上表现不及匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化组织工作需要对同一状态回答多个小型类型化问题，而标准交叉编码器等现有模型需对每个问题单独进行一次输入处理，效率低。

Method: 提出 Verdict-MQ，通过结构化注意力掩码使状态编码独立于问题、问题之间互不影响、选项顺序无关，实现一次读取案例即可回答所有问题。

Result: 在类型化决策基准上，395M Verdict-MQ 在五个新种子上均显著优于 421M Laya 专家（KL 0.080 vs 0.117，Brier 0.045 vs 0.061），准确率 0.801 vs 0.767；在 MultiRC 上胜过匹配基线，但在 RACE 和 BoolQ 上落后，训练不稳定；控制实验显示为每个问题单独复制状态在 RACE 和 MultiRC 上提升 0.9-3.5 点，但延迟增加 1.4-2.5 倍。

Conclusion: Verdict-MQ 在类型化决策任务上高效且与软金标签一致性更高，但在部分通用推理任务上表现不如匹配基线，且共享状态设计在多数场景下以更低延迟达到相当效果。

Abstract: Automating organizational work means answering many small typed questions about one piece of state (does this email need a reply, how urgent is it, who owns it) with probabilities good enough to act on without a human. The open typed-decision model we compare against, like a standard cross-encoder, answers each question with a separate pass over the input. We present Verdict-MQ, an open encoder that reads a case once and answers all of its questions: given identical retained state tokens after truncation (verified on every reported case), a structured attention mask makes the state encoding independent of the questions, keeps questions from influencing each other, and makes option order irrelevant by construction. On the typed-decisions benchmark, a 395M Verdict-MQ matches the soft gold labels significantly better than the released 421M Laya specialist on all five fresh seeds (KL 0.080 vs 0.117, Brier 0.045 vs 0.061), an advantage that survives per-system temperature scaling; its higher accuracy (0.801 vs 0.767) lies past the benchmark's own saturation point, so we read that margin as closer agreement with the benchmark's labelling teacher. On RACE, BoolQ and MultiRC, against a matched fine-tuned baseline it wins on MultiRC and loses on RACE and on BoolQ, where its training is unstable; we measure zero-shot references ourselves, including a closed general system (Jev) that beats every fine-tuned encoder. A controlled comparison measures what the shared state costs: giving each question its own question-aware copy of the state gains 0.9-3.5 points on RACE and MultiRC at 1.4-2.5x the per-case latency, and nothing measurable on typed decisions, with the invariance guarantees unchanged.

</details>


### [150] [APRiL: An Open-ended Benchmark for Chinese Social Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20785)
*Tian Lan,Han Zhang,Shanshan Wang,Yahan Yu,Jiang Li,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出APRiL，一个面向中文社交意识的开源开放式基准，包含2196个评估实例、六大类别和两类任务，通过实例特定评分标准评测模型社交情境分析与社交得体回应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益参与社交互动，恰当回应需识别隐含线索、推断心理状态、识别冲突并考虑后果。然而现有基准多聚焦单一社交能力、任务形式受限或交互场景单一，对多样情境下的社交意识覆盖不足。

Method: 构建APRiL开放式中文社交意识基准，含2196个社交意识评估实例，覆盖六大类别，设计两种互补任务：社交情境分析（评估对社交线索、心理状态与冲突结构的理解）和社交意识回应（评估能否将理解转化为恰当自然的回应）；为每个实例配备特定评分标准。

Result: 在来自六个模型家族的12个模型上进行实验，发现存在一致性的“理解—回应”差距：高质量的情境分析能提升回应质量，而薄弱分析可能降低回应质量。

Conclusion: APRiL为推进中文语境下具备社交意识的语言模型提供了系统性资源，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in social interactions, where appropriate responses require recognizing implicit cues, inferring mental states, identifying conflicts, and considering potential consequences. However, existing benchmarks often focus on specific social abilities, constrained task formats, or particular interaction settings, offering limited coverage of social awareness across diverse contexts. To address this gap, we introduce APRiL, an open-ended benchmark for Chinese social awareness with instance-specific rubrics. APRiL contains 2,196 Social Awareness Evaluation Instances across six categories and two complementary tasks: Social Scenario Analysis evaluates models' understanding of social cues, mental states, and conflict structures, while Socially Aware Response assesses whether they can translate this understanding into appropriate, natural responses. Experiments on 12 models from six families reveal a consistent understanding-to-response gap. Higher-quality scenario analyses improve responses, while weak analyses may reduce response quality. APRiL provides a systematic resource for advancing socially aware language models in Chinese contexts. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/APRiL-05DE/.

</details>


### [151] [PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20808)
*Giovana Kerche Bonás,Thales Sales Almeida,Marcelo da Silva Reis,Rodrigo Nogueira,Marcos Meideiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRISM框架通过跨精度、跨语言、跨规模的激活干预迁移，利用低资源设置（NF4量化标签与跨语言迁移）暴露并改进模型对齐，有效减少危险工具调用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献显示，语言模型在文本拒绝的同时仍可能出现不安全的工具调用，形成隐藏脆弱性。为系统研究这一隐蔽风险并改善模型对齐，作者提出PRISM框架。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，评估激活干预在精度、语言和模型规模上的迁移能力。核心方法为利用低资源设置：使用NF4量化副本的行为标签进行干预方向估计，并跨语言、跨规模映射配对良性激活以迁移安全干预。

Result: NF4行为标签在极少场景下即可稳定估计干预；五语言NF4监督的方向可迁移至未见的阿拉伯语，在无需目标语言调优的情况下将Llama-3.1-8B的不安全违规减少21.95个百分点；安全干预成功迁移至更大模型（最高70B参数）。

Conclusion: PRISM表明，来自压缩模型的辅助监督与多语言数据能够发现并缓解不同模型家族中的危险工具调用行为，并已开源代码与相关成果。

Abstract: Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.

</details>


### [152] [Wolof-Adapted Industry-Ready Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20824)
*Gwénolé Lecorvé,Delphine Charlet,Pierre Adam,Floriane Behanzin,Lionel Delphin-Poulat,Johannes Heinecke,Géraldine Damnati,Pelin Kaytmaz,Nourredine Khaous,Charbel Kindji,Meriam Maadi,Abdoulaye Mbaye,Claire Perroux,Ismaël Rousseau,Papa Séga Wadé,Gilles Xiberras,Olivier Zhang,Malek Zitouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对沃洛夫语（Wolof）开源权重模型支持薄弱、API模型存在成本与治理约束的问题，构建了0.6B-14B开源权重模型，通过指令微调、偏好对齐与模型合并进行适配，并在63项任务上验证了其理解与生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沃洛夫语使用者约2300万（主要在塞内加尔），但开源权重语言模型对其支持薄弱；而使用API模型会带来成本、治理和本地部署主权方面的限制。因此有必要构建适配沃洛夫语的开源权重模型，以支持本地化部署。

Method: 采用指令微调、偏好对齐和模型合并的方法，将0.6B至14B规模的开源权重模型适配到沃洛夫语，并介绍了相应的训练流程与数据。

Result: 在63项公开及内部任务上的大量实验表明，模型在沃洛夫语理解与生成任务上表现良好，同时在多语言和跨语言能力上保持有效；其中8B和14B模型相比规模大得多的商业系统具有较强竞争力。模型已在内部客服试点中评估，并已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过指令微调、偏好对齐与模型合并，可有效将中小规模开源权重模型适配到低资源语言沃洛夫语，并在理解、生成及跨语言任务上兼具竞争力，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Wolof is spoken by 23M people, mostly in Senegal, but remains weakly supported by open-weight language models, while API models raise cost, governance, and sovereignty constraints for local deployments. In this paper, we present 0.6B-14B open-weight models adapted to Wolof through instruction tuning, preference alignment, and model merging. After presenting the pipeline and data, extensive experiments on 63~public and in-house tasks show good performance on Wolof understanding and generation while remaining effective on other languages and cross-lingual skills, with the 8B and 14B models being particularly competitive with substantially larger commercial systems. The models are currently being evaluated in internal customer-care pilots and are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Orange/wolof-lm .

</details>


### [153] [CoTranslator: Reverse to Forward Chain-of-Thought Translator for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20828)
*Zinan Tang,Zhuoshi Pan,Kairui Chen,Ze Yan,Shaomian Zheng,Yukun Zhang,Gang Wang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoTranslator通过将逆向CoT翻译为正向验证分布，实现高效可迁移的推理蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏需要带有正确答案的思维链数据，但高效获取这类数据仍具挑战。正向生成加拒绝采样虽能产出经验证的纯问题响应，但对难题需要大量教师调用；逆向生成更便宜，但因依赖已知答案易导致事后合理化，且与学生无答案推理场景不匹配。

Method: 提出CoTranslator，学习将逆向CoT响应翻译到经验证的正向响应分布上。在少量配对的逆向与拒绝采样正向响应上训练，支持大规模生成与验证学生训练数据。

Result: 在6个数学模型、6个学生模型上，平均准确率较语义抑制方法最高提升5.82个百分点；两个4B学生保留拒绝采样95.0%的平均准确率，仅用35.9%的估算算力。无需重训即可迁移到新数据集、不同教师和STEM问题，4B与8B学生STEM平均准确率分别提升4.88和4.31个百分点。

Conclusion: CoTranslator为推理蒸馏提供了一种高效且可迁移的方法。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation requires chain-of-thought (CoT) data with correct answers, but obtaining such data efficiently remains challenging. Forward CoT generation (FCG) followed by rejection sampling (RS) produces verified, question-only responses, yet may require many teacher calls for difficult problems. Reverse CoT generation (RCG) is cheaper because it conditions on the known answer, but access to this answer can lead to post-hoc rationalization and a mismatch with the student's answer-free inference setting. We propose CoTranslator, which learns to translate reverse CoT responses toward the distribution of verified forward responses. Trained on a small set of paired reverse and rejection-sampled forward responses, it supports large-scale generation and verification of student training data. Across six student models evaluated on six mathematics benchmarks, CoTranslator improves average accuracy over semantic suppression by up to $5.82$ percentage points. For two 4B students, it retains $95.0\%$ of rejection sampling's average accuracy while using $35.9\%$ of its estimated compute. Without retraining, the same translator transfers to a new mathematics dataset, a different teacher, and STEM problems, improving average STEM accuracy by $4.88$ and $4.31$ percentage points for 4B and 8B students, respectively. These results show that CoTranslator offers an efficient and transferable approach to reasoning distillation.

</details>


### [154] [PEDBench: Can Speech Models Detect Phonetic Errors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20830)
*Zhonghao Shi,Zackary Rackauckas,Jasmine Yip,Savannah Halpern,Simon Campos,Lauren Brace,Emma Yang,Tiantian Feng,Xuan Shi,Shrikanth Narayanan,Teresa Satterfield,Ying Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PEDBench，首个覆盖自然到受控语音的语音错误检测基准，包含儿童真实错误、演员脚本错误和合成错误三类数据，评估13个先进语音模型后发现其难以可靠检测音素级错误，基于合成数据微调的语音语言模型在词级PED上优于现有基线，但仍存在仿真到真实的域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音素级错误检测（PED）对教育和临床语音评估与干预至关重要，但现有语音模型能否可靠检测细粒度语音错误尚不明确，主要原因是缺乏高质量评测数据。

Method: 构建PEDBench基准，涵盖自然到受控语音连续体：儿童真实发音错误录音（N=334）、人类演员脚本化错误发音（N=1,188）、基于音素条件文本转语音生成的合成错误（N=1,083）；所有语音标注由两名受过语音学训练的标注员转写与核验；评估13个先进语音模型，并用合成数据微调语音语言模型。

Result: 13个先进语音模型均无法可靠检测语音错误，即使在声学变异较小的演员与合成语音受控条件下亦然；模型精确率低，常将正确发音误判为错误；经合成数据微调的语音语言模型在词级PED上优于商业与开源基线，但仍存在仿真到真实的域差距。

Conclusion: PEDBench填补了高质量评测数据空白，揭示当前模型在音素错误检测上的不足；作者计划公开代码、配方及演员与合成数据集，以支持更准确的PED系统开发。

Abstract: Phonetic error detection (PED) aims to identify phoneme-level substitutions, deletions, and insertions and is essential for educational and clinical speech assessment and intervention. Despite recent advances in speech models for phoneme recognition, their ability to reliably detect fine-grained phonetic errors remains unclear because of a lack of high-quality evaluation data. To address this gap, we introduce PEDBench, the first PED benchmark spanning a continuum from natural to controlled speech. It comprises real-world recordings of spoken errors made by children (N=334), scripted error productions by human actors (N=1,188), and synthetic error productions generated using phone-conditioned text-to-speech (N=1,083). All phonetic annotations were transcribed and verified by two phonetically trained annotators. Our evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art speech models shows that they fail to reliably detect phonetic errors, even under controlled conditions with acted and synthetic speech, where acoustic variability is reduced. The models also exhibit low precision, frequently misclassifying correctly pronounced phonemes as errors. Our speech-language models, fine-tuned on synthetic data, outperform commercial and open-source baselines on word-level PED, although a sim-to-real domain gap remains. We plan to publicly release our code and recipes, along with our acted and synthetic datasets, to support the development of more accurate PED systems.

</details>


### [155] [Does Morphological Correctness Matter for Tokenization? A Scrambled Control Study in Tagalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20859)
*Abdul Rehman Antall,Baidya Nath Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了针对他加禄语中缀形态的 tokenizer MorphAustro-Tok，将其与 BPE 和打乱词素的对照进行比较，发现对机器翻译有稳定提升，但形态学上的正确性在实验规模下并未产生可检测的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型学感知的 tokenizer 通常声称尊重词素边界的切分能保留频次驱动切分所破坏的信息，但以往研究往往在“假设这些边界必须语言学正确”的前提下证明其有效，未将“尊重词素边界”与“词素分析是否正确”两个因素分离。作者希望厘清真正带来收益的是哪一部分。

Method: 以他加禄语的中缀现象为切入点（如 s-um-ulat 与 s-in-ulat，词根不连续，BPE 合并无法还原）。作者构建 MorphAustro-Tok，将词根作为连续单元输出，并附加表示位置的中缀 token。对照组包括语料匹配的 BPE 基线，以及在三个 fertility 水平中的两个上设置的“打乱词素”对照——该对照保持词素数量、可逆性和类型一致性，但随机化伪中缀。

Result: 在 33 次运行中，MorphAustro-Tok 相比匹配的 BPE 使他加禄语机器翻译分别提升 0.84、1.49 和 3.40 个句级 chrF++ 点。token 数量无法解释该增益，因为随着切分变粗，token 数反而增加；模型容量也不能解释：在最粗水平上，打乱对照组参数更多但得分更低。在两个受控水平上，打乱分析带来的增益变化小于种子间离散度，因此在该规模下未检测到形态学正确性的贡献。

Conclusion: 收益来源于尊重词素边界并以结构化方式表示不连续词根，而非词素分析在语言学上是否“正确”。所谓类型学感知 tokenizer 的优势，至少在该实验规模下，并不来自形态学正确性。

Abstract: Typology-aware tokenizers are motivated by the claim that segmentation which respects morpheme boundaries preserves information that frequency-driven segmentation destroys. Studies routinely establish that it helps while assuming the boundaries must be linguistically correct. We separate them. Tagalog marks voice with infixes (s-um-ulat 'wrote', s-in-ulat 'was written'), so the root is not contiguous and no sequence of BPE merges can recover it. We build MorphAustro-Tok, a tokenizer that emits the root as a contiguous unit followed by a positional infix token, and compare it against a corpus-matched BPE baseline and, at two of three fertility levels, against a scrambled-morpheme control that preserves morph count, invertibility and per-type consistency while randomising the pseudo-infix. Across 33 runs, MorphAustro-Tok improves Tagalog machine translation over matched BPE by 0.84, 1.49 and 3.40 mean sentence-level chrF++ points. Token count does not explain the gain, since it grows as segmentation coarsens, and neither does capacity: at the coarsest level the scrambled arm carries more parameters and scores lower. Scrambling the analysis moves the gain by less than between-seed dispersion at both controlled levels, so we detect no contribution from morphological correctness at this scale.

</details>


### [156] [Prune the Summary, Not the History: Source-Judged Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20927)
*Guanghui Min,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现长程LLM智能体的上下文压缩摘要混入了压缩器自身的推理内容，难与环境事实区分，导致失败；提出免训练的Source-Judged Compaction（SJC）后处理步骤，用非生成式判定模型Jev对每条摘要条目进行来源打分并删除无来源条目，在两个基准上提升了可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体依赖上下文压缩：当交互历史超出上下文窗口时，压缩器用自然语言摘要替换历史。但压缩提示词要求给出决策和下一步行动，因此摘要混合了环境确立的事实与压缩器自己写的推理，智能体事后无法区分二者。对广泛使用的结构化压缩器分析发现，83%的失败运行可追溯到执行了某条具体摘要条目的动作，且其中多数条目无环境输出支持。

Method: 提出Source-Judged Compaction（SJC），一种免训练的后处理步骤。它对每条摘要条目打分，判断其在环境输出或上一轮摘要中是否存在来源，并删除无来源的条目。该方法不改变智能体也不改变压缩器，每次压缩仅增加一次低成本的判定请求。判定器为Jev，一种非生成式决策模型，以校准概率而非文本回答来源问题。

Result: 在AppWorld和OfficeBench上，针对两种智能体模型，SJC相较底层压缩器将跨运行可靠性提升2.1%–8.9%，且不增加智能体的token消耗。

Conclusion: 上下文压缩摘要会引入无环境依据的推理内容并引发失败，通过轻量的来源判定与过滤（SJC）即可在无需训练、不增加token成本的前提下显著提升长程智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents rely on context compaction: when the interaction history outgrows the context window, a compressor replaces it with a natural-language summary. We show that these summaries contain more than a record of what happened. Compaction prompts ask for decisions and next steps, so each summary mixes facts established by the environment with reasoning written by the compressor, and the agent cannot tell the two apart afterwards. In an analysis of a widely used structured compressor, 83% of failed runs trace back to an action that carries out a specific summary entry, most of which no environment output supports. We propose Source-Judged Compaction (SJC), a training-free post-processing step that scores each summary entry for whether it has a source in the environment outputs or the previous summary, and removes the entries that do not. It changes neither the agent nor the compressor and adds one inexpensive judge request per compaction. The judge is Jev, a non-generative decision model that answers the source question with a calibrated probability instead of text. On AppWorld and OfficeBench with two agent models, SJC improves cross-run reliability over the underlying compressor by 2.1%-8.9% without increasing the agent's token usage.

</details>


### [157] [Same Context, Different Agents: A Behavioral Study of Semantic Layers, Orchestration and Compaction in Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20959)
*Zhivar Sourati,Marianne Menglin Liu,Zheng Wang,Rongguang Wang,Sai Ashish Somayajula,Tao Sheng,Kyu J. Han,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了三种上下文工程（CET）在数据智能体中的效果，发现其增益取决于智能体的行为类型，并依此给出了选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体需整合文件、数据库、文档和网络等异构数据，其成功依赖上下文。现有CET研究各自独立验证，未考虑智能体行为如何影响其效果。

Method: 综合分析语义层、编排、压缩三种CET单独及组合的效果，在5个基准的1,514个长时程任务上测试7种大语言模型，覆盖行为、准确率和成本。

Result: 无CET的智能体分为三类行为：调查者、不查数据即作答的过早作答者、放弃的提前停止者。各CET只有匹配对应行为组时才能将收益转化为准确率提升（如编排对过早作答者）；而对调查者，同样CET可能有害：语义层使其停止查看，编排使其过度自信致准确率降5个百分点，压缩使其重复步骤。组合CET的增益与损失均小于各自之和。

Conclusion: 提出了依据智能体行为、任务需求和准确率—成本权衡来选择CET的指导原则。

Abstract: Answering questions over heterogeneous data means combining evidence from files, databases, documents and the web. Data agents automate this, but their success depends on context. Three context-engineering techniques (CETs) help by shaping it: semantic layers describe the data, orchestration splits work among agents, and compaction condenses interaction history. Existing studies validate each CET alone, leaving open how agent behavior shapes its effect. We present a comprehensive analysis of the three CETs, alone and combined, with seven large language models (LLMs) on 1,514 long-horizon tasks from five benchmarks, covering behavior, accuracy and cost. We find that data agents without CETs form three behavior groups: investigators, premature answerers that answer without inspecting data, and early stoppers that give up. Each CET delivers its known benefit, from more investigation to smaller prompts, but only the behavior group it matches turns that benefit into accuracy, such as premature answerers under orchestration. For investigators, the same CETs can hurt: the semantic layer stops them from looking, orchestration makes them overconfident, costing 5 percentage points, and compaction makes them repeat steps. Combining CETs yields smaller gains and losses than their sum. We derive guidance for choosing CETs by agent behavior, task demands and accuracy-cost trade-offs.

</details>


### [158] [One Bias Score, Two Factors: How Mitigations Affect Option Bias in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20993)
*Hyunjong Ok,Minjae Park,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用"合并选择率标准差"统一了选项字母选择偏差与顺序敏感性两种视角，将其分解为翻转率与位置偏斜两个无量纲因子，并据此比较了先验校正与顺序聚合等缓解策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选选项重排会改变模型答案，既有研究分别从"选择偏差（偏好某些字母）"和"顺序敏感性（不同顺序下答案变化）"两个角度研究，但二者缺乏统一度量，导致难以比较不同缓解方法究竟消除了什么。作者希望建立一个无标签依赖的分解框架，将两者联系起来。

Method: 定义合并选择率标准差（pooled SD），衡量字母被选择的整体不均衡程度。在每道题各选项在每个位置出现次数相等的设计下，证明该量恰等于"翻转率"（跨顺序选中选项发生变化的题目比例）与"位置偏斜"（这些题目中字母选择率的标准差）之积。在41个检查点、12个基准上计算两因子，并考察先验校正与"顺序与其逆序聚合"两种缓解手段对它们的影响。

Result: 翻转率随准确率与输出不确定性变化，位置偏斜则跟踪估计的选项符号先验。针对选择偏差的先验校正主要降低位置偏斜；顺序对聚合（逆转缓解）主要降低翻转率。两者对"正确答案字母间准确率极差"的压缩幅度相近，但聚合带来的模型排名逆转更少。先验校正后再聚合可同时降低两个因子，并进一步压缩该准确率极差。不过聚合降低翻转率的部分原因是把题目固定在同一个错误选项上。

Conclusion: 建议同时报告翻转率与位置偏斜两因子，以区分不同缓解手段的作用机制，并配合准确率以区分"稳定性"与"正确性"；顺序聚合的稳定性提升需警惕其可能固化为一致错误。

Abstract: Reordering multiple-choice options can change a model's answer, an effect studied as selection bias (a preference for certain option letters) and as order sensitivity (changes in answers across option orderings). We connect these perspectives through the pooled selection-rate standard deviation (pooled SD), which measures how unevenly option letters are selected across questions and orderings. When every option of each question appears equally often at each position, the pooled SD is exactly the product of two label-free factors: the flip rate and the positional skew. The flip rate is the fraction of questions whose chosen option changes across orderings; the positional skew is the standard deviation of option-letter selection rates among those questions. Across 41 checkpoints and 12 benchmarks, the flip rate tracks accuracy and output uncertainty, while the positional skew tracks the estimated option-symbol prior. Prior correction for selection bias mainly reduces the positional skew. Aggregating each ordering with its reversal, a mitigation for order sensitivity, mainly reduces the flip rate. Both reduce the range of accuracy across correct-answer letters by similar amounts, but aggregation leaves fewer model-rank reversals. Applying prior correction before aggregation reduces both factors and further narrows this accuracy range. However, aggregation lowers the flip rate partly by settling questions on the same wrong option. We therefore recommend reporting both factors to distinguish mitigation effects, alongside accuracy to distinguish stability from correctness.

</details>


### [159] [Can LLMs Anticipate Their Cruxes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21006)
*David Reber,James Reber,Ari Holtzman,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从语言模型的输出中提取导致决策的“关键点”（cruxes），提出三种计算成本递增的策略，并发现只有通过大量采样响应并比较推理过程的方法才能完整识别关键点，提出了用重排序器降低计算成本的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型辅助决策时，其输出文本量巨大，难以直接理解其决策依据。作者希望提取真正驱动模型推荐某一行动的“关键点”（cruxes），即模型多次采样响应之间存在分歧、且改变其取值可能改变结论的子问题。

Method: 作者将“关键点”定义为模型响应分布的性质而非单个响应的性质，并提出三种计算成本递增的策略：（1）直接让模型先验地识别决策的关键点；（2）让模型先生成一次响应，再从中提取推理中出现的关键点；（3）生成大量响应，通过比较不同采样间的推理来提取关键点。第三种方法通过廉价的reranker调用实现，计算量约为judge的1/86。

Result: 在跨领域、跨模型的广泛实证评估中，模型先验评估的关键点与事后实际识别出的重要因素几乎不相关；使用单次响应有所改善，但仍不足以识别完整的关键点集合；只有多次采样的方法才能完整识别关键点。

Conclusion: 对于高风险决策以及理解语言模型决策空间而言，多次采样的关键点提取流程是不可避免的，低成本替代方案（先验评估或单次响应）并不充分。

Abstract: To use language models to help us make decisions we would like to understand the rationale behind the language model's outputs. In principle, the language model provides its rationale as part of its text output. However, the sheer volume of text makes this hard to use in practice. In this paper, we are interested in extracting the cruxes that lead a model to recommend one action over another. We take a crux to be a sub-question that a model's rollouts disagree on, where changing its value may have altered the conclusion. A crux is therefore a property of the distribution of a model's rollouts, not of any one rollout. We consider three strategies of increasing computational cost. First, we just ask a model to identify, a priori, what the cruxes of a decision will be. Second, we have the model actually go through the process of generating a response and then extract the cruxes that arose in its reasoning. Finally, we generate many responses and extract the cruxes by comparing the reasoning across the rollouts. We make this last procedure computationally tractable with cheap calls to a reranker, an estimated 86 times less compute than a judge. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation to assess whether the computationally inexpensive options are sufficient. They are not. Across domains and models, we find that a model's a priori assessment of the cruxes has little relation to the factors that are actually identified as important a posteriori. Using a single rollout helps, but is still inadequate for identifying the full set of cruxes. Accordingly, for high-stakes decisions (and to understand the space of language model decision making) the many rollout procedure is unavoidable.

</details>


### [160] [Learning to Read Out: Unembedding Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21025)
*Matteo He,William F. Shen,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出参数轨迹交叉编码方法，跟踪语言模型预训练中读出行（readout）的演化，发现隐藏状态在自身读出行表达任务信息之前就已编码该信息，行为时间线因此混淆了"编码"与"表达"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展性研究通常以模型何时开始输出正确token来判断能力的获得，但预测须经过仍在学习中的unembedding矩阵（readout），因此输出时间线可能无法反映模型真正学到能力的时间。

Method: 提出parameter-trajectory crosscoding：为每个检查点、每个token的readout行拟合一个稀疏字典；与作用于激活的crosscoder不同，该方法仅需检查点权重。随后用从头预训练、仅读出行学习率不同的小模型做对照实验。

Result: 在Pythia预训练进度0.4–1.1%的早期窗口内，读出行发生重组，WordNet词类变得可从其行中解码；此时隐藏状态的线性探针已能解码模型输出尚未体现的任务信息。提高读出行学习率可使其更早发育、差距更快弥合，且对最终损失影响很小。

Conclusion: 在训练早期（许多发展性转变被定日之处），行为时间点可能反映的是读出行学会表达任务信息的时间，而非模型真正学会该信息的时间。

Abstract: When does a language model acquire a capability? Developmental studies usually answer this by tracking when models start producing the correct token. But that prediction passes through the unembedding matrix, or readout, which is itself still being learned during pretraining. We show that a model's hidden states can encode task information before its own readout expresses it, so timelines read from outputs conflate what the model encodes with what its readout expresses. To track the readout directly, we introduce parameter-trajectory crosscoding, which fits one sparse dictionary to every token's readout row at every checkpoint. Unlike crosscoders on activations, it needs only checkpoint weights. We use it to locate a narrow early window, 0.4-1.1% of the way through Pythia's pretraining, in which the readout reorganizes and WordNet word categories become decodable from its rows. By then, linear probes on the hidden states already decode task information that the model's own outputs do not yet show. To test whether such gaps depend on how fast the readout learns, we pretrain small models from scratch that differ only in the readout's learning rate. We find that raising it makes the readout develop earlier and the gap close sooner, with little effect on final loss. Together, these results imply that early in training, where many developmental transitions are dated, a behavioral date can reflect when the readout learns to express task information and not when the model learns it.

</details>


### [161] [ReviewPilot: A Multi-Agent System for Screening and Data Extraction in Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21042)
*Zhaoqian Xue,Shaoze Zhou,Xuexin Li,Junting Mu,Haozheng Liu,Zhongyin Wang,Yichun He,Renkai Ma,Hao Zhou,Sarah Bratt,Siyuan Ma,Chen Chen,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReviewPilot是一个对话式多智能体系统，可自动完成PRISMA流程中的文献筛选与信息抽取，并保留筛选理由与数据来源，性能优于单提示LLM和AgentSLR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的深度研究工具虽能检索和总结文献，但不记录被排除的研究及抽取值的来源，难以满足系统综述的可追溯性要求。

Method: 开发对话式多智能体系统ReviewPilot，自动化PRISMA工作流；研究者可通过对话定制标准与模式，项目记忆保存配置与修订，每个筛选决策附理由、每个抽取值附来源。

Result: 在流行病学专家评审中，跨三个LLM均优于单提示LLM与AgentSLR，筛选宏F1最高0.776，抽取F1最高0.788；在生物医学与人机交互案例中与人工标注高度一致。

Conclusion: ReviewPilot能够自动化并增强PRISMA流程，提升可追溯性与性能，且具备跨领域迁移潜力。

Abstract: Systematic literature reviews follow the PRISMA process of screening scholarly works and extracting evidence from included studies. Deep research tools based on large language models (LLMs) can search and summarize the literature but do not record which studies are excluded or where extracted values come from. We develop a conversational multi-agent system called ReviewPilot that automates the PRISMA workflow from paper screening to information extraction. Researchers can tailor criteria and schemas through dialogue, and a project memory keeps their configurations and revisions. Each screening decision comes with its reason and each extracted value with its source. On epidemiological expert reviews, ReviewPilot outperforms a single-prompt LLM and AgentSLR across three LLMs, with screening macro F1 up to 0.776 and extraction F1 up to 0.788. Case studies in biomedicine and human-computer interaction show close agreement with human annotators, suggesting that ReviewPilot can transfer beyond epidemiology. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhaoqianXue/ReviewPilot.

</details>


### [162] [PremiseCheck: Efficient Fact-Checking of Human and AI Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21046)
*Dingjie Song,Zhiling Yan,Ziqian Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出ReviewFact基准（标注评审意见中的事实性错误）和PremiseCheck框架（由LLM将评审意见改写为显式前提，再由廉价决策模型逐条核对论文），在准确率接近前沿LLM的前提下显著降低成本与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审因投稿量增长而承压，人工或AI撰写的评审意见可能对论文内容作出错误陈述（事实性错误）。现有前沿LLM虽能较好核查意见，但需为每次核查通读全文，成本高；而廉价的决策模型在处理混合多个隐含断言的整条意见时表现不佳。

Method: 提出两项贡献：（1）ReviewFact基准，在NLP、机器学习与自然科学三个领域用统一定义标注评审意见中的事实性错误，同时衡量准确率、成本与延迟；（2）PremiseCheck框架，先由LLM在不阅读论文的情况下将每条评审意见改写为显式前提，再由决策模型逐条对照论文核查各前提。

Result: 前沿LLM核查意见效果好但需通读全文；廉价决策模型在整条混合意见上差距明显。PremiseCheck在单独核查每条意见时，三个领域的准确率均与GPT-5.5相当，成本约为其一半；在NLP评审上成本约为四分之一，延迟不到一半。

Conclusion: 通过将"阅读论文"与"判断断言"解耦，PremiseCheck以更低成本与延迟达到与前沿LLM相当的评审事实性核查准确率，为缓解同行评审压力提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Peer review is under strain from the growth of submissions, and review comments written by humans or AI can misstate what a paper says. We introduce ReviewFact, a benchmark that labels such factual errors in review comments under one definition across NLP, machine learning, and the natural sciences, and that measures cost and latency alongside accuracy. A frontier LLM checks comments well but must read the whole paper for each check, while cheap decision models, which score whether a statement holds in a text, fall far short on whole comments that mix several implicit assertions. PremiseCheck is a framework that combines the two. An LLM rewrites each comment as explicit premises without reading the paper, and a decision model checks each premise against the paper. When each comment is checked on its own, PremiseCheck reaches accuracy comparable to GPT-5.5 across all three fields at about half its cost, and on NLP reviews at a quarter of its cost and less than half its latency.

</details>


### [163] [SHIP: Structured and Hierarchical Insight with Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21048)
*Kai Chen,Guisi Ni,Albert Cheu,Kassem Fawaz,Yuzheng Hu,Ryan McKenna,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出差分隐私洞察的形式化框架，将其定义为可复用表示，并推出SHIP方法——自动映射查询属性、构建分层混合分类法并发布DP合成表，用于保护隐私的文本分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本数据中提取洞察同时保护用户隐私日益重要，但现有方法产生的私有洞察形式多样、目的各异，难以评估和比较其效用。作者希望建立统一基础来评估私有洞察的效用。

Method: 将DP洞察形式化为用于回答分析查询的可复用表示；提出SHIP方法，自动将查询映射到属性，构建分层混合分类法，并发布DP合成表以供进一步分析；同时开发以QA性能和结构化保真度为主的评估协议。

Result: 实验表明，相较于现有私有洞察基线，SHIP在QA性能和结构化保真度两项指标上均表现优异，同时识别出对结构化表示仍然具有挑战性的查询类型。

Conclusion: SHIP通过可复用表示和合成表的方式，为差分隐私洞察提供了统一且有效的效用评估基础，并在多项指标上优于现有基线，但仍存在部分难以处理的查询。

Abstract: Extracting insights from textual data while protecting user privacy is increasingly important. Existing methods produce private insights in diverse forms for different analytical purposes, making their utility difficult to assess and compare. In this work, we formulate differentially private (DP) insights as reusable representations for answering analytical queries, providing a common basis for utility assessment. Answering these queries, however, requires fine-grained details and cross-attribute statistics that existing representations may omit. To capture this information, we introduce Structured and Hierarchical Insight with Privacy (SHIP), which automatically maps queries to attributes, builds a hierarchical hybrid taxonomy, and releases a DP synthetic table for further analysis. We also develop an evaluation protocol based primarily on QA performance and structured fidelity. Our experiments demonstrate SHIP's strong performance on both measures relative to existing private insight baselines, while identifying queries that remain challenging for structured representations.

</details>


### [164] [Multimodal AI Detection for Math-Heavy Scientific Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21062)
*Yixuan Huang,Danish Pruthi,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了AI生成科学内容的检测问题，发现现有检测器在处理数学密集内容时准确率大幅下降，并提出了基于LaTeX和图像的新检测方法SnapJudge，在证明和散文检测上显著优于现有最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在科学写作中的应用日益普及，需要更高透明度和对AI使用的披露。现有AI内容检测器仅依赖从PDF提取的文本，未能利用数学密集科学内容中丰富的视觉格式信息，导致检测性能下降。

Method: 从2022年前的arXiv LaTeX源中选取人类撰写的证明和散文段落，使用15个现代LLM在同一论文上下文中重新生成，得到配对的三种表示形式：LaTeX源、PDF渲染图像和PDF提取文本。发现Pangram 4在数学内容增多时准确率下降。通过微调Qwen-3.5-27B检测器弥补差距，并提出SnapJudge——基于图像的检测器，直接处理论文散文和证明的截图。

Result: Pangram 4的准确率随段落中数学内容增加而显著下降，尤其在文本评估时比LaTeX更严重。微调后的检测器可恢复性能，LaTeX输入显著优于文本。SnapJudge在0.1%假阳性率下，对前沿模型生成的证明检测率达92%，散文达93%，大幅超越Pangram 4和基于文本的检测器。

Conclusion: AI生成数学内容并非无法检测，关键是利用格式和视觉信息。LaTeX保留了更多格式信息因而检测更优，但因实际中LaTeX源很少可用，基于图像的SnapJudge成为更实用的方案，性能显著优于现有方法。数据和模型已公开。

Abstract: As large language models become increasingly capable of generating, and assisting with, scientific writing, there is a need for greater transparency and disclosure around the use of AI in research. Consequently, a veritable subfield of research and a number of commercial services aim to flag AI-generated content automatically. However, existing detectors which rely only on text extracted from PDFs fail to benefit from the rich visual information contained in the formatting of math-heavy scientific content. To study this, we select human-written proof and prose passages from pre-2022 arXiv LaTeX sources and re-generate them using 15 modern LLMs within the context of the same paper. This leaves us with coupled human and AI outputs in three different representations: the LaTeX source, a rendered image of the PDF, and the text extracted from the PDF. We find that the accuracy of the state-of-the-art Pangram 4 drops significantly as the mathematical content in a passage increases, especially when evaluated on text as opposed to LaTeX. However, this is not an inherent limitation of detecting AI-generated math: finetuning Qwen-3.5-27B based detectors on this domain recovers the gap, with LaTeX inputs performing significantly better than text since they preserve more formatting information. Since LaTeX sources are rarely available in practice, we also train SnapJudge: an image-based detector operating on screenshots of both prose and proofs from papers. At 0.1% false positive rate, SnapJudge achieves a detection rate of 92% on proofs and 93% on prose generated by frontier models, substantially outperforming both Pangram 4 and our text-based detector. We release our data, models and code at https://nlplab.cs.duke.edu/snapjudge.

</details>


### [165] [A Jury, Not an Electorate: Label-Free Reliability Estimation for Ensembles of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21085)
*Alessandro Rozza,Davide Mambelli,Steven Motta,Juri Taresch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将语言模型集成视为"陪审团"而非"选民"，通过EM从未标注的答案一致性模式中推断各专家的可靠性，决策规则等价于对专家答案分布进行对数几率加权的线性打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数集成方法为每个专家分配固定声誉（常由标签学习得到），本文质疑这种声誉能否直接从专家之间的同意/分歧模式中推断出来。

Method: 把专家建模为陪审团：每题存在潜在真答案，每位专家以未知可靠性报告答案，用EM仅从无标签的答案配置中估计可靠性；其决策规则化简为对专家答案分布的对数几率加权线性得分。

Result: 在1560题基准上，由9个最大3B参数专家组成的陪审团与使用标签拟合权重的相同融合规则表现相当，监督未带来统计显著优势；同时在全基准上优于匹配自洽性的最佳单模型和最强调的无标签替代方案。

Conclusion: 改变的不是陪审团如何投票，而是如何学会信任谁；可靠性可仅从一致性模式中无监督推断，无需标签监督。

Abstract: An ensemble of language models is a jury, not an electorate. The answers are testimony from witnesses of unknown reliability, and the question is not what the majority said but who is credible. Most ensemble methods assign each expert a fixed reputation, often learned from labels. We ask whether it can instead be inferred from the pattern of agreement and disagreement among the experts. We model the experts as a jury: each question has a latent true answer, each expert reports it with unknown reliability, and those reliabilities are estimated by Expectation Maximisation (EM) from unlabelled answer configurations alone. We show that its decision rule reduces to a log-odds weighted linear score over the experts' answer distributions. What changes is not how the jury votes, but how it learns whom to trust. On a 1560-question benchmark, the jury, with nine experts of up to 3B parameters, performs comparably to the same fusion rule with label-fitted weights, showing no statistically significant advantage from supervision. Instead, it outperforms the best single model with matched self-consistency and the strongest label-free alternative on the full benchmark.

</details>


### [166] [ToolBottleneckBench: Measuring Tool-Agent Performance under Controlled Pressures and Fixed Harness Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21098)
*Zichuan Xu,Yuyang Dong,Makoto Onizuka,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ToolBottleneckBench 基准，在固定 harness 预算与受控压力下测量工具智能体表现，发现可见工具菜单是唯一对模型产生大幅惩罚的压力，且该惩罚主要来自完成上限处的截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具智能体的成功分数同时取决于模型与运行它的 harness 预算，二者混淆导致难以判断性能差异的真正来源，因此需要一个在受控压力和固定预算下评测的基准。

Method: 构建 ToolBottleneckBench：合成企业任务保持固定，每次只变化一种压力（注册工具目录、可见工具菜单、认证工作流、返回记录编码）；用精确评分器记录每个失败回合的首个未满足义务及终止它的 harness 限制。在 21 种模型与 harness 配置上评测，并对比将完成上限提高十六倍的效果。

Result: 在三种作用于模型的压力中，只有可见菜单带来大幅惩罚，且该惩罚与完成上限的截断相关；将上限提高十六倍可在截断严重的配置中消除大部分惩罚。多数失败终止于 harness 限制，且失败记录识别所指派压力的能力并不比工具调用次数更可靠。

Conclusion: 有效的修复在于改变哪些工具到达模型或由谁发起调用，仅有两种修复（均不改善模型行为）击败所有配置切换；建议在报告压力效应时一并报告其所处的 harness 预算。基准已开源于 https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench。

Abstract: The success score of a tool agent reflects both its model and the budgets of the harness that runs it. We present ToolBottleneckBench, a benchmark that measures tool-agent performance under controlled pressures and fixed harness budgets. Synthetic enterprise tasks stay fixed while one pressure varies: the registered tool catalog, the visible tool menu, the certified workflow, or the encoding of returned records. An exact grader records the first unmet obligation of each failed episode and the harness limit, if any, that ended it. Across 21 model and harness configurations, the visible menu is the only one of the three pressures that act on the model with a large penalty, and this penalty tracks truncation at the completion cap. Raising the cap sixteenfold removes most of the penalty in the truncation-heavy configurations. Most failures end at a harness limit, and the failure record identifies the assigned pressure no more reliably than a count of tool calls. The repairs that help change which tools reach the model or who issues the calls, and only two repairs, neither improving model behavior, beat every configuration switch. We recommend reporting pressure effects with the harness budgets under which they were measured. We release the benchmark at https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench.

</details>


### [167] [Compression Does Not End at Selection: Post-Selection Positional Geometry for Sparse KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21126)
*Wanyang Hong,Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoPERelay：在不重新选择内容的前提下，对稀疏KV缓存中保留token的RoPE位置坐标进行紧凑重排，提升长上下文问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏KV压缩保留部分历史状态，但保留了完整历史中的原始位置坐标。在RoPE下，被逐出token留下的位置空缺会持续干扰query-key匹配，限制被保留证据的利用。因此需要研究"内容选择之后"的位置几何优化维度。

Method: 将位置组织视为独立优化维度，提出RoPERelay：为缓存键分配紧凑且保持顺序的RoPE坐标，通过逆旋转与再旋转迁移已缓存键的相位，并同步当前query与后续生成。无需重新选择、历史重预填或额外缓存预算，直接复用现有K/V。

Result: 在2个模型、6种稀疏选择器、3类长期记忆任务的36组对比中，32组答案质量提升。Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct配合BM25在LongMemEval-S上准确率从23.6%升至35.4%；完整请求时间开销约1%-3%。

Conclusion: 位置组织是稀疏KV缓存内容选择之后一个独立且有效的优化维度。

Abstract: Sparse KV compression reduces long-context inference costs by retaining selected historical states, yet these states typically keep their coordinates from the full history. Under Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), gaps left by evicted tokens continue to affect query-key matching, potentially limiting the use of retained evidence. We study post-selection positional geometry on a fixed selector-produced cache, examining how positional organization affects the use of retained information and which coordinate and continuation rules improve answer quality. To reorganize this geometry, we propose RoPERelay, which assigns compact, order-preserving RoPE coordinates, relocates cached key phases through inverse rotation and re-rotation, and synchronizes the current query and subsequent generation. It reuses existing K/V without renewed selection, historical re-prefill, or additional cache budget. Across 36 comparisons spanning two models, six sparse selectors, and three long-term memory tasks, RoPERelay improves answer quality in 32 cases. For Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with BM25 on LongMemEval-S, accuracy increases from 23.6% to 35.4%. Measured complete-request time overhead is approximately 1%-3% in the evaluated configurations. These findings establish positional organization as a distinct optimization dimension for sparse KV caches after content selection.

</details>


### [168] [When Judges Agree and Are Wrong: Consistency-Accuracy Dissociation as a Blind Spot for Label-Free Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21128)
*Xiang Zhang,Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文直接检验了"一致性可替代正确性"这一隐含假设，发现在四个金融问答数据集上，60%的错误答案位于高一致性区域，其中77.7%表现为完全答案塌缩，即所有扰动变体下给出相同的错误答案，且这种高一致性/错误象限无法被任何基于一致性的评估信号检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签评估方法（如自洽性过滤、多数投票聚合、基于一致性的可靠性打分）隐含假设：在缺少真实标签时，一致性可以作为正确性的代理。然而这一假设从未被直接、逐样本地检验。作者希望验证该假设是否成立，尤其是在高风险领域。

Method: 使用四个金融问答数据集（含选择题与自由形式题）、十个一致性维度、两个前沿模型家族，在单个样本层面（而非聚合层面）测量一致性与准确率的联合分布，并检验该现象对一致性阈值t的稳健性。

Result: 60%的错误答案落在高一致性区域（一致性≥0.8）；其中77.7%出现完全答案塌缩（所有扰动变体下答案完全相同且错误）。该现象在t=0.50至t=1.00范围内持续存在，跨模型家族共享，并集中在特定扰动维度与领域的交叉处。

Conclusion: 基于一致性/一致同意的高一致性信号无法检测此类错误，模型在错误时反而显得最可靠。作者绘制了该象限的分布图，分析了其稳定性，并讨论了对使用一致性信号替代人工标签的评估流程的启示。

Abstract: Label-free evaluation methods, such as self-consistency filtering, majority-vote aggregation, and agreement-based reliability scoring, rely on an implicit assumption that consistency can serve as a proxy for correctness when ground-truth labels are unavailable or expensive to obtain. In this paper, we test this assumption directly. Using four financial QA datasets (multiple-choice and free-form), ten consistency dimensions, and two frontier model families, we measure the joint distribution of consistency and accuracy at the level of individual examples rather than in aggregate. We find that 60% of all incorrect answers fall in the high-consistency region (consistency $\geq$ 0.8), and that 77.7% of these show complete answer collapse, i.e. identical wrong answers across all perturbation variants. This high-consistency/incorrect quadrant cannot be detected by any evaluation signal that is built on agreement alone, as the model appears maximally reliable while being wrong. We show that the phenomenon is robust to the choice of consistency threshold t (it persists from t=0.50 to t=1.00), is shared across model families, and is concentrated in specific intersections of perturbation dimension and domain. We map where this quadrant occurs, analyze its stability properties, and discuss the implications for evaluation pipelines that use agreement-based signals in place of human labels.

</details>


### [169] [CoSTA: Compressed Speech Token Adaptation for Efficient Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21143)
*Yingzhuo Deng,Chong Li,Bingxin Liu,Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Can Xie,Siyao Song,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoSTA框架，在不替换原语音分词器和解码器的前提下，将生成与合成词元空间解耦，从而加速语音对话模型的生成与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音对话模型通常让生成与合成共享同一语音词元空间，导致自回归序列过长、推理成本高；而低速率分词器虽能缩短序列，却带来压缩与质量的权衡，且替换成本高，仍未打破生成与合成的耦合。

Method: CoSTA是一个保持兼容性的后处理框架：通过均值初始化的词表扩展、发音适应和对话适应，用压缩表示扩展生成词元空间，再在合成前确定性地扩展回原始语音词元空间，全程保留原语音分词器与解码器。

Result: 在双语语音对话基准上，CoSTA实现约1.7倍的语音词元生成加速，降低全响应延迟并提升吞吐量，同时保持响应质量；分析显示在有限预算下交互更持久，并在压缩、解码效率与质量之间形成实用权衡。实验还表明其具备跨架构迁移能力，且能与低速率分词互补。

Conclusion: CoSTA通过解耦生成与合成词元空间，在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升语音对话模型的生成效率，为低延迟、高吞吐的语音对话提供了一种兼容性强且可与低速率分词协同的实用方案。

Abstract: Spoken Dialogue Models (SDMs) typically share a speech-token space for generation and synthesis, leading to long autoregressive sequences and costly inference. Lower-rate speech tokenizers shorten sequences but may introduce compression-quality trade-offs and costly replacement without breaking this coupling. To address this limitation, we propose CoSTA, a compatibility-preserving post-hoc framework that decouples generation and synthesis token spaces while retaining the original speech tokenizer and decoder. CoSTA extends the generation token space with compressed representations via mean-initialized vocabulary extension, pronunciation adaptation, and dialogue adaptation, followed by deterministic expansion back to the original speech-token space before synthesis. On bilingual speech-dialogue benchmarks, CoSTA achieves approximately $1.7\times$ expanded speech-token generation speed, lower full-response latency, and higher throughput while preserving response quality. Analyses show more sustained interaction under limited budgets and a practical trade-off among compression, decoding efficiency, and quality. Further experiments demonstrate cross-architecture transfer and complementarity to low-rate tokenization.

</details>


### [170] [From Observation to Intervention: Distilling Self-Generated In-Context Demonstrations via Executable Causal Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21149)
*Minming Ye,Min Liu,Haoang Chi,Ciheng Wu,Naiyang Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有自生成ICL方法仅靠"输出正确"筛选示例，受观察等价性困扰，无法区分真实因果理由与虚假事后理由；本文提出ECI2D，用可执行程序+因果干预检验"迁移不变性"来筛选示例，在数学、逻辑、常识推理上以更少示例持续优于观察式ICL，分布偏移时提升超7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自生成ICL让LLM自行合成few-shot示例，常优于人工提示。但现有筛选方法基于"观察"：只要理由能导出正确输出就视为高质量。作者指出这种筛选存在观察等价性问题——真实的因果理由与虚假的事后理由会产生相同输出，仅从观察上统计不可区分，因此无法保证示例质量。

Method: 受独立因果机制启发，作者提出忠实理由应满足"迁移不变性"：因果理由在逻辑相似的样本上应保持有效。据此实例化ECI2D（Executable Causal Intervention for Demonstrations Distillation）：将语言理由抽象为可执行程序，对逻辑相似样本施加因果干预（do-operator），只保留体现迁移不变性的示例。

Result: 在数学、逻辑与常识推理基准上的实验表明，ECI2D一致优于基于观察的ICL，同时保留的示例数量远少；当测试分布发生偏移时，性能提升超过7个点。

Conclusion: 基于观察的示例筛选受观察等价性限制，无法区分因果与虚假理由；改用因果干预下的迁移不变性作为筛选准则，可在更少示例下获得更稳健、更可迁移的自生成ICL示例，尤其在分布偏移场景收益显著。

Abstract: Self-generated In-Context Learning (ICL) enables LLMs to synthesize their own few-shot demonstrations, often outperforming manual prompts. However, existing methods filter demonstrations by observation, assuming a rationale is high-quality if it leads to the correct output. We show such filtering suffers from \textit{observational equivalence}, whereby both faithful causal rationales and spurious post-hoc rationales can yield same outputs and are statistically indistinguishable from observation alone. Inspired by independent causal mechanisms, we argue that faithful rationales imply \textit{transfer invariance}, in which causal rationales should remain effective across logically similar samples. We instantiate this criterion as \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{I}ntervention for \textbf{D}emonstrations \textbf{D}istillation (ECI2D), which abstracts language rationales into executable programs and applies the causal interventions ($do$-operator) on logically similar samples, retaining only those demonstrations that imply transfer invariance. Experiments on math, logical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ECI2D consistently outperforms observation-based ICL while retaining far fewer demonstrations, with gains exceeding 7 points as the test distribution shifts.

</details>


### [171] [Stop Training Against Soft Users: Mentalized Adversarial Self-Play for Proactive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21156)
*Zanwu Liu,Dan Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Yongbo Mao,Xiaoyang Guo,Yonglin Wu,Peiyue Jiang,Yangyang Wei,Zecheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MASP框架，将主动对话建模为系统与理性抵抗用户之间的部分可观测博弈，通过BDI潜在状态建模用户心理、分离奖励侧教师心智化与策略侧学生心智化，并交替进行自博弈更新，把对理性抵抗的鲁棒性作为训练目标。实验表明相比SFT和Planner-only方法，MASP在成功率与理性约束成功率上显著提升，减少平均轮次，对困难用户表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动对话规划器多依赖固定或过度顺从的模拟器进行训练与评估，导致高模拟成功率可能源于用户的"软性偏置"而非真正的对话鲁棒性。作者希望将"对理性抵抗的鲁棒性"从单纯的评估条件转变为可训练的目标。

Method: 提出MASP（Mentalized Adversarial Self Play）框架：将主动对话视为系统与理性抵抗用户之间的部分可观测博弈；用基于BDI的潜在状态表示用户演化的心理状态；将奖励侧的教师心智化与策略侧的学生心智化分离；交替更新系统与用户策略进行自博弈训练。

Result: 在多个主动对话基准上，与常用的SFT和Planner-only方法相比，MASP显著提升成功率（SR）与理性约束成功率（RCSR），降低平均轮次（AT），并且在让任务目标更难实现的"困难用户"面前表现更好。

Conclusion: MASP通过对抗自博弈与心智化机制，将鲁棒性融入训练过程，能够有效提升主动对话智能体应对理性抵抗用户的能力，优于现有SFT与仅规划器方案。

Abstract: Proactive dialogue agents must guide multi-turn interactions toward task goals while adapting to users whose intentions and attitudes evolve. Existing planners improve system side action selection through search, learned policies, or user aware planning, but are often trained and evaluated against fixed or overly compliant simulators; as a result, high simulated success can reflect soft user bias rather than robust dialogue ability. We introduce MASP, a Mentalized Adversarial Self Play framework that treats proactive dialogue as a partially observable game between a system and a rationally resisting user. MASP represents the user's evolving mental state with a BDI based latent state, separates reward side teacher mentalization from policy side student mentalization, and alternates self play updates for the system and user policies. This turns robustness to rational resistance into a training objective rather than only an evaluation condition. Across diverse proactive dialogue benchmarks, compared with the widely used SFT and Planner-only approaches, MASP substantially improves success rate (SR) and rationality constrained success (RCSR), reduces average turns (AT), and performs better against hard users who make the task goal more difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [172] [Automatic Sentence Completion Using Bengali Audio Speech with a Transformer-Based Early Speech Recognition System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21164)
*Md Asif Akram,Arif Faisal,Sakir Hossain Faruque,Sharun Akter Khushbu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个集成系统，通过孟加拉语语音和文本分析检测痴呆迹象，并在对话中为患者提供句子自动补全支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆严重损害沟通能力，患者难以表达想法或完成句子，家属和照护者难以理解其需求，且孟加拉语等低资源语言的痴呆分析研究存在空白。

Method: 采集痴呆与非痴呆人群的录音，提取声学特征；使用基于Transformer的模型将语音转写为文本进行语言分析。分类任务中评估多种机器学习和深度学习模型，采用声学与文本表示；句子补全任务则构建基于Bi-LSTM网络并结合注意力机制的序列学习模型。

Result: 在分类任务中，LSTM模型架构在建模语音和语言数据的上下文模式方面展现出较强能力；Bi-LSTM加注意力机制模型用于逐步补全对话中部分表达的孟加拉语句子。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了低资源语言痴呆分析的空白，并实现了面向认知障碍的句子自动补全框架。

Abstract: Dementia severely impairs communication abilities and turns everyday conversations into challenging experiences. Individuals affected by dementia frequently struggle to express their thoughts or complete sentences, making it difficult for family members and caregivers to understand their needs. This study proposes a novel approach to assist dementia patients by developing an integrated system that detects signs of dementia through Bangla speech and text analysis and provides sentence auto-completion support during conversations. To achieve this, recorded voices from individuals with and without dementia were analysed, and key acoustic features were extracted to capture vocal patterns associated with cognitive decline. A transformer-based model was employed to transcribe voice signals into text for linguistic analysis. In the classification task, several Machine Learning (ML) and Deep Learning (DL) models were evaluated using both acoustic and textual representations, and the LSTM model architectures demonstrated strong capabilities for modelling contextual patterns in speech and language data. For the sentence auto-completion task, a sequential learning model based on a Bidirectional LSTM (Bi-LSTM) network with an attention mechanism was developed to sequentially complete partially expressed Bangla sentences during conversation. This research bridges the gap in dementia analysis in an underrepresented language and implements a sentence auto-completion framework for cognitive disorders.

</details>


### [173] [From Long Context to Selective Memory:A Survey of Memory-Centric Compression for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21165)
*Chaogui Gou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述将长上下文压缩重构为推理期资源分配的内存管理问题，提出活跃/非活跃内存划分与信息生命周期框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文能力对LLM处理复杂任务愈发关键，但扩大上下文窗口无法消除推理期的计算、存储与信息选择压力。现有提示压缩、KV缓存管理、高效服务、检索增强与智能体记忆研究彼此割裂，存储与计算间的信息流动缺乏统一理解。

Method: 将长上下文压缩重构为内存管理问题：区分直接参与计算的活跃内存与计算路径之外但可存储、检索、更新、遗忘的非活跃内存；把卸载KV缓存、持久前缀缓存、预计算KV状态刻画为激活路径上的中间状态；将写入、卸载、召回、检索注入、物化、驱逐、整合、遗忘形式化为信息生命周期中的状态转移；并提出统一核算这些操作的预算化成本模型。

Result: 该框架统一了短期上下文压缩、长期记忆管理及二者之间的转换，并表明核心挑战不只是缩减信息量，而是决定哪些信息、在何时、以何种形式参与高昂的推理。

Conclusion: 总结评估维度、基准缺口与开放挑战，主张未来研究从静态、逐点压缩转向动态、可治理、生命周期感知的内存管理。

Abstract: Long-context capability has become essential for large language models (LLMs) to handle complex tasks, yet continuously expanding the context window does not remove the computational, storage, and information-selection pressures incurred at inference time. Existing work on prompt compression, KV-cache management, efficient serving, retrieval augmentation, and agent memory is usually discussed in isolation, leaving the flow of information between storage and computation insufficiently understood. This survey reframes long-context compression as a memory-management problem for allocating inference-time resources. We distinguish compute-active memory, which directly participates in model computation, from compute-inactive memory, which lies outside the computation path but can be stored, retrieved, updated, or forgotten, and we characterize offloaded KV caches, persistent prefix caches, and precomputed KV states as intermediate states on the activation path. On this basis, we formulate writing, offloading, recall, retrieval-and-injection, materialization, eviction, consolidation, and forgetting as state transitions within an information lifecycle, and introduce a budgeted cost model that places these operations in a single cost account. The resulting framework unifies short-term context compression, long-term memory management, and the transitions between them, and shows that the central challenge is not merely reducing information volume but deciding which information should participate in costly inference, when, and in what form. We further summarize evaluation dimensions, benchmark gaps, and open challenges, and argue that future research should move from static, pointwise compression toward dynamic, governable, lifecycle-aware memory management.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Retrieval Failure: Understanding Why LLMs Miss Evidence in Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21184)
*Jiaqi Wei,Wenxuan Huang,Chengdong Xu,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的ExecutionRAG，用"要求寄存器"记录未解决的子问题及其支撑证据，以区分RAG多跳检索失败中"证据不可达"与"搜索漂移"两种成因；在固定检索器、语料和生成模型条件下，恢复到的每个金标准段落都证明原检索器本可获取该段落。在1,000道MuSiQue问题上，含未检索必需证据的错误答案数量显著下降，但总错误未成比例减少——瓶颈从"引导搜索"转向"验证证据充分性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳RAG中，当错误答案缺少必需段落时，无法区分是段落对检索器不可达，还是智能体未能发出可检索到它的查询。标准检索评测将这两种原因混为一谈。作者希望量化其中有多少表面上的检索失败实际源于"搜索漂移"——即搜索已不再追踪某个未回答的子问题。

Method: 在固定检索器、语料库与生成模型的前提下，提出免训练的ExecutionRAG。它引入一个"要求寄存器"（requirement register），记录尚未解决的开子问题及其支撑证据。该寄存器只改变搜索引导方式，不改变证据的可访问性，因此每当它找回到一个金标准段落，就证明该段落原本对原检索器是可获取的。

Result: 在1,000道MuSiQue问题上：使用all-MiniLM-L6-v2时，存在未检索到必需证据的错误答案从382降至234（占系统错误答案的69%→44%）；使用Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B时从383降至274（66%→48%）。但证据恢复并未带来总错误的成比例下降——随着缺证据型失败减少，过早作答和弃答反而增加。

Conclusion: 核心发现是剩余瓶颈发生了转移：从"引导搜索指向必需证据"转向"验证已检索证据是否充分"。这表明多跳RAG的改进重点应从检索引导转向证据充分性的验证。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires an agent to retrieve evidence for a sequence of dependent sub-questions. When a wrong answer lacks a required passage, the passage may be inaccessible to the retriever, or the agent may have failed to issue a query that would retrieve it. Standard retrieval evaluations conflate these two causes. We study how much apparent retrieval failure is instead caused by search drift: the search no longer tracks an unanswered sub-question. Holding the retriever, corpus, and generation model fixed, we introduce ExecutionRAG, a training-free requirement register that records open sub-questions and their supporting evidence. The register changes search guidance but not evidence access, so each gold passage it recovers certifies that the passage was reachable by the original retriever. On 1,000 MuSiQue questions, the number of wrong answers with unretrieved required evidence falls from 382 to 234 with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 and from 383 to 274 with Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B. These counts correspond to 69% to 44% and 66% to 48% of each system's wrong answers, respectively. Crucially, evidence recovery does not produce a proportional reduction in total error: premature resolutions and abstentions increase as missing-evidence failures decrease. The central finding is therefore a shift in the remaining bottleneck, from guiding search toward required evidence to verifying that the retrieved evidence is sufficient.

</details>


### [175] [KBS: A Korean Benchmark Suite for Foundation Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21227)
*Yeonkyoung So,Jongmin Kim,Sungmok Jung,Gyuseong Lee,Sangho Kim,Jongyeon Park,Joonhak Lee,Seho Pyo,Gyeongje Cho,Seorin Kim,Jisoo Kim,Suyoung Park,Hyunji M. Park,Yelim Ahn,Yeongho Seo,Jaejin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了韩语基准测试套件KBS，涵盖6项能力、9,396个测试项，并通过对多类模型的评测揭示韩语评测结果高度依赖任务构建、评分规则、提示格式与模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多依赖翻译方式适配韩语，难以充分反映韩语特性与真实能力评估需求，因此需要一个结合审查、本地化、直接构建与重新设计的韩语基准套件。

Method: 构建KBS套件，包含Ko-ARC、Ko-GSM8K、Ko-EQ-Bench、Ko-WinoGrande、Ko-LAMBADA、Ko-IFEval六项任务；结合审查、本地化、直接韩语构建与面向韩语的重设计，避免纯翻译式适配；在考虑模型访问方式、提示格式与评分差异的协议下评测韩语开发模型、多语种开源模型和闭源API模型。

Result: 韩语基准分数取决于任务构建方式、评分规则、提示设置和模型后训练情况。

Conclusion: 韩语评测应同时报告最终分数与获得该分数所使用的具体设置，以保证评测的可比性与透明度。

Abstract: We present a Korean Benchmark Suite (KBS) comprising Ko-ARC, Ko-GSM8K, Ko-EQ-Bench, Ko-WinoGrande, Ko-LAMBADA, and Ko-IFEval, covering six capabilities across 9,396 items. The suite combines review, localization, direct Korean construction, and redesign for Korean, avoiding a translation-only approach to benchmark adaptation. We evaluate Korean-developed models, multilingual open models, and closed API models under protocols that account for model access, prompt format, and scoring differences. Results show that Korean benchmark scores depend on task construction, scoring rules, prompting, and model post-training. These findings suggest that Korean evaluation should report both final scores and the settings used to obtain them.

</details>


### [176] [What Should We Remove? Evaluating the Reliability and Costs of RLVR Data Decontamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21229)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文审计了16个开放RLVR数学语料库（1,673,514条训练数据）与6个基准（1,949题）的污染检测策略，评估8种匹配策略的可靠性与数据损失成本，发现语义阈值策略误删率高，而基于判官的策略在精确率-召回率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数学语料库通常会进行基准重叠筛查，但清理策略的可靠性和数据损失成本尚未被充分研究。现有工作多关注重叠数量，而忽视了清理本身的精度与代价。

Method: 构建包含1,673,514条训练数据与1,949道基准题的审计集；基于594对独立双盲复核的训练-基准配对，评估8种基于精确、词汇和语义匹配的策略；使用校准集与留出集（基准题和训练样本族不重叠）；对80个抽样训练项的83个候选匹配进行人工复核，测量条目级误删。

Result: 在余弦阈值0.80下，59个抽样删除中有52个（88%）被确认为误删；预注册网格中的语义阈值（0.80-0.98）均未达到90%保守精确率目标；现有判官裁决策略在留出集上达到98.1%已解决精确率和98.2%候选帧召回率，6个抽样删除中零确认误删，1例未解决。

Conclusion: 在所评估的候选帧内，判官裁决策略比语义阈值方案具有更优的精确率-召回率平衡；召回率无法衡量候选帧之外的污染。作者发布复核标签、各策略决策和覆盖核算，以支持评估清理的可靠性与成本，而非仅关注重叠计数。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) math corpora are routinely screened for benchmark overlap, but the reliability and data-loss costs of cleaning policies remain underexamined. We audit sixteen open RLVR math corpora comprising 1,673,514 corpus-specific training items against six benchmarks containing 1,949 questions. We evaluate eight policies based on exact, lexical, and semantic matching using 594 independently and blindly dual-reviewed training-benchmark pairs. Calibration and held-out sets have disjoint benchmark questions and training-example families. A separate review of all 83 candidate matches for 80 sampled training items measures item-level false removal. At cosine threshold 0.80, 52 of 59 sampled removals (88%) were confirmed false removals; this is not a demonstrated population rate.
