No semantic threshold in our preregistered grid (0.80-0.98) meets the 90% conservative-precision target on calibration. The existing judge-adjudicated policy achieves 98.1% resolved precision and 98.2% candidate-frame recall on held-out corpora. It has zero confirmed false removals among six sampled removals, with one unresolved case; this does not establish zero risk. Within the evaluated candidate frame, this policy offers a more favorable precision-recall balance than the semantic-threshold alternatives tested. Recall does not measure contamination outside the candidate frame. We release reviewed labels, per-policy decisions, and coverage accounting to support evaluation of cleaning reliability and cost rather than overlap counts alone.

</details>


### [177] [System1-Arena: Which Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21244)
*Tianyu Chen,Xinyang Liu,Ruyi An,Zhendong Wang,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出System1-Arena基准，比较System-1模型与生成式推理模型在离散决策任务上的表现，发现大型System-1模型在非智能体任务上可媲美快速闭源LLM，但闭环环境下仍以大型开源LLM表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常需做出离散决策（如标记钓鱼、转交人工），System-1模型单次前向即输出概率，System-2方法则生成推理步骤。哪些决策可由System-1处理、哪些需生成式推理尚不明确，因此需要统一基准来系统比较。

Method: 构建System1-Arena基准，包含105个任务、来自268个公开来源的9.2万问题，分为文本分类与知识、应用决策、评估与控制、智能体与行动四组，并含138个闭环环境（网络购物、SWE-bench编程、机器人、游戏、驾驶等），实时模式下世界随决策演变。评估30个模型：7个闭源LLM、13个以答案token读取的开源LLM、10个System-1模型，统一提供相同决策请求并为其概率打分。

Result: 最大的System-1模型在非智能体准确率上匹配或超过快速闭源LLM，且概率校准更好，延迟仅为其四分之一。推理LLM准确率最高，但成本约为快速LLM的70倍。闭环环境中，大型开源LLM成功率最高（达32.9%），小型System-1模型很少成功。

Conclusion: System-1模型在非智能体离散决策上具备高效且校准良好的优势，可替代快速闭源LLM；但在闭环智能体任务中，大型开源LLM表现更佳，小型System-1模型能力有限，说明生成式推理在复杂交互决策中仍不可替代。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often make discrete decisions, such as flagging phishing or escalating to a human. System-1 models, such as Jev and the OpenAI Decisions API, return probabilities over options in a single forward pass, while System-2 approaches generate reasoning steps. Which decisions can System-1 models handle, and which require generative reasoning? We introduce System1-Arena, a benchmark that gives all models the same decision requests and scores their probabilities. It contains 92k questions across 105 tasks from 268 public sources, in four groups: text classification and knowledge, applied decisions, evaluation and control, and agent and action. It also includes 138 closed-loop environments, from web shopping and SWE-bench coding control to robotics, games, and driving; in real-time mode, the world evolves while a model decides. We evaluate 30 models: 7 closed LLMs, 13 open LLMs read at the answer token, and 10 System-1 models. The largest System-1 models match or exceed fast closed LLMs in non-agentic accuracy, with better-calibrated probabilities at one-quarter of the latency. A reasoning LLM is the most accurate, at about 70x the cost of a fast LLM. In closed loops, large open LLMs succeed most often (up to 32.9%); small System-1 models rarely succeed.

</details>


### [178] [FGSS-BO: Feedback-Guided Hypothesis Management for LLM-Based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21246)
*Hao Chen,Ziyang Ling,Ronald X. Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出反馈引导搜索状态（FGSS），一个结构化假设管理接口，将语言引导与观测证据、可执行参数干预和比较支持更新相连接，并在LLAMBO基础上实例化为FGSS-BO，在部分任务上取得优于基线的最佳值和搜索AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的贝叶斯优化虽利用历史观测指导搜索，但所提出的搜索方向及其修正过程往往保持隐含，缺乏结构化、可检验的假设管理机制，难以将语言引导与观测证据和可执行干预对接。

Method: 提出FGSS结构化假设管理接口：由LLM反思器提出并修正假设，确定性管理者验证假设并维护其身份和测试记录，演化状态指导候选构造与优先级。在LLAMBO上实例化为FGSS-BO，保留其代理模型与默认期望改进选择，并采用有界测试策略。

Result: 在两个回顾性协议/媒体任务（使用最近邻响应预言机）上，FGSS-BO的样本均值最终最优值和搜索AUC均高于匹配的LLAMBO基线及所评估的非LLM基线；散文式反思和仅EI对照显示混合的组件效应；六个直接训练超参数优化任务显示任务相关的迁移效果，在diabetes SVM上最强；精确网格和反应对照考察了其他设置。

Conclusion: FGSS使假设、其执行过程以及跨轮次修正可被检查，同时在选定设置下支持搜索增益，为语言引导的贝叶斯优化提供了结构化和可解释的搜索状态管理方式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-guided Bayesian optimization (BO) uses past observations to guide search, but proposed directions and their revision can remain implicit. We propose Feedback-Guided Search State (FGSS), a structured hypothesis-management interface connecting language-based guidance to observed evidence, executable parameter interventions, and comparative support updates. An LLM reflector proposes and revises hypotheses; a deterministic curator validates them and maintains identities and test records. The evolving state guides candidate construction and priorities. Instantiated on LLAMBO, FGSS-BO retains its surrogate and default expected improvement selection, with a bounded testing policy. On two retrospective protocol/media tasks with nearest-neighbor response oracles, it attains higher sample mean final best and search AUC than the matched LLAMBO baseline and evaluated non-LLM baselines. Prose-reflection and EI-only controls show mixed component effects. Six direct-training hyperparameter-optimization tasks show task-dependent transfer, strongest on diabetes SVM; exact-grid and reaction controls examine other settings. FGSS exposes hypotheses, their execution, and cross-round revisions for inspection while supporting search gains in selected settings.

</details>


### [179] [LLM-Based Weak Annotation for Cybersecurity Tweet Classification: A Teacher-Student Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21254)
*Ferry Afit Kurniawan,Kazushi Okamoto,Koki Karube,Kei Harada,Atsushi Shibata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Teacher-Student弱监督框架，用LLM作为教师为未标注网络安全推文生成弱标签，从而训练领域专用PLM，无需大规模人工标注。实验表明仅用LLM标签训练的PLM F1均超0.92，与全人工监督模型相差不超过3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体（如X）上的网络安全讨论增长迅速，成为重要CTI来源，但内容量远超专家可审核标注的能力，标注数据成为监督学习的主要瓶颈；LLM作为弱标注器在其他领域已有潜力，但在网络安全推文分类及下游领域PLM训练中的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 提出Teacher-Student弱监督框架：选取一个LLM作为Teacher，为未标注的网络安全推文生成弱标签，再以此训练网络安全领域专用的预训练语言模型（PLM），避免大规模人工标注。

Result: 仅使用LLM生成标签训练的PLM在所有评估架构上F1分数均持续高于0.92，与完全人工监督模型相比差距在3个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的弱标注在专家标注资源有限时，是构建网络安全专用训练数据的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) relies on the timely identification of emerging threats, enabling organizations and security analysts to support situational awareness and decision-making. Social media platforms such as X (formerly Twitter) have become valuable CTI sources because vulnerabilities and attack activities are often discussed there before appearing in formal reports. However, the rapid growth of cybersecurity discussions on social media has created more content than domain experts can realistically review and annotate, making labeled data a major bottleneck for developing supervised learning approaches. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as weak annotators in other domains, their effectiveness for cybersecurity tweet classification and downstream training of domain-specific pre-trained language models (PLMs) remains underexplored. To address this challenge, we propose a Teacher-Student weak supervision framework in which a selected LLM serves as a Teacher to generate weak labels for unlabeled cybersecurity tweets, enabling the training of cybersecurity-specific PLMs without large-scale manual annotation. Experimental results show that PLMs trained solely on LLM-generated labels consistently achieve F1 scores above 0.92, remaining within 3 percentage points of fully human-supervised models across all evaluated architectures. These findings suggest that LLM-based weak annotation offers a practical alternative for constructing cybersecurity-specific training data when expert annotation resources are limited.

</details>


### [180] [TRACER: Trajectory-Aware Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21267)
*Tanish Kher,Sarvesh DK,Abedelkadir Asi,Sparsha Chennarayapatna Sundaresh,Dezhi Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACER 是一种检索式前端，通过 trie 索引已观察到的工具调用序列，并用学习到的经验与上下文先验混合来排序后续工具，从而在仅暴露少量工具的情况下提升工具选择准确率并大幅降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体需要从工具注册表中选择工具，但暴露整个注册表会带来高昂的 token 成本，且随注册表规模增长会损害选择效果。因此需要一种能在不暴露全部工具的前提下提升选择效率与准确率的方法。

Method: TRACER 作为一种检索前端，将观察到的工具调用序列索引到 trie 中，并使用学习到的经验先验与上下文先验的混合来对后续候选工具进行排序，每次仅暴露少量工具（如 128 个中的 5 个）。

Result: 在混合零售与电信的 τ² 环境中，TRACER 将 GPT-5.4 的工具选择准确率提升 4.7 个百分点，同时将每步输入 token 减少 80%。在自主整轮执行中，它以每步少 61% 的输入 token 达到与完整注册表选择相当的任务成功率。在六个环境和四个模型上，TRACER 在 24 组模型-环境组合中的 21 组优于完整注册表选择，平均提升 6 至 10 个百分点，三个例外最多落后 2.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: TRACER 能有效降低 token 成本并提升工具选择准确率，且在不同环境与模型上具有较好泛化性；作者还分析了两种模式下的失败案例，以指明未来研究方向。

Abstract: Modern agents select tools from registries, but exposing entire registries incurs high token costs and can impair selection as they grow. We present TRACER, a retrieval front-end that indexes observed tool-call sequences in a trie and ranks continuations using a learned blend of empirical and contextual priors. On a mixed retail-and-telecom $τ^2$ environment, TRACER improves GPT-5.4's tool-selection accuracy by 4.7 points while reducing input tokens per step by 80%, exposing just 5 of 128 tools. In autonomous full-turn execution, it matches GPT-5.4's full-registry task success with 61% fewer input tokens per step. Across six environments and four models, TRACER improves tool-selection accuracy over full-registry selection in 21 of 24 model-environment pairs, with average gains of 6 to 10 points; the three exceptions trail by at most 2.2. We analyze failures in both regimes to identify directions for future work.

</details>


### [181] [Making every stage of medical device document assessment with large language models deterministic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21300)
*Ryosuke Suzuki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究用大语言模型为医疗器械合规评估自动生成初步判定（如“满足/不满足”、理由与引证）时的可复现性问题。实验显示14种商业部署在同一请求下均产生差异，12种出现标签变化；通过构建端到端确定性流水线，在血糖仪与输液泵文档上实现了跨重跑、中断、编辑、同型号GPU及多日的一致性输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械合规需专家判断文件记录是否满足法规要求并留痕供他人核验。为每个评估项用大语言模型生成初步评估可提升效率，但若模型输出不可复现，则难以作为审计与变更控制的可靠依据。作者因此关注其确定性。

Method: 对十四个商业部署（三家供应商七种模型、六条调用路径）重复发送同一请求，并使用各接口接受的确定性采样设置；尝试减少推理、引入检索段落提示。另构建覆盖文本抽取与OCR、分块、嵌入、索引、检索、推理的端到端确定性流水线，检验各阶段输出是否字节级一致。

Result: 每个商业部署至少对一个请求返回不同评估，十二个部署标签发生变化；减少推理未降低变异，检索段落提示仅对最小模型消除变异；同一开放权重模型因服务方式不同而可复现或不可复现。自建流水线在血糖仪与输液泵文档上各阶段跨重跑、中断、编辑、同型号GPU和多日输出字节一致；但OCR、嵌入与近似检索在服务未固定不变时会变异，输出也随GPU型号与推理引擎调度设置变化。

Conclusion: 端到端确定性可以实现，并为审计和变更控制提供可测试的基础；GPU型号与推理引擎调度设置应纳入记录配置。商业部署目前普遍不可复现，不能直接作为合规判定依据。

Abstract: To show that a medical device complies with regulation, experts judge whether documents and records meet each requirement and record the judgment for others to verify. For each assessment item that experts define, large language models could prepare a preliminary assessment: a label such as Satisfied or Not satisfied, a reason and quoted evidence. We sent fourteen commercial deployments of such models (seven models from three providers, through six routes) the same request repeatedly, with the deterministic sampling settings each accepted; every deployment returned differing assessments for at least one request, and in twelve the label changed. Reducing reasoning where the interface allows it did not reduce the variation, and prompts of retrieved passages removed it only for the smallest models. The same open-weight model was reproducible or not depending only on how it was served. A pipeline we built to be deterministic at every stage -- text extraction and optical character recognition (OCR), chunking, embedding, indexing, retrieval and inference -- returned byte-identical output at each stage across reruns, interruptions, edits, machines of one graphics processing unit (GPU) model and days, for blood glucose meter and infusion pump documents. OCR, embedding and approximate retrieval varied when their own serving was not made invariant, and outputs changed with the GPU model and with the inference engine's scheduling settings, which therefore belong in the recorded configuration. End-to-end determinism is achievable and gives a testable basis for audit and change control.

</details>


### [182] [VALCON: Benchmarking and Enhancing Value Consistency in Large Language Models via Dual-Role Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21304)
*Junru Wu,Jingting Zheng,Yuqi Ren,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VALCON框架，将大模型的价值一致性操作化为三个基准套件，通过双角色变化合成评估数据，揭示生成与评估角色之间的价值不一致，并给出机理分析与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM价值对齐评估过度依赖输出合规性，忽视了模型在生成者与评估者角色切换时是否保持价值一致；而现代智能体与对齐流程常依赖同一模型既生成动作又评估其可接受性，因此该一致性日益重要。

Method: 提出VALCON角色驱动的数据构建框架，通过双角色变化将价值一致性操作化为VAB-Consistency、VAB-Boundary、VAB-Challenge三个基准套件；将模型的生成行为与评估判断在同一原则下系统配对，合成能暴露隐藏价值差异的评估数据；并进行机理探测与表征导向的干预。

Result: 在28个LLM上的实验显示普遍存在跨角色不一致：输出高度合规的模型常无法使其判断与自身生成保持一致。机理探测发现与这种行为不对称相关的角色依赖表征解耦；基于此的表征导向干预能有效缓解一致性失败。

Conclusion: 该工作将价值一致性确立为独立的对齐维度，并提供了完整的评估—分析—缓解流程，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Current value alignment evaluations for large language models (LLMs) heavily rely on output compliance, overlooking whether LLMs maintain value consistency when alternating between generator and evaluator roles. Such consistency is increasingly important because modern LLM agents and alignment pipelines often rely on the same LLM to both generate actions and evaluate their acceptability. To mitigate this limitation, we propose VALCON, a role-driven data construction framework that operationalizes value consistency into three benchmark suites (VAB-Consistency, VAB-Boundary, VAB-Challenge) via dual-role variation. By systematically pairing an LLM's generative behavior with its evaluative judgments under identical principles, VALCON synthesizes target evaluation data that exposes hidden value discrepancies. Experiments on 28 LLMs reveal pervasive cross-role inconsistency: models with highly compliant outputs frequently fail to align their judgments with their own generations. To understand and address this gap, we perform mechanistic probing that uncovers a role-dependent representational decoupling associated with this behavioral asymmetry. Guided by these insights, we propose a representation-directed intervention that effectively mitigates consistency failures. Our work establishes value consistency as a distinct alignment dimension and provides a complete evaluation-analysis-mitigation pipeline. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON.

</details>


### [183] [Hybrid Generative-Tagging Decoding for Efficient Product Attribute-Value Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21329)
*Keshav Kolluru,Manas Gupta,Maroof Ahmad,Suchitra Sathyanarayana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出混合生成-标注解码架构，结合标注抽取源文本片段与开放式生成，在vLLM中集成后提升属性覆盖率10%、正确属性-值对增加63%、延迟降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商产品目录构建需要属性-值抽取。现有方法各有缺陷：标注模型速度快且有源文本依据，但无法产出需重格式化的值，也无法生成标签空间外的稀有属性名；生成模型逐token自回归生成，速度慢且属性覆盖有限。因此需要融合两者优势。

Method: 提出混合生成-标注解码的新模型架构：对高频且值可直接复制的属性，由标注头直接从源文本抽取值；生成头则专注于剩余部分——标注器标签空间外的稀有属性及不可复制的值（包括隐式或规范化值）。该混合解码器集成到vLLM推理中。

Result: 属性覆盖率提升10%，抽取到的正确属性-值对增加63%，且精度无损，同时延迟降低44%。

Conclusion: 混合生成-标注解码通过让标注与生成各司其职，兼顾速度、源文本依据与开放域覆盖，是一种面向电商属性-值抽取的高效且高精度的方案。

Abstract: Attribute-value extraction is important for building high-quality product catalogs in e-commerce. Existing systems either rely on generative or tagging strategies for extracting the attribute names and their corresponding values. Tagging models directly label spans in the source, making them fast and grounded, but they cannot produce values that require reformatting or generate rare attribute names outside their label space. Generative models avoid these drawbacks by generating both attribute names and values token by token, but this auto-regressive nature makes them slow and limits attribute coverage. We introduce hybrid generative-tagging decoding, a novel model architecture that combines tagging source spans and open-ended generation. For frequent attributes whose values are copyable, a tagging head extracts values directly from the source. This lets the generative head focus on the remainder - rare attributes outside the tagger's label space and non-copyable values, including implicit or normalized values. Integrated into vLLM inference, the hybrid decoder raises attribute coverage by 10% and extracts 63% more correct attribute-value pairs at no precision cost, while reducing latency by 44%.

</details>


### [184] [QuasiLeak-Bench: Evaluating LLM-Based Privacy Detectors on Policy-Conditioned Quasi-Evidence Group Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21333)
*Yufan Zhu,Yangfan Jiang,Yang Fei,Shizheng Hou,Shubhankar Mohapatra,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 QuasiLeak-Bench 基准，用于评估在给定披露政策下，检测器能否恢复由多个弱准标识符共同构成的"准证据组"并标记违规披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言消息中常含单独看来很弱的准标识符，但它们组合起来可共同泄露个人隐私属性（即"准证据组"）。现有隐私基准只关注显式标识符、属性推断或任务驱动披露，缺乏对这一类联合推断的测量；且实际是否违规还取决于发送方指定的披露政策。

Method: 提出 QuasiLeak-Bench，一个政策条件下的准证据组恢复基准，评估检测器是否恢复完整准证据组并标记政策禁止的披露。为大规模获得可靠真值，采用反向构建流程：先选取弱准标识符，将其组合为经验证可联合泄露目标属性的准证据组，再围绕该证据生成文档，并进行自动化质量控制。

Result: 实验显示现有隐私检测器在 QuasiLeak-Bench 上迁移效果差；本地可部署的 LLM 检测器远落后于专有模型，主要因为漏掉大部分准证据组。但在训练集上微调的 4B 模型在留出文档上超过了最强的提示模型。

Conclusion: 该任务具有可学习性，微调小模型即可超越强提示模型，说明准证据组恢复是可行且值得进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Natural language messages often contain quasi-identifiers that are weak in isolation but can jointly reveal an individual's private attributes. We call such a jointly revealing set of cues a quasi-evidence group. Existing privacy benchmarks focus on explicit identifiers, attribute inference, or task-driven disclosures, leaving inference from quasi-evidence groups largely unmeasured. In practice, whether exposing such a quasi-evidence group violates privacy also depends on sender-specified disclosure policies. We introduce QuasiLeak-Bench, a benchmark for policy-conditioned quasi-evidence group recovery, which evaluates whether detectors recover complete quasi-evidence groups and flag the disclosures the given policy prohibits. To obtain reliable ground truth at scale, a reverse-construction pipeline first selects weak quasi-identifiers, composes them into quasi-evidence groups verified to jointly reveal a target attribute, and generates documents around this evidence, followed by automated quality control. Experiments show that existing privacy detectors transfer poorly to QuasiLeak-Bench, and locally deployable LLM-based detectors fall far behind proprietary models, mainly because they miss most quasi-evidence groups altogether. However, a 4B model fine-tuned on our training split surpasses the strongest prompted model on held-out documents, showing that the task is learnable.

</details>


### [185] [OpCredit: Operation-Aware Policy Learning for Agentic Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21345)
*Yucheng Li,Mingyuan Li,Xiaxia Wang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OpCredit，一种操作感知的策略学习框架，通过结合下游结果信用与即时转移信用，为知识图谱问答中的单个操作分配信用，在多个数据集和骨干模型上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic KGQA 依赖可执行操作序列，但轨迹级奖励掩盖了单个操作的贡献：成功轨迹可能包含无效操作，失败轨迹也可能包含有用操作。因此需要评估单步操作的价值。

Method: OpCredit 结合两类信用信号：1）Branch Outcome Credit (BOC)，复用普通 rollout，在匹配的导航上下文下比较不同操作后观察到的终端任务奖励；2）Frontier Transition Credit (FTC)，衡量操作引起的、以答案为条件的前沿潜力变化。两种互补信号在训练中共同监督对应的决策片段，推理时仅使用学到的策略和标准 KG 接口。

Result: 在 ComplexWebQuestions、WebQSP 和 GrailQA 上，使用三种骨干模型，KGQA 性能均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 通过操作级信用分配，OpCredit 有效区分单个操作的贡献，提升了 KGQA 的策略学习效果，且推理阶段不引入额外结构或接口负担。

Abstract: Agentic knowledge graph question answering(KGQA) relies on sequences of executable operations, but trajectory-level rewards obscure their individual contributions. Successful trajectories may contain ineffective operations, while failed trajectories may contain useful ones. To assess individual operations, we propose OpCredit, an operationaware policy-learning framework that combines downstream outcome credit with immediate transition credit: Branch Outcome Credit (BOC) reuses ordinary rollouts to compare terminal task rewards observed after different operations under matched navigation contexts; Frontier Transition Credit (FTC) measures operation-induced changes in answer-conditioned frontier potential. The two complementary signals jointly supervise the corresponding decision spans during training, while inference uses only the learned policy and standard KG interface. Experiments on ComplexWebQuestions, WebQSP, and GrailQA across three backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in KGQA performance.

</details>


### [186] [Emotion Is Not External Knowledge: Internal Attention Signals for Affective State Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21347)
*Wenqiu Tang,Zhen Wan,Yihui Wang,Takahiro Komamizu,Ichiro Ide*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SIAB方法，通过因果定位稀疏情感注意力头并在答案位置提升其关注的内容token，无需外部标注即可提升多轮对话情感状态推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多轮交互中需维护隐式对话状态（如情感状态），这类状态由稀疏上下文线索推断且在话题转移后仍相关。已有注意力干预方法依赖带标注的域内注意力头画像和外部指定的目标跨度，难以跨情感证据分布迁移。

Method: Self-targeted Internal Attention Boosting (SIAB)：在合成最小对比对上通过因果定位（激活修补）定位稀疏情感头，并在答案位置提升这些头已经关注的内容token；被编辑的头与目标token均来自模型自身行为，因此同一头集合可跨任务复用。

Result: 在ECC与GoEmotions上、三种LLM及多种提示设置下评估：SIAB相比提示匹配基线在ECC上最高提升+10.3 macro-F1、GoEmotions上+5.5；结合PASTA式外部监督可达+13.84准确率点和+12.51 macro-F1点；机制分析表明增益源于稀疏的情感富集头，且层间邻近性影响可加性。

Conclusion: 模型自身行为可自监督地提供可复用的情感注意力头，SIAB无需域内标注即可有效路由情感证据并提升多轮情感状态推断，且与外部监督方法互补。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate in multi-turn interactions that require maintaining latent conversational states, not only explicit facts. We study affective state as an implicit state inferred from sparse contextual cues that remain relevant after topic shifts, and ask whether LLMs expose internal attention signals for routing such evidence at inference time. Prior attention interventions rely on labeled in-domain head profiles and externally specified target spans, limiting transfer across affective-evidence distributions. We propose Self-targeted Internal Attention Boosting (SIAB), which localizes sparse affective heads via causal localization (in detail activation patching) on synthetic minimal pairs and boosts, at the answer position, the content tokens these heads already attend to; both edited heads and target tokens are derived from the model's own behavior, enabling the same head set to be reused across tasks.We evaluate on ECC and GoEmotions across three LLMs and multiple prompting settings. Across settings, SIAB improves prompt-matched baselines by up to +10.3 macro-F1 on ECC and +5.5 on GoEmotions. Combined with PASTA-style external supervision, it achieves up to +13.84 accuracy points and +12.51 macro-F1 points, while mechanistic analyses trace the gains to sparse, emotion-enriched heads and show that layer proximity affects additivity.

</details>


### [187] [RenoLayout: Unified Floorplan Renovation and Furniture Layout for Constraint-Aware Residential Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21366)
*Jingguo Liu,Yuanqing Liu,Chong Zhang,Yue Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RenoLayout，将住宅设计建模为设计点引导的条件生成任务，把改造前住宅状态与设计点集转化为可执行的Geometry-as-Code程序，并构建设计点体系、自动标注方法、三段式训练流程及首个统一住宅设计基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户型生成方法通常假设输入为空白建筑边界，无法处理改造场景；同时户型改造与家具布局被当作独立任务，忽视二者内在的空间依赖。实际住宅设计需在保留不可变结构的同时改造可编辑空间元素，并在设计目标与物理约束下优化家具摆放。

Method: 将住宅设计形式化为设计点引导的条件生成任务；提出RenoLayout框架，把改造前住宅状态与设计点集转换为可执行的Geometry-as-Code程序；构建涵盖结构改造与家具布局的123个细粒度设计点的Design Map；提出Difference-Grounded Design-Point Annotation，基于VLM一致性验证从改造前后设计对中自动推导设计点集；采用CPT–SFT–RL三阶段流水线训练。

Result: 构建包含123个细粒度设计点的设计图谱；提出自动设计点标注方法并配以VLM一致性验证；发布RenoLayout Benchmark，包含200个真实世界改造前后案例及完整家具布局，并涵盖设计关键的结构与建筑设备约束，据称是首个统一住宅设计基准。

Conclusion: RenoLayout通过设计点引导的条件生成与三阶段训练，统一了住宅户型改造与家具布局任务，为真实改造场景下的可执行设计生成提供了方法论与评测基准。

Abstract: Residential design aims to transform existing homes into functional living spaces while satisfying diverse design preferences and physical constraints. However, existing floorplan generation methods typically assume empty building boundaries as input, whereas renovation requires reasoning over existing floorplans. Furthermore, floorplan renovation and furniture layout are commonly addressed as separate tasks, overlooking their inherent spatial dependency. Consequently, practical residential design must preserve immutable structural components while transforming editable spatial elements. It must also optimize furniture placement under design objectives and physical constraints. To address these challenges, we formulate residential design as a design-point-guided conditional generation task and propose RenoLayout, a framework that translates a pre-design residential state together with a design-point set into an executable Geometry-as-Code program. To enable controllable supervision, we construct a Design Map comprising 123 fine-grained design points covering structural renovation and furniture layout. We further introduce Difference-Grounded Design-Point Annotation to derive design-point sets automatically from before--after design pairs with VLM-based consistency verification. RenoLayout is trained using a three-stage CPT--SFT--RL pipeline, progressively improving spatial reasoning, instruction following, and constraint compliance. We also introduce the RenoLayout Benchmark, a set of 200 real-world before--after renovation cases with complete furniture layouts. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first benchmark for unified residential design. These cases include design-critical structural and building-service constraints.

</details>


### [188] [ESG-Kor: A Korean Dataset for ESG-related Information Extraction and Practical Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21368)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Geonyeong Son,Misuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了名为ESG-Kor的韩语数据集，用于自动抽取ESG（环境、社会、治理）信息，并通过多种韩语预训练语言模型验证其有效性，同时展示了在中小企业文档中的实际应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来预训练语言模型使用日益广泛，特定领域任务的数据集重要性显著上升。ESG信息近期受到重视，但缺乏用于自动抽取ESG信息的韩语数据集，因此需要构建相应数据集以支持该领域研究与应用。

Method: 从韩国企业的可持续发展报告中抽取ESG各组成要素信息，依据ESG评估机构提供的客观规则进行人工标注，构建包含118,946个句子的ESG-Kor数据集；随后使用多个韩语预训练语言模型进行ESG分类性能验证，并将分类模型扩展到中小企业文档进行信息抽取与关键议题分析。

Result: 使用多个韩语预训练语言模型进行分类实验，取得了显著的性能表现，验证了ESG-Kor数据集的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: ESG-Kor数据集能够有效支持ESG信息的自动抽取，且通过向中小企业文档扩展并基于ESG关键议题进行深入分析，展示了该数据集在ESG领域的实际应用潜力和使用场景。

Abstract: With the expansion of pre-trained language model usage in recent years, the importance of datasets for performing tasks in specialized domains has significantly increased. Therefore, we have built a Korean dataset called ESG-Kor to automatically extract Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) information, which has recently gained importance. ESG-Kor is a dataset consisting of a total of 118,946 sentences that extracted information on each ESG component from Korean companies' sustainability reports and manually labeled it according to objective rules provided by ESG evaluation agencies. To verify the effectiveness and applicability of the ESG-Kor dataset, classification performance was confirmed using several Korean pre-trained language models, and significant performance was obtained. Additionally, by extending the ESG classification model to documents of small and medium enterprises and extracting information based on ESG key issues and in-depth analysis, we demonstrated potential and practical use cases in the ESG field.

</details>


### [189] [DPA-MoE: A Runtime Precision Control Framework for MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21378)
*Jaeyun Kim,Hoonki Lee,Susie Shin,Hyunmin Kim,Dahyun Lee,Sangyeob Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DPA-MoE是一个运行时精度控制框架，将MoE中被路由的专家权重编码为可前缀解码的分块，并利用路由器门控分数在推理时动态分配精度，从而实现单一检查点多精度运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型虽通过将每个token路由到少量专家来降低计算量，但每个被选中的专家仍以固定精度使用，缺乏根据运行时情况灵活调整精度的能力。

Method: 使用SharedScale AnyBCQ将专家前馈权重编码为可前缀解码的分块；离线分析每个专家的重建残差；推理时利用实时路由器门控分数进行路由后的分块分配。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B和DeepSeekMoE-16B上，DPA-MoE在相同预算下相比均匀分块分配方案，在KL散度和下游准确率上均有提升，原型系统中仅有适度的稳态吞吐开销。

Conclusion: DPA-MoE使单一编码的MoE检查点无需重新训练或重新编码即可在多种有效权重预算下运行，验证了路由后动态精度分配的有效性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models reduce computation by routing each token to a small set of experts, but each selected expert is still used at a fixed precision. We present DPA-MoE, a runtime precision control framework that makes routed experts any-precision. DPA-MoE encodes expert feed-forward weights as prefix-decodable chunks using SharedScale AnyBCQ, profiles per-expert reconstruction residuals offline, and allocates chunks at inference time using live router gate scores. This post-routing allocation lets a single encoded MoE checkpoint operate at multiple effective weight budgets without retraining or re-encoding. Experiments on Qwen3-30B-A3B and DeepSeekMoE-16B show that DPA-MoE improves KL divergence and downstream accuracy over uniform chunk allocation at matched budgets, with modest steady-state throughput overhead in our prototype.

</details>


### [190] [Calibration Language Shapes Block-Influence Pruning in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21383)
*Vedant Pandhare,Piyush Dhoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究Block Influence (BI)深度剪枝中英文校准分布向印度语言迁移的可靠性，发现英文校准系统性低估印度语言后层BI，多语言校准可缓解但效果因架构与剪枝预算而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BI剪枝依赖校准分布，若英文校准无法代表印度语言，则剪枝排名与所删块可能失配。作者希望检验跨语言（含跨文字）迁移是否稳健，并明确其适用边界。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、Llama-3.2-1B、Gemma-2-2B上，用互不重叠的维基百科文章对英语、印地语、马拉地语、泰米尔语进行校准与评估；各选择器预算相同（150块），泰米尔语不参与多语言聚合以测试跨文字迁移；比较晚层BI、bootstrap区间、全局排名相关性与低BI块选择，并测试均值、排名均值、极小极大排名聚合器。

Result: 三种模型的印度语言晚层BI比英语高20.7–55.5%，所有预设的"晚层减中层"对比95% bootstrap区间均大于零；英文校准与印度语言校准所选低BI块不同，尽管全局排名相关性高。等预算混合英/印地/马拉地BI可缓解Qwen（6块）与Gemma（各深度，含留出泰米尔语）的困惑度损伤；但对Llama不通用：2块时改善印地语与马拉地语却严重损害泰米尔语，较大剪枝预算下不同校准选出相同块且困惑度崩塌；排名均值与极小极大排名聚合器未优于原始均值。

Conclusion: 结论是诊断性主张而非通用剪枝规则：仅用英文BI会系统性遗漏印度语言的后层活动，但多语言校准需按架构与剪枝预算逐一验证。

Abstract: Block Influence (BI) ranks Transformer blocks for depth pruning from their input-output cosine change, but the ranking depends on the calibration distribution. We study whether English calibration transfers to Indian languages in Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, Llama-3.2-1B, and Gemma-2-2B. English, Hindi, Marathi, and Tamil use disjoint Wikipedia articles for calibration and evaluation; every selector receives the same 150-chunk budget, and Tamil is excluded from multilingual aggregation to test cross-script transfer. Across all three models, late-block BI is 20.7-55.5% higher for the three Indian languages than for English, and every predeclared late-minus-middle contrast has a 95% bootstrap interval above zero. This mismatch changes the low-BI blocks selected for removal despite high global rank correlations. Averaging BI over equal English, Hindi, and Marathi budgets mitigates the resulting perplexity damage for Qwen at six removed blocks and for Gemma at all tested depths, including held-out Tamil. The mitigation is not universal: on Llama, balancing improves Hindi and Marathi at two removed blocks but severely harms Tamil, while larger pruning budgets select the same blocks and collapse perplexity regardless of calibration. Rank-mean and minimax-rank aggregators do not dominate the raw mean. These results support a diagnostic claim rather than a universal pruning rule: English-only BI systematically misses late-layer activity for Indian-language text, but multilingual calibration must still be validated per architecture and pruning budget.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Invariance: Factor-wise Selective Treatment of Shifts for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21403)
*Guimin Hu,Qiwei Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出FaShift框架，通过因子化诊断潜在表示，将偏移信息区分为非预测、稳定预测和不稳定预测三类，并分别采取抑制、保留或衰减策略，在多模态情感数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布偏移对鲁棒多模态学习构成根本性挑战。现有方法通常通过抑制环境相关变化来追求不变性，对所有偏移信息统一处理，但偏移信息实际上有不同预测后果：有的预测贡献小，有的仍具可靠信息，有的预测效应依赖环境。因此需回答：哪些偏移信息应被抑制、保留或衰减？

Method: 提出FaShift——一种因子级偏移框架。依据分布变化、干预效应和跨环境稳定性来诊断潜在表示因子。通过因子化多模态表示并评估这些互补属性，区分非预测因子、稳定预测因子以及预测有用但不稳定的因子，随后选择性地抑制、保留或衰减其贡献，而非强制统一不变性。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和CH-SIMS上，FaShift在三个数据集的ID准确率和F1上均达到最佳。与报告OOD结果的最强竞争者相比，MOSI的OOD准确率/F1提升1.5/3.6个百分点，MOSEI提升1.7/2.7个百分点，并在CH-SIMS上取得最佳OOD准确率和F1。

Conclusion: FaShift通过因子级诊断与差异化处理偏移信息，优于统一不变性策略，有效提升多模态学习在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distribution shifts pose a fundamental challenge to robust multimodal learning. Existing methods typically promote invariance by suppressing environment-dependent variations, implicitly applying a uniform treatment to shifted information. However, shifted information can have different predictive consequences: some contributes little to prediction, some remains reliably informative, and some exhibits environment-dependent predictive effects. This raises a fundamental question: which shifted information should be suppressed, preserved, or attenuated? To address this question, we propose FaShift, a factor-wise shift framework that diagnoses latent representation factors according to their distributional changes, intervention effects, and cross-environment stability. By factorizing multimodal representations and evaluating these complementary properties, FaShift distinguishes non-predictive factors, stably predictive factors, and predictively useful but unstable factors. It then selectively suppresses, preserves, or attenuates their contributions, rather than enforcing uniform invariance. Experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that FaShift achieves the best ID accuracy and F1 across all three datasets. Compared with the strongest competitors reporting OOD results, it improves OOD accuracy/F1 by 1.5/3.6 points on MOSI and 1.7/2.7 points on MOSEI, while achieving the best reported OOD accuracy and F1 on CH-SIMS.

</details>


### [192] [Identifying Effective Strategies in Prompt Evolution for Stress and Emotion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21407)
*SeungHyun Yi,Seungjun Yi,Sriram Sattiraju,Haeun Park,Joakim Nguyen,Terence Lim,Joseph Skrovan,Mehak Beri,Hitakshi Modi,Eloy Carrion,Andrew Well,Carlos M. Mery,Alexander Rasgon,Mia K Markey,Ying Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较六种方法在两种模型规模下对社交媒体压力与情绪数据进行预测，发现GEPA及其扩展方法（GEPA+merge）在多种设置中优于初始提示，并识别出五种与性能提升相关的提示策略及四项跨任务观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期压力检测与风险预测对及时的心理健康评估与支持至关重要。现有大语言模型虽在社交媒体压力预测上显示潜力，但单纯依赖准确率会掩盖假阳性问题；同时提示修改在不同模型与任务上收益不一致，且其与策略组合、指令变化的关系尚不明确。

Method: 在社交媒体压力与情绪数据上比较六种方法，覆盖两种模型规模；采用GEPA及将两个提示的指令合并的扩展方法（GEPA+merge），并分析提示策略组合与指令变化对开发集表现的影响。

Result: GEPA与GEPA+merge在多个设置中相较初始提示提升了预测表现；识别出五种与开发收益相关的提示策略：引导关注情绪证据、区分当前与过去痛苦、示例化间接线索、要求情绪背景、引导标签选择。此外记录四项跨任务与评估设置的观察，其中二分类压力预测早期即有改善，而多标签情绪预测初期下降后部分恢复；其余涉及指令错误或冲突、小样本比较上的提升在全量开发数据上不成立，以及所选提示的开发与报告的测试排名变化。

Conclusion: 研究明确了有效的提示演化方法、与收益相关的策略组合，以及任务与评估层面的局限，为将提示演化方法应用于社交媒体压力检测提供了指导。

Abstract: Early stress detection and risk prediction are crucial for timely mental health assessment and support. While large language models have recently shown promise for social media stress prediction, relying on accuracy can obscure false positives. Also, prompt revisions yield inconsistent gains across models and tasks, with unclear links to strategy combinations and instruction changes. By comparing six methods on social media stress and emotion data across two model sizes, we found that GEPA and its extension combining instructions from two prompts (GEPA+merge) improved prediction over initial prompts in several settings. We also identified five prompting strategies associated with development gains. These strategies included directing attention to emotional evidence, distinguishing current from past distress, illustrating indirect cues, requiring emotional context, and guiding label selection. Moreover, we documented four observations across tasks and evaluation settings. In a separate set of searches, binary stress prediction improved early, whereas multilabel emotion prediction initially declined and partly recovered. The other three concerned incorrect or conflicting instructions, gains on small comparison samples that did not hold on full development data, and changes in selected prompts' development and reported test rankings. These findings identify effective prompt evolution methods, strategy combinations associated with gains, and task and evaluation limits, providing guidance for applying prompt evolution methods to social media stress detection.

</details>


### [193] [HyCo: Hybrid Drafting for Cost-Efficient Mobile-Cloud Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21436)
*Hanbin Bae,Heejin Choi,Joohyung Lee,Byoung Jin Choi,Sangjun Park,Hoon-Young Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyCo是一种设备-云协同的推测解码框架，将服务器模型原生多token预测（MTP）头的深度分配给设备端，将备选分支交给一次SLM前向，从而减少移动NPU上的重复计算并降低服务器GPU开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备-云推测解码可减少服务器计算，但在移动NPU上用小型语言模型（SLM）扩展深层草稿树需要反复前向传播，成本高。因此需要一种方法在保持草稿质量的同时降低设备端重复计算与服务器端开销。

Method: HyCo将深度分配给服务器模型原生MTP头并在设备端执行，把备选分支合并为一次SLM前向。服务器返回MTP头所需的隐藏状态、输入嵌入和键值条目，而非执行其顺序步骤。通过量化、受限词表和1K token窗口使两个草稿器适配NPU。另引入ε阈值规则，放松对验证器采样分布的精确保持。

Result: 在Galaxy S26 Ultra、LTE/5G单客户端场景下，相比服务器端MTP自推测，HyCo在Spec-Bench上降低每输出token的服务器GPU时间38.6%，在32K token摘要上降低29.2%。相同接受规则下，三次Spec-Bench运行显示比纯SLM草稿器多节省9.0个百分点，交付速度提升1.9倍。相比Cloud+MTP，评审分数高0.2分（配对95% CI：-0.9至+1.3）；探索性翻译任务偏向SpecEdge-ε。无放松时，HyCo exact在两个数据集上分别节省27.7%和11.9%。

Conclusion: HyCo通过设备端执行MTP头、SLM一次前向生成备选分支以及受限NPU适配，有效降低了服务器GPU时间并提升了交付速度，同时以ε阈值规则在分布精确性与效率之间取得可接受的折中。

Abstract: Device-cloud speculative decoding reduces server computation, but growing a deep draft tree with a small language model (SLM) requires repeated passes on a mobile neural processing unit (NPU). We present HyCo, which assigns depth to the server model's native multi-token prediction (MTP) head, executed on the device, and alternative branches to one SLM pass. The server returns the hidden state, input embedding, and key-value entries needed by the head instead of executing its sequential steps. Quantization, a restricted vocabulary, and a 1K-token window fit both drafters to the NPU. An ε-threshold rule additionally relaxes exact preservation of the verifier's sampling distribution. In single-client runs with a Galaxy S26 Ultra over LTE/5G, HyCo reduces server GPU time per output token relative to server-side MTP self-speculation by 38.6% on Spec-Bench and 29.2% on 32K-token summarization. With the same acceptance rule, three Spec-Bench runs per system show 9.0 percentage points more saving and 1.9x delivery speed than an SLM-only drafter. HyCo differs from Cloud+MTP by +0.2 judge-score points (paired 95% CI: -0.9 to +1.3); exploratory translation favors SpecEdge-ε. Without relaxation, HyCo exact saves 27.7% and 11.9% on the two datasets.

</details>


### [194] [SITAR: A Situational Distortion Metric for Automatic Speech Recognition Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21440)
*Mingzhe Lu,Jinhao Liu,Yunpeng Li,Hang Su,Lichun Fan,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SITAR，一种面向ASR的情境扭曲度量，通过有界递归公式聚合情境严重性与累积程度，训练轻量级BERT回归器对参考-假设对进行单次前向打分，并提供涵盖外在效度与内在可靠性的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR转写中词汇或语义相似的错误可能对语音所传达情境产生截然不同的影响，捕捉这些差异需要理解话语所描述、确立或意图实现的内容，而非仅靠上下文评估；但依赖LLM评判会带来计算成本与评分波动问题。

Method: 提出SITAR端到端构建框架：将有界递归公式用于聚合情境严重性与累积程度形成统一分数；借助有限离线LLM支持生成目标，训练轻量级BERT族回归器，对参考-假设对在一次前向传播中打分，避免运行时高开销；并形式化涵盖宏观/微观外在效度与内在评分器可靠性的验证协议。

Result: 在多样数据集与模型上的外在效度评估表明，SITAR能可靠追踪宏观能力缩放并提供正交诊断价值；在专门构建的受控基准上，内在可靠性评估确认评分工具本身运行稳健。

Conclusion: SITAR有望成为现有ASR指标的实用、轻量级补充；作者将在内部评审后发布代码、模型权重、数据集等完整配方以支持社区研究。

Abstract: In automatic speech recognition (ASR), transcription errors with similar lexical or semantic discrepancies can have markedly different consequences for the situation conveyed by speech. Capturing these differences requires not merely contextual assessment, but an understanding of what the spoken discourse describes, establishes, or seeks to bring about. However, relying on LLM judges introduces computational cost and scoring variability. We introduce SITAR, a situational distortion metric for ASR, realized through a comprehensive end-to-end construction framework. Specifically, this framework aggregates situational severity and cumulative extent into a unified score via a bounded recursive formulation. Using targets derived from this formulation with limited offline LLM support, we train SITAR as a lightweight BERT-family regressor that scores reference-hypothesis pairs in a single forward pass, entirely free of any prohibitive runtime costs. Recognizing that introducing a new metric demands rigorous justification, we formalize a principled validation protocol spanning both extrinsic validity, assessed at macro and micro levels, and intrinsic scorer reliability. The former, evaluated across diverse datasets and models, demonstrates that SITAR reliably tracks broad capability scaling while providing orthogonal diagnostic utility, whereas the latter, examined on a purpose-built controlled benchmark, confirms the operational soundness of the scoring instrument itself. Through systematic validation, we demonstrate that SITAR holds strong potential as a practical, lightweight complement to existing ASR metrics. We will release the full recipes (code, model weights, dataset, etc.) after the internal review to support community research.

</details>


### [195] [Translation Circuit Discovery and Targeted Optimization for Low-Resource Multilingual Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21456)
*Atai Waris Khan,Abdullah Mazhar,Raghu Kumar,Md Shad Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言解码器LLM中低资源翻译的机制，提出TITAN框架识别并优化关键翻译电路，仅更新不到5%参数即显著提升印地语至乌尔都语和信德语翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型翻译性能虽强，但低资源语言翻译的内部机制尚不清楚，需要机制性的理解与高效的适配方法。

Method: 提出TITAN机制框架，结合因果干预、稀疏特征分解和定向偏好优化，定位并优化transformer中关键的翻译组件，仅优化稀疏且因果重要的电路，参数更新少于5%。

Result: 相比最强多语言基线，印地语至乌尔都语BLEU提升33.75%，印地语至信德语提升45.86%；人类专家评估确认在充分性和流畅度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 多语言翻译通过稀疏结构化电路在共享语义子空间上实现，而非以英语为中心的枢轴表示；机制电路发现可同时提升低资源翻译性能并提供可解释性洞见。

Abstract: Recent multilingual large language models achieve strong translation performance, yet the internal mechanisms responsible for low-resource multilingual translation remain insufficiently understood. In this paper, we study translation circuits in multilingual decoder-only LLMs for Hindi-to-Urdu and Hindi-to-Sindhi (Devanagari). We propose TITAN, a mechanistic framework that combines causal intervention, sparse feature decomposition, and targeted preference optimization to identify and optimize crucial translation components in the transformer. Unlike conventional full-parameter fine-tuning, TITAN restricts optimization to sparse, causally important circuits, enabling scalable and parameter-efficient adaptation while updating fewer than 5% of model parameters. Experimental results show that TITAN achieves BLEU improvements of 33.75% on Hindi-to-Urdu and 45.86% on Hindi-to-Sindhi over the strongest multilingual baselines. Human expert evaluation further confirms substantial gains in adequacy and fluency across both language pairs. Additional analyses reveal that multilingual translation is implemented through sparse, structured circuits operating over a shared semantic subspace rather than through an English-centric pivot representation. Overall, our findings demonstrate that mechanistic circuit discovery can directly improve low-resource multilingual translation while providing interpretable insights into multilingual transformer behavior.

</details>


### [196] [Trajectory-Level Speculation with Semantic Verification for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21463)
*Zilin Wang,Yinhong Liu,Chao Chen,Qun Liu,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecAgent 将强模型从"每步生成者"转变为"弱模型草稿的审阅者"：弱模型基于真实观测起草只读工具调用步骤，强模型通过单次预填充并行验证并提交最长有效前缀、修复首个被拒步骤，在保持接近强模型成功率的同时大幅降低内存流量与时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体由单一强模型生成每一步，但许多步骤其实很常规，小模型即可胜任。已有协作方案（路由器、协作智能体、推测式逐step校验）仍把强模型当作生成者，强模型仍需承担大量解码成本。作者希望让强模型只做审阅者而非作者，从而削减时延与内存流量。

Method: 提出 SpecAgent 协作协议：弱模型在起草过程中执行只读工具调用，使每一步都基于真实观测，并在遇到首个会修改环境的动作时停下；强模型随后以单次预填充（prefill）并行验证该草稿片段，提交其判断为语义有效的最长前缀，并修复第一个被拒步骤。由于验证用并行预填充替代自回归解码，强模型仅需解码简短判定与偶发修复。

Result: 在 τ²-Bench 上，SpecAgent 成功率与强模型相差不到 1 个百分点，同时内存流量和墙钟时间约减半；在 AutomationBench 和 VitaBench 上，弥补了 44–75% 的质量差距，且两项开销均下降。在 MCP-Atlas 的边缘-云部署（三种云模型）中，弥补了弱-强质量差距的 53–75%，而强模型生成的 token 数最多仅为原来的一半。

Conclusion: 强模型无需撰写智能体的每一步：审阅有真实观测支撑的草稿即可保留大部分质量，同时降低时延与内存流量。

Abstract: Large language model agents solve tasks through long sequences of tool calls, and the prevailing design has a single strong model generate every step, although many steps are routine enough for a much smaller model. Existing ways of sharing this work keep the strong model as the generator: routers and collaborative agents decide which model handles each query or step, and speculative methods check a small model's drafts one step at a time, usually against the strong model's own action. We introduce SpecAgent, a collaboration protocol that turns the strong model from the author of each step into a reviewer of steps drafted by a weak model. The weak model executes read-only tool calls as it drafts, so that every step is grounded in real observations, and holds the first action that would modify the environment. The strong model then verifies the drafted segment in a single prefill pass, commits the longest prefix it judges semantically valid, and repairs the first rejected step. Because verification replaces autoregressive decoding with parallel prefill, the strong model decodes only short verdicts and occasional repairs rather than every step. On $τ^2$-Bench, SpecAgent comes within one point of the strong model's success rate while roughly halving memory traffic and wall time, and on AutomationBench and VitaBench it recovers 44-75% of the quality gap while lowering both. In an edge-cloud deployment on MCP-Atlas with three cloud models, it closes 53-75% of the weak-strong quality gap while the strong model generates at most half as many tokens. These results suggest that a strong model need not author every step of an agent: reviewing grounded drafts retains most of its quality while cutting both latency and memory traffic.

</details>


### [197] [Can LLMs Tell Stories Out of Order? A Corpus and Benchmark for Nonlinear Narrative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21468)
*Syed Rifat Raiyan,Md. Tariquzzaman,Md Farhan Ishmam,Tenghao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个包含83部出版作品的基准，用于衡量语言模型讲述非线性故事的能力。实验发现，19个模型在默认情况下比人类原作更倾向于按时间顺序讲述；即使被要求采用特定结构，也仅约一半情况落入人类范围。读者和自动评判均更偏好模型默认写出的线性故事，而非按要求写出的非线性故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故事存在"事件发生顺序"与"叙述顺序"两种次序，二者不一致时即为非线性故事。现有研究缺乏系统基准来检验语言模型能否真正讲好非线性故事，因此作者构建基准并评估模型在叙事结构上的表现。

Method: 构建含83部出版作品的基准，每部标注同一事件列表的两种顺序，以及包含因果依赖、角色知识和叙事所提出与解决问题的故事图。让19个模型在给定事件下生成故事，并比较不同条件下的故事：仅给事件、给故事图、限定为出版叙述顺序。评估采用自动评判与71名人类读者共同完成。

Result: 给定时间顺序事件时，19个模型平均比出版作品更按时间顺序讲述；被要求特定结构时，仅约一半落入人类范围。读者和自动评判都更偏好模型默认写出的线性故事，而非按请求写出的非线性故事；81%的决定性判断中读者认为非线性故事更难跟随。读者还更偏好由故事图生成的故事，而非受限于出版叙述顺序的故事（72%决定性判断），但自动评判未检测到这一差异。

Conclusion: 遵循"重新排序故事"的指令，尚不等同于能出色地"打乱顺序讲述"。语言模型在非线性叙事上仍与人类作者存在差距，且自动评判可能遗漏读者能感知的质量差异。

Abstract: Every story has two orders: the order in which its events happen and the order in which they are told. A story is nonlinear when the two differ. We introduce a benchmark of 83 published works that measures whether language models can tell nonlinear stories. For each work, we annotate one list of events in both orders, together with a story graph of causal dependencies, character knowledge, and the questions the telling raises and resolves. Given events in chronological order, all 19 models tell them, on average, more chronologically than the published works do; asked for a specific structure, they land within the human range about half the time. We then compare a model's stories of the same events under different conditions, using both an automated judge and 71 human readers. Both prefer the chronological story a model writes by default to the nonlinear one it writes on request, which readers find harder to follow in 81% of decisive judgments. Readers also prefer stories written from the story graph to stories constrained to the published telling order (72% of decisive judgments), a difference the judge does not detect. Our results suggest that following an instruction to reorder a story is not yet the same as telling it well out of order.

</details>


### [198] [OPRD: On-Policy Reminder Distillation for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21496)
*Yuechen Luo,Zhiyi Lai,Miao Ziqi,Zihang Xu,Jingxuan Xu,Songwei Yu,Wenqiang Zhu,Weihao Li,Junhao Fan,Haoyang Huang,Yanan Wu,Ken Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 On-Policy Reminder Distillation（OPRD），通过多尺度提醒监督与基于结果的路由，在不改变推理时接口的前提下提升仓库级代码智能体的任务解决率与过程可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码任务要求智能体在长程交互中不仅生成正确代码，还需保持约束并响应工具反馈。诊断分析显示，智能体常常无法利用上下文中已有的相关信息，而最终的通过/失败结果只能提供稀疏的过程监督。

Method: OPRD 结合 Multi-Scope Reminder 监督与基于结果的路由，作用于无提醒的学生 rollout：成功的 rollout 提供自回归自模仿目标，失败的 rollout 则由教师对同一学生生成前缀重新评估，施加 Reminder 条件下的反向 KL 监督。Global Reminder 将最终测试诊断转化为轨迹级指导，Turn-local Reminder 仅作用于相关环境观测后的下一步决策。教师与学生共享 Coding SFT 与 Instruction-Following（IF）SFT 的两阶段初始化，推理时学生不使用 Reminder。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Pro 和 SWE-bench Multilingual 上，OPRD 相比开源 Qwen3.5-4B 基座模型，基准平均任务解决率提升 11.3 个百分点；在相同诊断协议下，聚合错误事件数从 446 降至 222，减少 50.2%。

Conclusion: OPRD 通过将稀疏的结果信号转化为多尺度的过程监督，有效提升了仓库级代码智能体的任务解决能力并显著减少了过程性错误，且推理阶段无需额外提醒即可保持性能。

Abstract: Reliable repository-level coding requires agents not only to generate correct code but also to preserve constraints and respond to tool feedback across long interactions. However, our diagnostic analysis shows that agents often fail to act on relevant information already available in context, while final Pass/Fail outcomes provide only sparse process supervision. We introduce On-Policy Reminder Distillation (OPRD), which combines Multi-Scope Reminder supervision with outcome-based routing on reminder-free student rollouts. Successful rollouts provide autoregressive self-imitation targets, whereas failed rollouts receive Reminder-conditioned reverse-KL supervision from a teacher that re-evaluates the same student-generated prefixes. A Global Reminder converts final-test diagnostics into trajectory-level guidance, while Turn-local Reminders apply only to the next decision following the relevant environment observation. The teacher and student share a two-stage initialization from Coding SFT and Instruction-Following (IF) SFT. At inference, the student operates without Reminders. On SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Pro, and SWE-bench Multilingual, OPRD improves the benchmark-averaged task resolution rate by 11.3 points over the open-source Qwen3.5-4B base model. Under the same diagnostic protocol, it reduces the aggregate error-event count from 446 to 222, a 50.2% reduction.

</details>


### [199] [Writability and the Pathway: A Controlled Study of Knowledge Injection into Addressed Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21553)
*Wei Gao,Cheng Tian,Duan Yang,Jianqing Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控GPT-2实验与Qwen、DeepSeek生产级记忆实验，检验"写入成功"能否转化为新问题下的知识检索，结果支持有界写入的可行性，但首token排名提升不等于完整答案成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆为事后知识注入提供了局部界面，但一次成功写入并不必然迁移到新问题，也未必保持未针对行为不变。因此需要区分"训练表达""问题形式行为"与"保持性"三个层面，明确写入的条件与边界。

Method: 在冻结GPT-2上附接新初始化模块，采用2×2设计：仅行更新 vs 行+投影更新，各配有/不配固定模块残差锚定；三个采样种子各复现50个训练完成。生产记忆实验分别使用Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-FP8与DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash，采用梯度写入与零阶（SPSA）写入对比，并设置独立重置目标与匹配对照。

Result: GPT-2各实验组均复现训练完成；自然问题目标串命中率：两个仅行更新组平均5.3%，无锚定投影更新28.7%，有锚定22.0%，严格答案得分均为零；锚定降低漂移，但词面命中不足以证明实体特定检索。Qwen中梯度写入使指定首答案token在132个独立重置目标中排名第一（93个新提升、39个原本已居首）；匹配的24目标对比中Adam取得13个新提升、SPSA仅2个，但计算流程不对等。80行案例含保存续写与重放记录，DeepSeek六个零阶案例仅1个新提升、1个回退。

Conclusion: 结果支持有界写入的可行性，并将训练表达、问题形式行为与保持性相区分；首token排名不能证明完整答案成功，也不构成可写记忆的普遍条件。

Abstract: Addressed conditional memory provides a local surface for post-hoc knowledge injection, but a successful write need not transfer to a new question or preserve untargeted behavior. This study combines a controlled GPT-2 comparison with production-memory experiments on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-FP8 and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash. Newly initialized modules attached to frozen GPT-2 are tested in a 2 x 2 design: row-only versus row-plus-projection updates, each with or without fixed module-residual anchoring. All arms reproduce 50 trained completions in each of three sampling seeds. Natural-question target-string hits average 5.3% for both row-only arms, 28.7% for unanchored projection updates, and 22.0% with anchoring, while strict answer scores remain zero. Anchoring lowers measured drift; lexical hits do not establish entity-specific retrieval. In the archived Qwen experiments, gradient writes make the designated first answer token top-ranked for 132 independently reset targets, comprising 93 new promotions and 39 already top-ranked targets. A separate matched 24-target comparison gives 13 new promotions with Adam versus two with SPSA at equal iteration counts but unequal computational procedures. An 80-row case includes saved continuations and a replay record, while reformulations bound its scope. Six DeepSeek zero-order cases yield one new target-token promotion and one regression. These results support bounded writing feasibility and separate trained expression, question-form behavior, and preservation; first-token rankings do not establish complete-answer success or universal conditions for writable memory.

</details>


### [200] [DialFork: Going Beyond Factuality to Evaluate Procedural Hallucination in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21561)
*Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Dairazalia Sánchez-Cortés,Yanis Labrak,Jordi Luque,Milos Cernak,Petr Motlicek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DialFork基准，用于衡量大模型作为对话代理时的"过程幻觉"问题，即话语流畅但偏离预定对话流程的失败模式，并提供FlowKL与FlowScore两个流程感知指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实幻觉已被广泛研究，但另一类失败模式被忽视：过程幻觉。代理生成的每句话都流畅且事实上合理，却在整体上偏离了预期的对话流程。现有评测缺乏针对原始对话文本的过程性偏差测量手段。

Method: 构建DialFork：在受控轮次位置对合规对话进行分叉，并注入四类幻觉，形成覆盖12个领域、24个任务、共36,278段对话的数据集，由30名标注者验证。定义三个评测任务，分别在集合、单对话、轮次三个粒度刻画过程偏差：部署监控、过程检索、实时护栏。针对前两项提出对话流程感知指标FlowKL与FlowScore，依据对话展开方式而非内容进行比较。

Result: 在对话级别，FlowScore在七个编码器上均显著优于所有基线，表明语义相似度无法替代过程相似度。

Conclusion: DialFork以MIT许可发布（https://github.com/idiap/dialfork），呼吁社区在衡量对话内容的同时也衡量对话流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed as conversational agents are increasingly expected to follow procedural logic. While factual hallucination has received extensive attention, a different failure mode has been overlooked: procedural hallucination, in which an agent produces individually fluent and factually plausible utterances while deviating from the intended conversational process. We introduce DialFork, the first benchmark to measure this failure mode on raw dialogue text. DialFork is built by forking conformant dialogues at controlled turn positions and injecting one of four hallucination types, yielding 36,278 dialogues across 24 tasks in 12 domains; 30 human annotators validate the construction. We define three evaluation tasks that characterize procedural deviation at three granularities: how far a set of dialogues deviates from conformant ones (monitoring deployed agents), how far a single dialogue does (procedural retrieval), and at which turn a dialogue first goes off-track (real-time guardrails). For the first two, we propose dialog-flow-aware metrics, FlowKL and FlowScore, that compare dialogues by how they unfold rather than their content. At the dialogue level, FlowScore significantly outperforms all baselines across seven encoders, showing that semantic similarity is no substitute for procedural similarity. We release DialFork under the MIT license at https://github.com/idiap/dialfork, hoping the community will measure conversational process alongside content.

</details>


### [201] [Rethinking the Role of Tool Graph Memory in Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21571)
*Uiji Hwang,Richeng Xuan,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了"工具图记忆"在LLM智能体工具调用中的作用，发现其提升有限且成本高昂，收益主要来自额外计算而非记忆本身，改用文本化经验存储更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究提出"工具图记忆"，将过往经验存储为工具转移路径以指导后续工具选择，但该组件究竟能稳定提升智能体工具使用能力，还是会损害其表现，尚不明确。作者希望厘清其真实有效性。

Method: 在9个模型和2个基准上评估工具图记忆，与基线对比；并设置随机指导、无指导等对照，剥离记忆内容与额外计算的作用；同时尝试将同样经验以文本化洞见形式存储并与工具图记忆对比。

Result: 工具图记忆很少显著优于基线，且其收益（若存在）需付出两倍以上的LLM调用代价；其指导常不准确，随机或无指导仍保留大部分收益，说明收益来自额外计算而非记忆本身；即便提示了正确的下一工具，仍存在大量失败（如不调用工具直接放弃）。文本化洞见优于工具图记忆，且几乎不增加LLM调用。

Conclusion: 记忆更适合作为软性先验而非对下一工具的硬性规定，这导向更有效且更具泛化性的方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents solve complex tasks by calling external tools over multiple turns. Recent work has proposed tool graph memory, which stores past experience as tool transitions to guide subsequent selection. However, it remains unclear whether this component consistently improves agents' tool use or undermines it. We evaluate tool graph memory on nine models and two benchmarks, finding that it seldom improves significantly over baselines, and that its gains, where present, cost over twice the LLM calls. The memory's guidance is often incorrect, and random or no guidance retains most of the gain, implying that the benefit stems from extra computation rather than the memory itself. Moreover, even with the correct next tool suggested, many failures remain, such as giving up without a tool call. Storing the same experience instead as textual insights outperforms tool graph memory with almost no extra LLM calls. This suggests that memory serves better as a soft prior than as a prescription of the next tool, leading to a more effective and generalizable solution.

</details>


### [202] [ContextLift: Breaking the Spurious Correlation Behind Conditional Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21633)
*Florian Mai,David Kaczér*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ContextLift，一种反事实数据增强方法，通过打破微调领域上下文与错位回答之间的伪相关，缓解条件性错位，同时保持窄任务性能与良性微调不受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在窄领域错位数据上微调语言模型会导致广泛的错位。现有最有效的训练期防御是插入通用安全数据，但一旦提示与微调上下文相似，错位就会重新出现。作者认为这种条件性错位是一种伪相关：训练混合中微调领域上下文只与错位回答共现，导致模型依赖上下文而非请求本身来决定行为。

Method: 提出 ContextLift 反事实数据增强，无需模型、标签或领域知识：将一半插入的安全数据行复制，并从随机训练提示中逐字提取一句上下文句子加入，同时保持其对齐的回答不变，从而打破上下文与错位回答之间的相关性。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 上针对不良安全、法律和医疗建议微调，通用插入在领域线索下分别留下 18.9%、41.9%、58.2% 的错位回答；ContextLift 在相同数据与算力预算下将其降至 2.6%、6.1%、12.8%，且不损害窄任务与良性微调任务，在错位数据仅占微调集 10% 少数时同样有效。

Conclusion: ContextLift 通过反事实增强消除上下文与错位回答的伪相关，显著降低条件性错位，并具备高效、通用、无需额外资源的优点。

Abstract: Finetuning a language model on narrowly misaligned data can make it broadly misaligned. Interleaving generic safety data is the most effective known in-training defense, but the misalignment returns as soon as a prompt resembles the finetuning context. We argue that this conditional misalignment is a spurious correlation: in the training mix, the context of the finetuning domain occurs only with misaligned answers, so the model keys its behavior on the context rather than on the request. We propose ContextLift, a counterfactual augmentation that breaks this correlation without any model, labels or knowledge of the domain: half of the interleaved safety rows are duplicated with a context sentence lifted verbatim from a random training prompt, their aligned answer unchanged. On Qwen2.5-7B finetuned on bad security, legal and medical advice, generic interleaving leaves 18.9%, 41.9% and 58.2% misaligned answers under a domain cue; ContextLift reduces this to 2.6%, 6.1% and 12.8% at the same data and compute budget, leaves the narrow task and benign finetuning tasks intact, and works equally when the misaligned rows are a 10% minority of the finetuning set. Code: https://github.com/davidkaczer/contextlift

</details>


### [203] [Enhancing Multimodal RLVR with Pairwise Token Contrast and Adaptive Trajectory Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21671)
*Yiran Zhang,Hanbo Huang,Jielan Li,Zhen Zhao,Shiyu Liang,Hongxia Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PACE，通过成对 token 对比与自适应轨迹置信度改进多模态强化学习的信用分配，在多骨干与多个基准上取得最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR（如 GRPO、自蒸馏变体）将序列级优势广播到每个 token，但错误通常只集中于少数关键步骤；且基于教师-学生似然差的信用信号无法区分模型是依赖图像还是语言捷径，且在教师无信息时仍被均匀应用。

Method: PACE 由两部分组成：1）成对 token 对比（Pairwise token contrast）：在四种教师视角下对回答打分，即交叉"正确/错误答案提示"与"保留/移除图像"，对每个 token 比较两种提示，抵消两种提示共享的偏移，从而揭示该 token 的支持是否依赖图像；2）自适应轨迹置信度（Adaptive trajectory confidence）：衡量教师在给定正确提示与图像时对回答的预测程度，并在批次内归一化，用以控制 token 对比在该回答内重新分配信用分量的幅度。

Result: 在三个骨干网络上取得最佳平均准确率；在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上于所有评测的多模态推理基准中排名第一。消融实验表明，将成对 token 对比替换为单视角信号会降低性能，而自适应轨迹置信度可带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 两个组件均不可或缺：成对 token 对比提供了图像依赖性的细粒度信用信号，自适应轨迹置信度进一步调节信用再分配，共同提升多模态推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves multimodal reasoning, but group-relative policy optimization broadcasts a single sequence-level advantage to every token of a response, although errors usually localize to a few decisive steps. Recent self-distilled variants refine credit with a teacher--student likelihood gap. However, this signal cannot tell whether it relies on the image or on a language shortcut, and is applied uniformly even when the teacher is uninformative. To address this, we propose Pairwise token contrast and Adaptive trajectory ConfidencE (PACE). Pairwise token contrast scores each response under four teacher views, crossing a correct or incorrect answer hint with the image present or removed, and compares the two hints for every token, which cancels shifts shared by both hints and reveals whether the token's support relies on the image. Adaptive trajectory confidence measures how well the teacher predicts each response given the correct hint and the image, normalizes this score within the batch, and uses it to set how far the token contrast redistributes credit within that response. PACE achieves the best average accuracy on three backbones and ranks first on all evaluated multimodal reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Experiments show that both components matter: replacing pairwise token contrast with a single-view signal underperforms our method, while adaptive trajectory confidence adds further improvement.

</details>


### [204] [No Single Tokenizer Feature Reliably Predicts Downstream Language Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21679)
*Antonio Lopardo,Luis Frentzen Salim,Shikhar Srivastava,Kevin Jiang,Mridul Sharma,Catherine Arnett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了约200个覆盖九种语言的语言模型及其分词器，发现现有内在分词器指标无法一致预测下游性能，并提出一种新的语言学驱动的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词是语言建模流程中的关键环节但理解不足；关于哪些分词器特征能在不进行完整训练的情况下预测更好的语言模型，现有证据混杂不一。

Method: 分析约200个在九种不同语言上训练的语言模型，同时评估模型及其分词器，检验各种内在分词器评估指标与多类基准下游性能之间的关系，并提出新的语言学驱动的度量。

Result: 尽管提出的内在分词器指标数量与种类众多，但没有任何指标能跨模型家族、任务和语言一致地预测下游性能；新提出的语言学驱动度量能捕捉现有指标遗漏的分词器质量方面。

Conclusion: 需要新颖的分词器评估指标与方法；新指标虽有价值，但其预测能力可能因模型家族、语言和任务而异。

Abstract: Tokenization is a critical component of the language modeling pipeline, but it is poorly understood. Tokenizers can impact downstream performance, but there is currently mixed evidence about which tokenizer features predict a better language model without requiring a full training run. In this paper, we analyze approximately 200 language models trained on nine different languages. We evaluate both models and their tokenizers and test whether there is a relationship between any proposed intrinsic tokenizer evaluations and model performance, as measured by a wide variety of benchmarks. Despite the quantity and variety of proposed intrinsic tokenizer metrics, we find that none consistently predicts downstream performance across model families, tasks, and languages. This motivates the need for novel tokenizer evaluation metrics and approaches. We propose novel linguistically grounded metric introduced in this work shows that new metrics can capture aspects of tokenizer quality that existing metrics miss, though their predictive power is likely to vary across model families, languages, and tasks.

</details>


### [205] [Data Strategies for Large Language Models: A Unified Perspective on the Training Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21681)
*Yuanjian Xu,Jianing Hao,Tianze Sun,Yongyao Duan,Sam Chen,Jinyi Han,Wanbo Zhang,Yuxuan Sun,Guang Zhang,Zhong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述将大语言模型训练中的数据策略统一表述为"在候选样本上分配训练预算的策略"，并按决策粒度、模型依赖性与时间自适应性对方法进行分类，同时比较其计算需求与实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据策略决定模型在固定数据预算下能获得哪些能力，但该领域研究跨越预训练与后训练，术语与评估协议不一致，缺乏统一视角，因此需要系统梳理。

Method: 作者将高质量数据定义为具有"训练依赖性效用"（取决于目标、模型及训练状态而非文本本身），并据此把各类数据策略统一建模为预算分配策略，按决策粒度、模型依赖性和时间自适应性进行组织与分类。

Result: 细粒度或状态依赖的策略提供更强控制力，但会带来额外的打分、搜索或 rollout 成本；跨模型尺度的可迁移性证据以及可比的端到端成本证据仍然有限。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了贯穿训练生命周期的数据策略统一框架与分类方式，并维护持续更新的论文与公开实现列表（GitHub 链接），同时指出跨尺度迁移与成本可比性是未来需解决的关键缺口。

Abstract: Data strategy plays a central role in training large language models (LLMs). It governs the selection, weighting, and scheduling of training data, thereby determining the capabilities a model can acquire from a fixed data budget. We treat high-quality data as having high training-dependent utility, which depends on the target objective, the model, and its training state rather than on the text alone. Research in this area now spans pretraining and post-training, but related ideas use inconsistent terminology and evaluation protocols. This work reviews data strategies across the training lifecycle and formulates them as policies that allocate a training budget over candidate examples. We organise methods by their decision granularity, model dependence, and temporal adaptivity, then compare their computational requirements and empirical support. Fine-grained or state-dependent policies offer more control but incur additional scoring, search, or rollout costs. Evidence for transfer across model scales and comparable end-to-end cost remains limited. A continuously updated list of papers, along with their public implementations, is available at https://github.com/xuyj233/data_strategy_survey.

</details>


### [206] [Abstracts or full text? Evaluating input scope and computational efficiency in biomedical lay summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21690)
*Egecan Çelik Evgin,İlknur Karadeniz,Olcay Taner Yıldız*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究两个问题：(1)摘要是否包含足够信息生成与全文相当的通俗摘要；(2)少样本学习能否媲美任务微调。基于两个生物医学通俗摘要数据集和两个新模型进行评测，发现基于摘要的方法整体更优，且运行效率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究文章面向专家撰写，术语难懂，自动通俗摘要可帮助患者和公众理解医学知识。但基于全文生成通俗摘要需要大量算力，且微调大模型资源开销大，因此需要探讨更高效的替代方案。

Method: 使用两个公开的生物医学通俗摘要数据集和两个较新的模型，设置基于摘要与基于全文的对比，以及少样本学习与任务特定微调的对比，从可读性、事实一致性、语义相似度和运行时间效率四个维度进行评测。

Result: 基于摘要的摘要生成整体表现更好。在两个数据集和模型上，基于摘要的微调速度是全文方法的4.70倍，基于摘要的零样本和少样本提示速度是全文方法的5.31倍。

Conclusion: 基于摘要的提示方法在保持通俗摘要质量的同时降低了运行成本，说明摘要信息足以支撑高质量的通俗摘要生成，且少样本学习是微调的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Biomedical research articles are mainly written for experts and often contain terms that are difficult for the general public to understand. Automated lay summarization has become a solution to make biomedical knowledge easier for patients and the public to understand. However, generating lay summaries from full-text articles requires more computing power because the articles are long and costly to process. Fine-tuning large language models for this task also requires substantial resources. This study asks two questions: (1) whether abstracts contain enough information to produce lay summaries as good as those based on full-text articles, and (2) whether few-shot learning performs as well as task-specific fine-tuning. The study used two public biomedical lay summarization datasets and two recent models, evaluated for readability, factual consistency, semantic similarity, and runtime efficiency. Results show that abstract-based summarization performs better overall. Across both datasets and models, abstract fine-tuning is 4.70 times faster, while abstract zero- and few-shot prompting is 5.31 times faster than full-text methods. These results show that abstract-based prompting reduces runtime cost while maintaining lay summary quality.

</details>


### [207] [Retrieved but Not Read: Understanding Why Search-Augmented LLMs Fail to Use Available Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21731)
*Owen Walters,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，搜索增强的大模型常常不打开来源网页、仅凭搜索摘要作答，即便检索到相关证据，也可能给出过时答案并产生不忠实引用；证据的"可获取"与"被有效利用"之间存在明显落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型通过网络搜索可获取最新信息，但即便存在相关证据，它们有时仍会输出过时答案，这一现象的成因尚不清楚。作者希望探究"检索到证据"与"真正有效使用证据"之间的差距及其机制。

Method: 构造训练截止日期之后其取值发生变化的时序事实；评测一个专有模型在真实部署的网页搜索下的表现，以及六个开放权重模型配置在受控检索下的表现；并通过受控实验与逐层分析（layer-wise analysis）进行剖析。

Result: 模型常在未打开来源页面的情况下直接依据搜索摘要作答，而这与更高的过时答案率和引用不忠实率相关。即使在检索证据影响了模型内部预测的情况下，模型在最终答案中仍可能倾向采用过时信息，说明证据的可得性与其有效利用之间存在缺口。

Conclusion: 搜索增强的大模型在可靠使用检索证据方面仍存在明显不足，作者希望这些发现能推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: Web search allows LLMs to access up-to-date information, but it remains unclear why they sometimes produce outdated answers even when relevant evidence is available. To study this problem, we construct temporal facts whose values changed after the models' training cutoffs. We evaluate one proprietary model under deployed web search and six open-weight model configurations under controlled retrieval. We find that models often answer from search snippets without opening the source pages, which is associated with higher rates of outdated answers and unfaithful citations. Our controlled experiments and layer-wise analysis reveal a gap between the availability of evidence and its effective use. Even when retrieved evidence influences internal predictions, models may still favour outdated information in their final answers. We hope these findings will help advance research on the reliable use of retrieved evidence in search-augmented LLMs.

</details>


### [208] [SceneWeave: Audience-Guided Scene Exploration for Dramatic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21737)
*Aolin Li,Xiao Xu,Haihua Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SceneWeave通过生成焦点场景的对比性草稿，比较其互动效果，进而修订人物塑造、铺垫、发展和场景顺序，最终生成完整剧本，以实际场景写作驱动改编决策并传播其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将散文改编为剧本需要把叙述和内心体验转化为能传达关系与心理矛盾的场景。规划虽能明确预期效果，但如何在对话和行动中实现这些效果仍未被解决。

Method: 基于编剧对场景修订及其与整体作品关联的叙述，提出SceneWeave：为焦点场景撰写对比性处理方案，比较其产生的互动，并在此基础上前置铺垫、后续发展及场景顺序进行调整，之后生成完整剧本。同时开发了任务特定的写作模型与人类偏好模型，前者从改编样例和人类偏好中学习，后者结合人类与合成监督。

Result: 盲测读者比较显示，SceneWeave优于基线工作流和基于描述的控制方法；内容评估表明其减少了叙事冗余并具有强心理刻画。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于用真实的场景写作来指导改编决策，并使其后果在整个作品中传播。

Abstract: Adapting prose into a script requires transforming narration and inner experience into scenes that convey relationships and psychological contradictions. Planning specifies intended effects but leaves their realization in dialogue and action unresolved. Drawing on screenwriters' accounts of revising scenes and their connections to the surrounding work, we present SceneWeave, which uses alternative scene drafts to revise the adaptation as a whole. It writes contrasting treatments of a focal scene, compares the interactions they create, and revises characterization, preceding preparation, subsequent development, and scene order before generating the complete script. The central contribution is to use actual scene writing to inform adaptation decisions and propagate their consequences across the work. We also develop a task-specific writing model and a human-preference model for comparing script drafts. The writing model learns from adaptation examples and human preferences; the preference model combines human and synthetic supervision. Blind reader comparisons favor SceneWeave over a baseline workflow and description-based controls, while content assessment indicates reduced narrative redundancy and strong psychological treatment.

</details>


### [209] [Reliable Knowledge Acquisition for Continual Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21739)
*Yixu Huang,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RIKA框架，通过层次化知识抽取与基于证据的知识准入机制，提升基础模型智能体在开放环境中的持续学习可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型驱动的智能体进入开放环境后，需从经验中持续学习；但模型参数固定，只能依赖上下文复用过往经验。现有记忆与经验学习系统要么只关注事实，要么只关注流程，且缺乏对从任务流中抽取的断言可靠性的评估机制。研究发现，代表性系统产生的条目中约12–33%在复用时反而损害后续任务表现。

Method: RIKA（Reliable Knowledge Acquisition）结合两部分：一是对事实性、概念性、程序性和元认知知识进行层次化抽取；二是Evidence-based Knowledge Admission，利用来源证据与后续经验来决定哪些断言可以进入任务智能体的上下文，且无需额外的验证任务。

Result: 在Continual Learning Bench的六条任务流和四种模型骨干上，RIKA在每种骨干模型上都取得了最高的综合得分。

Conclusion: RIKA通过多层次知识抽取与证据驱动的准入控制，有效解决了经验复用中的不可靠问题，在多种任务与模型设置下均表现最优，为基础模型智能体的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As agents powered by foundation models move into open-ended environments, continual learning from experience becomes essential. For agents built on models with fixed parameters, this learning requires reusing prior task experience in context to improve performance and efficiency on future tasks. Existing memory and experience-learning systems typically focus on either retained facts or procedures, and lack a mechanism for establishing the reliability of claims extracted from the ongoing task stream. Indeed, we find that approximately 12--33\% of entries produced by representative systems harm future task performance when reused. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{RIKA} (Reliable Knowledge Acquisition), which combines hierarchical extraction of factual, conceptual, procedural, and metacognitive knowledge with Evidence-based Knowledge Admission, which uses source evidence and subsequent experience to govern which claims enter the task agent's context, without additional validation tasks. Across six task streams from Continual Learning Bench and four model backbones, RIKA achieves the highest aggregate score on every backbone.

</details>


### [210] [Bias Mitigation in Audio-Enabled LLMs through Adversarial Training and Distillation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21763)
*Adnan Al Ali,Jindřich Libovický,Anne Lauscher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语音大模型（AuLLMs）中由副语言线索引发的性别偏见问题，提出用对抗方法「遗忘」模型对性别的感知，以及将性别无关模型蒸馏为「感知但不偏见响应」的模型，并在三个AuLLMs上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AuLLMs允许用户直接通过语音交互，无需中间转录，并能编码非语言线索，从而提升可及性与能力。但非语言线索可能导致模型基于推断出的属性产生有偏行为。本文聚焦性别属性，旨在消除模型对性别的感知依赖，同时保留其他任务性能；并考虑在实际中「感知性别但无偏见响应」这一更温和的需求。

Method: 采用对抗方法对性别感知进行「遗忘」（unlearning），使模型不再依赖性别线索；进一步提出蒸馏方法，将性别无关（gender-agnostic）模型蒸馏为一个「感知性别但对性别无关教师保持相似响应、除非被明确询问」的模型。方法应用于三个AuLLMs。

Result: 在三个AuLLMs上，性别遗忘部分取得令人满意的结果，且在其他基准任务上性能无损失；蒸馏部分取得部分令人满意的结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明可通过对抗方法在不损失其他任务性能的前提下消除AuLLMs的性别感知，适用于不希望进行性别推断的场景；蒸馏方法则为需要「感知但无偏见」的场景提供了更温和的替代方案，但其效果仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Audio-enabled large language models (AuLLMs) allow users to interact with them simply by talking to them, without the need for an intermediate transcription. This expands the model's accessibility and capabilities by encoding non-verbal cues. However, using non-verbal cues may lead to biased behaviour based on the attributes inferred (or assumed) from them. In this work, we focus on the gender attribute and show that models' perception of it can be unlearned using adversarial methods without a loss in performance on other benchmarked tasks. Such models are useful in situations where gender inference is unwanted. However, in other practical settings, gender perception (without bias) may be desired.As a less radical measure, we distil the gender-agnostic model, creating a model that perceives gender but responds similarly to the gender-agnostic teacher, unless explicitly asked.We apply our newly proposed methods to three AuLLMs and obtain satisfactory results in the gender-unlearning part and partially satisfactory results in the distilling part.

</details>


### [211] [In the Guise of Correction: How Framing and Cultural Adaptation Erode LLM Safety on Real-World Criminal Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21789)
*Mohsinul Kabir,Md. Tariquzzaman,Karam S. R. Elradie,Polydoros Giannouris,Yuechen Jiang,Shaoxiong Ji,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GUISE：一个基于真实案件与UNODC ICCS分类构建的犯罪协助场景基准，跨三种语言框架与五种文化评估大模型安全性，揭示"框架效应"和"文化适配"带来的严重越狱风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准多依赖合成提示，分类体系根基薄弱，且缺乏对严重犯罪类别的聚焦分析。作者希望构建一个扎根真实案件、分类严谨、并覆盖多语言多文化的评测资源，以诊断模型在拒绝对齐上的缺陷。

Method: 提出GUISE基准：场景取自真实犯罪案例，按UNODC ICCS分类组织；每个场景以三种语言框架呈现，并在五种文化背景下评估。同时提出两层评估框架，同时判定回答的有害性与可操作提升程度。

Result: (i) 仅框架变化即可导致不安全合规，平均不安全合规率从直接请求的28.9%升至"纠错式提问"下的66.4%（峰值97.3%，p<0.05）；(ii) 文化适配提示在四种非英语语言中均比直译版本引发显著更高的不安全合规（+7至+18个百分点）；(iii) 最不安全模型的有害回答带有明显盎格鲁中心语域，且该泄漏程度随回答有害性单调上升（ρ=0.46–0.63，均p<0.001）。

Conclusion: GUISE提供了扎根现实、细粒度诊断的资源，可助力提升模型跨语言、跨文化的拒绝对齐能力；作者开源代码与数据以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks rely largely on synthetic prompts with loosely grounded taxonomies and offer little focused analysis of severe crime categories. We introduce GUISE, a benchmark of criminal-assistance scenarios sourced from real-world cases and organised under a taxonomy from the UNODC International Classification of Crime for Statistical Purposes (ICCS). Each scenario is rendered under three linguistic framings and evaluated across five distinct cultures. Using our proposed two-tier evaluation framework, which judges both harmfulness and operational uplift, we find that: (i) framing alone drives unsafe compliance, with the mean unsafe compliance rate rising from 28.9% under direct requests to 66.4% under correction seeking (peaking at 97.3%; p < 0.05), as models correcting deliberately distorted details supply executable criminal steps; (ii) culturally adapted prompts elicit significantly more unsafe compliance than literal translations of the same requests in all four non-English languages (+7 to +18 points), a risk that translation-based benchmarks miss; and (iii) unsafe responses from the least-safe models carry strong Anglocentric registers, and the extent of this leakage increases monotonically with response harmfulness ($ρ$ = 0.46 to 0.63 across languages, all p < 0.001). GUISE offers a grounded resource and fine-grained diagnostics for strengthening refusal alignment across languages and cultures. We publish our code and data to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [212] [Behavioral Forgetting Is Not Privacy in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21799)
*Tyler Lizzo,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估仅关注指定基准分布上的行为遗忘，不足以保障隐私。作者通过跨语言查询的对照案例研究表明，遗忘在不同语言和书写脚本间差异显著，单一路径即可暴露行为遗忘与信息不可获取之间的差距，需扩展评估范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘旨在从大语言模型中移除特定信息，但现有评估通常只在指定基准分布上衡量遗忘效果。作者认为这种"行为遗忘"不足以作为面向隐私的遗忘标准，因为同一信息可通过多种访问路径被恢复。因此评估应检验遗忘是否在所有访问路径上保持一致。

Method: 采用受控案例研究，围绕跨语言查询展开：在多个多语言模型家族中，对语义等价的信息使用不同语言和书写脚本进行查询，观察遗忘表现的变化。

Result: 跨多个多语言模型家族，当语义等价的信息以不同语言和脚本查询时，遗忘程度出现显著差异。单一路径即可暴露出行为遗忘与信息真正不可获取之间的实质差距。

Conclusion: 行为遗忘不足以衡量隐私导向的机器遗忘。应倡导对访问路径泛化性和对抗性可恢复性与分布内遗忘一并评估。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information from large language models, but existing evaluations often measure forgetting only on a designated benchmark distribution. We argue that such behavioral forgetting is an insufficient standard for privacy-oriented unlearning, and that evaluation should instead test whether forgetting persists across the access paths through which the same information may be recovered. We demonstrate this with a controlled case study of cross-lingual querying: across multiple multilingual model families, forgetting varies substantially when semantically equivalent information is queried in different languages and scripts. A single access path is therefore enough to expose a real gap between behavioral forgetting and information inaccessibility, motivating broader evaluation of access-path generalization and adversarial recoverability alongside in-distribution forgetting.

</details>


### [213] [Content associations with answer selection and engagement in supply chain community question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21807)
*Hisatoshi Naganawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究以Yahoo!问答（Chiebukuro）物流相关语料为对象，结合名词与语义名词聚类两个层面，探究何种内容与最佳答案选择和问题参与度相关，并揭示回答层与提问层存在差异化的内容—结果画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区问答平台是重要的知识共享生态，但既有研究多聚焦单一词汇特征或宽泛主题，缺少同时对词汇层与主题聚类层的考察；作者希望厘清物流语境下哪些名词及其语义聚类既影响最佳答案选择，又影响问题参与度。

Method: 采集以"物流"为关键词的大量问题与回答层帖子，使用离线机器翻译流程并人工校验名词级保真度；最佳答案选择采用问题线程内的条件逻辑回归，控制答案长度、顺序及问答名词重叠；参与度分两阶段建模：问题是否获得任何回复，以及在获得回复条件下吸引的回复数量。

Result: 操作性物流名词（如distribution、warehouse、center）与最佳答案选择相关，而更广泛的市政与基础设施名词（如car、tax、shinkansen）与更高参与度相关；聚类层面，多数语义名词聚类与最佳答案选择相关，而与回复可能性和回复量相关的聚类则大体不同。

Conclusion: 研究发现回答层与提问层具有差异化的内容—结果画像，并给出了物流相关社区问答话语中参与度与答案选择的领域级描述性图谱。

Abstract: Community question-answering platforms are important ecosystems for knowledge sharing, yet research on what makes an answer best or a question engaging has often focused on either individual lexical features or broad topics.
